Method: 研究采用LLM-to-LLM交互方式，在一个文本型RPG环境中进行实验，使用9种倾向×4种动机构成的36种带明确伦理真值的行为配置文件作为测试平台，构建两阶段系统：先推断目标智能体特征，再生成引导其违背自身信念与动机的欺骗性回应。

Result: 欺骗效果集中在特定行为配置而非均匀分布；88.5%的成功欺骗依赖‘误导’（真实但策略性表述）而非‘伪造’；动机可被准确推断（>98%），是主要攻击路径；而信念系统较难识别（上限49%）或利用。

Conclusion: 不同智能体配置面临不同的欺骗风险，当前基于事实核查的防御手段难以应对策略性表述的欺骗，需针对动机推理等关键弱点加强防护机制。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents increasingly operate in multi-agent systems, understanding adversarial manipulation becomes critical for defensive design. We present a systematic study of intentional deception as an engineered capability, using LLM-to-LLM interactions within a text-based RPG where parameterized behavioral profiles (9 alignments x 4 motivations, yielding 36 profiles with explicit ethical ground truth) serve as our experimental testbed. Unlike accidental deception from misalignment, we investigate a two-stage system that infers target agent characteristics and generates deceptive responses steering targets toward actions counter to their beliefs and motivations. We find that deceptive intervention produces differential effects concentrated in specific behavioral profiles rather than distributed uniformly, and that 88.5% of successful deceptions employ misdirection (true statements with strategic framing) rather than fabrication, indicating fact-checking defenses would miss the large majority of adversarial responses. Motivation, inferable at 98%+ accuracy, serves as the primary attack vector, while belief systems remain harder to identify (49% inference ceiling) or exploit. These findings identify which agent profiles require additional safeguards and suggest that current fact-verification approaches are insufficient against strategically framed deception.

</details>


### [332] [Hospitality-VQA: Decision-Oriented Informativeness Evaluation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07868)
*Jeongwoo Lee,Baek Duhyeong,Eungyeol Han,Soyeon Shin,Gukin han,Seungduk Kim,Jaehyun Jeon,Taewoo Jeong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在酒店业决策导向型视觉问答（VQA）中的应用，提出“信息性”框架并构建了面向 hospitality 领域的VQA数据集，实验表明现有VLMs缺乏内在的决策感知能力，需领域微调才能提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VQA基准多关注事实正确性，未能反映用户在酒店选择等决策场景中真正需要的信息，因此需要新的评估框架和数据集来衡量图像-问题对提供的实际有用信息。

Method: 提出了“信息性”（Informativeness）框架以量化 hospitality 场景中图像-问题对的信息价值，并基于此构建了一个涵盖多种设施类型、反映用户真实信息需求的酒店领域VQA数据集，随后在多个先进VLM上进行实验评估。

Result: 实验发现当前VLM在未经微调时无法有效利用与决策相关的关键视觉信号，缺乏内在的决策意识；仅在经过适度的领域特定微调后，模型才展现出可靠的 informativeness 推理能力。

Conclusion: VLMs在决策导向的 hospitality 领域应用中存在局限，需结合领域特定设计和训练才能满足用户实际信息需求，本研究为面向决策的多模态理解提供了新评估标准和数据基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive multimodal understanding in general domains. However, their applicability to decision-oriented domains such as hospitality remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate how well VLMs can perform visual question answering (VQA) about hotel and facility images that are central to consumer decision-making. While many existing VQA benchmarks focus on factual correctness, they rarely capture what information users actually find useful. To address this, we first introduce Informativeness as a formal framework to quantify how much hospitality-relevant information an image-question pair provides. Guided by this framework, we construct a new hospitality-specific VQA dataset that covers various facility types, where questions are specifically designed to reflect key user information needs. Using this benchmark, we conduct experiments with several state-of-the-art VLMs, revealing that VLMs are not intrinsically decision-aware-key visual signals remain underutilized, and reliable informativeness reasoning emerges only after modest domain-specific finetuning.

</details>


### [333] [Visualizing Coalition Formation: From Hedonic Games to Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07890)
*Pedro Henrique de Paula França,Lucas Lopes Felipe,Daniel Sadoc Menasché*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将图像分割作为亲和博弈中联盟形成的视觉诊断测试平台，通过建模像素为图上的智能体，研究粒度参数对均衡碎片化和边界结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立多智能体系统与图像分割之间的联系，利用亲和博弈的框架分析联盟形成过程中的结构性问题。

Method: 将像素建模为图上的智能体，引入粒度参数调节机制，分析其对均衡状态的影响，并在Weizmann单对象基准上评估多联盟均衡与真实前景的重叠情况。

Result: 观察到随着粒度参数变化，系统从凝聚态过渡到可恢复的碎片化均衡，最终因过度碎片化导致内在失效。

Conclusion: 机制设计参数显著影响均衡结构，该工作为多智能体系统的机制设计提供了可视化验证手段。

Abstract: We propose image segmentation as a visual diagnostic testbed for coalition formation in hedonic games. Modeling pixels as agents on a graph, we study how a granularization parameter shapes equilibrium fragmentation and boundary structure. On the Weizmann single-object benchmark, we relate multi-coalition equilibria to binary protocols by measuring whether the converged coalitions overlap with a foreground ground-truth. We observe transitions from cohesive to fragmented yet recoverable equilibria, and finally to intrinsic failure under excessive fragmentation. Our core contribution links multi-agent systems with image segmentation by quantifying the impact of mechanism design parameters on equilibrium structures.

</details>


### [334] [A Lightweight Traffic Map for Efficient Anytime LaCAM*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07891)
*Bojie Shen,Yue Zhang,Zhe Chen,Daniel Harabor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种利用LaCAM*在搜索过程中构建动态轻量级交通图的新方法，以提升多智能体路径规划的解质量，实验表明该方法优于现有引导路径方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Frank-Wolfe优化的引导路径方法计算开销大且为静态，主要仅对LaCAM*找到首个解有帮助，难以有效应对大规模问题和持续优化需求。

Method: 利用LaCAM*在搜索过程中动态构建轻量级交通图，以此指导配置选择，避免交通拥堵，提升搜索效率与解质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在两种MAPF变体上均实现了比当前最先进的引导路径方法更高的解质量。

Conclusion: 通过动态交通图引导LaCAM*搜索，能更高效地生成高质量无碰撞路径，推动了多智能体路径规划技术的发展。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) aims to compute collision-free paths for multiple agents and has a wide range of practical applications. LaCAM*, an anytime configuration-based solver, currently represents the state of the art. Recent work has explored the use of guidance paths to steer LaCAM* toward configurations that avoid traffic congestion, thereby improving solution quality. However, existing approaches rely on Frank-Wolfe-style optimization that repeatedly invokes single-agent search before executing LaCAM*, resulting in substantial computational overhead for large-scale problems. Moreover, the guidance path is static and primarily beneficial for finding the first solution in LaCAM*. To address these limitations, we propose a new approach that leverages LaCAM*'s ability to construct a dynamic, lightweight traffic map during its search. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves higher solution quality than state-of-the-art guidance-path approaches across two MAPF variants.

</details>


### [335] [EveryQuery: Zero-Shot Clinical Prediction via Task-Conditioned Pretraining over Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07900)
*Payal Chandak,Gregory Kondas,Isaac Kohane,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EveryQuery是一种基于电子健康记录（EHR）的新型基础模型，通过任务条件预训练实现零样本临床预测，避免了传统自回归生成的高计算成本和统计噪声问题，支持直接推理和可提示化输入，在多数任务中表现优于基线模型，尤其在罕见事件预测上优势明显，但在需要多代码析取推理的任务上仍有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于自回归生成的EHR基础模型在零样本临床预测中存在计算昂贵、统计噪声大且无法直接根据临床问题进行条件化预测的问题，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出EveryQuery模型，采用任务条件化的预训练方式，将患者历史与结构化查询作为输入，通过单次前向传播直接估计未来结果的发生概率；预训练阶段使用随机组合的查询任务与患者上下文，使模型学会响应任意输入提示。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上，EveryQuery在39个随机采样任务中的82%优于自回归基线模型，平均AUC提升+0.16（95% CI: [0.10,0.22]），对罕见临床事件预测优势显著，且在预训练未见的任务上表现稳定；但在如30天再入院等需多代码析取推理的任务上表现较差。

Conclusion: EveryQuery实现了高效、可提示的零样本临床预测，克服了自回归方法在罕见事件预测中的局限，展示了任务条件化预训练的有效性，但当前查询语言在复杂逻辑表达上仍有限制，未来需改进以支持更复杂的临床推理任务。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on electronic health records (EHR) have demonstrated zero-shot clinical prediction capabilities by generating synthetic patient futures and aggregating statistics over sampled trajectories. However, this autoregressive inference procedure is computationally expensive, statistically noisy, and not natively promptable because users cannot directly condition predictions on specific clinical questions. In this preliminary work, we introduce EveryQuery, an EHR foundation model that achieves zero-shot inference through task-conditioned pre-training. Rather than generating future events, EveryQuery takes as input a patient's history and a structured query specifying a clinical task, and directly estimates the likelihood of the outcome occurring in the future window via a single forward pass. EveryQuery realizes this capability by pre-training over randomly sampled combinations of query tasks and patient contexts, directly training the model to produce correct answers to arbitrary input prompts. This enables zero-shot prediction for any task in the query space without finetuning, linear probing, or trajectory generation. On MIMIC-IV, EveryQuery outperforms an autoregressive baseline on 82% of 39 randomly sampled prediction tasks, with a mean AUC improvement of +0.16 (95% CI: [0.10,0.22]). This advantage remains consistent on tasks that were explicitly held out from the pre-training distribution. Further, EveryQuery's performance gains are most pronounced for rare clinical events, affirming and demonstrating a solution to the fundamental limitation of autoregressive inference for low-prevalence outcomes. However, at present, EveryQuery underperforms on tasks requiring disjunctive reasoning over multiple codes, such as 30-day readmission, exposing a concrete expressiveness limitation of the current query language.

</details>


### [336] [Advancing Automated Algorithm Design via Evolutionary Stagewise Design with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07970)
*Chen Lu,Ke Xue,Chengrui Gao,Yunqi Shi,Siyuan Xu,Mingxuan Yuan,Chao Qian,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EvoStage的进化式分阶段算法设计范式，通过结合多智能体系统与“全局-局部视角”机制，利用大语言模型实现高效、准确的自动化算法设计，在芯片布局和黑箱优化任务中显著超越人类专家设计与现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于黑箱建模的LLM算法设计缺乏对问题内在机制的理解，易产生幻觉式设计，难以满足工业级复杂场景对算法设计的严苛要求。

Method: 受思维链（CoT）启发，EvoStage将算法设计分解为多个可管理的阶段，并引入实时中间反馈进行迭代优化；结合多智能体系统和“全局-局部视角”机制以缩小搜索空间并避免陷入局部最优。

Result: 在开源基准测试中，EvoStage仅用几次进化步骤即超越人类专家和现有LLM方法，并在所有测试芯片案例上达到历史最优的半周长线长结果；在商用3D芯片布局工具中部署后，性能指标显著提升，实现破纪录的效率。

Conclusion: EvoStage有效弥合了LLM驱动的自动化算法设计与实际工业需求之间的差距，有望大幅提升现实世界中的算法设计效率与人类生产力。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of human science and technology, problems in industrial scenarios are becoming increasingly challenging, bringing significant challenges to traditional algorithm design. Automated algorithm design with LLMs emerges as a promising solution, but the currently adopted black-box modeling deprives LLMs of any awareness of the intrinsic mechanism of the target problem, leading to hallucinated designs. In this paper, we introduce Evolutionary Stagewise Algorithm Design (EvoStage), a novel evolutionary paradigm that bridges the gap between the rigorous demands of industrial-scale algorithm design and the LLM-based algorithm design methods. Drawing inspiration from CoT, EvoStage decomposes the algorithm design process into sequential, manageable stages and integrates real-time intermediate feedback to iteratively refine algorithm design directions. To further reduce the algorithm design space and avoid falling into local optima, we introduce a multi-agent system and a "global-local perspective" mechanism. We apply EvoStage to the design of two types of common optimizers: designing parameter configuration schedules of the Adam optimizer for chip placement, and designing acquisition functions of Bayesian optimization for black-box optimization. Experimental results across open-source benchmarks demonstrate that EvoStage outperforms human-expert designs and existing LLM-based methods within only a couple of evolution steps, even achieving the historically state-of-the-art half-perimeter wire-length results on every tested chip case. Furthermore, when deployed on a commercial-grade 3D chip placement tool, EvoStage significantly surpasses the original performance metrics, achieving record-breaking efficiency. We hope EvoStage can significantly advance automated algorithm design in the real world, helping elevate human productivity.

</details>


### [337] [Adaptive Collaboration with Humans: Metacognitive Policy Optimization for Multi-Agent LLMs with Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07972)
*Wei Yang,Defu Cao,Jiacheng Pang,Muyan Weng,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HILA框架，通过引入人类专家参与多智能体协作，结合双环策略优化实现自主决策与持续学习，提升系统在复杂任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统受限于预训练模型的知识边界，在面对新挑战时容易集体失败，缺乏对外部知识尤其是人类专家知识的有效利用。

Method: 提出HILA框架，引入元认知策略决定何时自主解决问题或向人类专家求助；采用双环策略优化：内环使用带成本感知奖励的组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行即时决策，外环通过持续学习将专家反馈转化为高质量监督信号以增强推理能力。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学和问题求解基准上，HILA结合双环策略优化 consistently 优于先进的多智能体系统。

Conclusion: HILA为构建可协作、可持续改进的智能体系统提供了原则性基础，推动多智能体系统从封闭世界走向开放协作。

Abstract: While scaling individual Large Language Models (LLMs) has delivered remarkable progress, the next frontier lies in scaling collaboration through multi-agent systems (MAS). However, purely autonomous MAS remain ''closed-world'' systems, constrained by the static knowledge horizon of pre-trained models. This limitation makes them brittle on tasks requiring knowledge beyond training data, often leading to collective failure under novel challenges. To address this, we propose the Human-In-the-Loop Multi-Agent Collaboration (HILA) framework, a principled paradigm for human--agent collaboration. HILA trains agents to learn a metacognitive policy that governs when to solve problems autonomously and when to defer to a human expert. To operationalize this policy, we introduce Dual-Loop Policy Optimization, which disentangles immediate decision-making from long-term capability growth. The inner loop applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a cost-aware reward to optimize deferral decisions, while the outer loop implements continual learning, transforming expert feedback into high-quality supervised signals that strengthen the agent's reasoning ability. Experiments on challenging mathematical and problem-solving benchmarks show that HILA, equipped with Dual-Loop Policy Optimization, consistently outperforms advanced MAS, establishing a principled foundation for collaborative and continually improving agentic systems.

</details>


### [338] [CDRRM: Contrast-Driven Rubric Generation for Reliable and Interpretable Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08035)
*Dengcan Liu,Fengkai Yang,Xiaohan Wang,Shurui Yan,Jiajun Chai,Jiahao Li,Yikun Ban,Zhendong Mao,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CDRRM的对比驱动型评分标准奖励模型，通过“先对比后综合”的范式生成高质量、情境感知的评分标准，显著提升了奖励建模的可解释性、数据效率和鲁棒性，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统奖励模型缺乏可解释性和对专家标注的依赖，现有基于评分标准的方法存在标准冗余、噪声大且难以控制偏差（如啰嗦、位置偏见）的问题，亟需一种系统化方法生成高质量、紧凑且公平的评分标准。

Method: 提出CDRRM框架，采用“对比-合成”两阶段范式：首先对偏好样本进行多维对比分析以识别因果判别因素，然后将这些因素合成为简洁的情境感知评分标准，并用于引导奖励判断。

Result: 在RewardBench、RMBench和RMB三个权威基准上的实验表明，CDRRM在多种领域中均取得最先进性能，有效缓解了LLM评估中的常见偏差，且仅用3k高质量样本训练即可使冻结的预训练判断模型超越全微调基线。

Conclusion: CDRRM为奖励建模提供了一条可扩展、可解释且数据高效的路径，解决了现有方法在透明性、偏差控制与数据效率之间的权衡难题。

Abstract: Reward modeling is essential for aligning Large Language Models(LLMs) with human preferences, yet conventional reward models suffer from poor interpretability and heavy reliance on costly expert annotations. While recent rubric-based approaches enhance evaluation transparency, they lack systematic quality control, yielding noisy and redundant criteria, failing to mitigate persistent biases (e.g., verbosity, position) in LLM evaluators, and creating a scalability-reliability trade-off. To address these limitations, we propose CDRRM (Contrast-Driven Rubric Reward Model), a framework built on a novel Contrast-then-Synthesis paradigm for high-quality rubric generation and guided preference judgment. CDRRM first conducts multi-dimensional contrastive profiling on preference pairs to identify causal discriminative factors, then synthesizes these insights into compact, context-aware rubrics to guide preference judg- ments. Extensive experiments on three authoritative benchmarks (RewardBench, RMBench, RMB) demonstrate that CDRRM achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse domains and effectively mitigates aforementioned evaluation biases. Notably, our approach delivers exceptional data efficiency: training the rubric generator on only 3k high-quality samples empowers a frozen pre-trained judge model to outperform fully fine-tuned baselines. This work offers a scalable, interpretable, and data-efficient path for reward modeling.

</details>


### [339] [S2S-FDD: Bridging Industrial Time Series and Natural Language for Explainable Zero-shot Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08048)
*Baoxue Li,Chunhui Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种信号到语义的故障诊断框架（S2S-FDD），通过将时序传感器信号转化为自然语言描述，并结合多轮树形诊断与历史维护文档，实现可解释的零样本故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统故障诊断模型输出抽象，无法回答“为什么”或“如何修复”等问题；大语言模型难以直接处理高维时序工业信号，存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 设计了信号到语义算子，将时间序列信号转换为自然语言摘要，并采用多轮树结构诊断方法，结合历史维护文档和动态信号查询进行推理，支持人工反馈持续优化。

Result: 在多相流过程数据上验证了该方法在可解释性零样本故障诊断中的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: S2S-FDD框架有效 bridged 传感器信号与自然语言语义之间的鸿沟，提升了故障诊断的可解释性与实用性。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis is critical for the safe operation of industrial systems. Conventional diagnosis models typically produce abstract outputs such as anomaly scores or fault categories, failing to answer critical operational questions like "Why" or "How to repair". While large language models (LLMs) offer strong generalization and reasoning abilities, their training on discrete textual corpora creates a semantic gap when processing high-dimensional, temporal industrial signals. To address this challenge, we propose a Signals-to-Semantics fault diagnosis (S2S-FDD) framework that bridges high-dimensional sensor signals with natural language semantics through two key innovations: We first design a Signal-to-Semantic operator to convert abstract time-series signals into natural language summaries, capturing trends, periodicity, and deviations. Based on the descriptions, we design a multi-turn tree-structured diagnosis method to perform fault diagnosis by referencing historical maintenance documents and dynamically querying additional signals. The framework further supports human-in-the-loop feedback for continuous refinement. Experiments on the multiphase flow process show the feasibility and effectiveness of the proposed method for explainable zero-shot fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [340] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning for Tool Use in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08068)
*Yaoqi Ye,Yiran Zhao,Keyu Duan,Zeyu Zheng,Kenji Kawaguchi,Cihang Xie,Michael Qizhe Shieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为In-Context Reinforcement Learning (ICRL) 的新框架，仅使用强化学习并结合少样本提示来训练大语言模型调用外部工具，无需监督微调，实现了数据高效且可扩展的工具使用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂任务上的表现受限于内部知识，虽然可通过外部工具增强，但现有方法依赖大量标注数据进行监督微调，成本高且难以扩展，因此需要一种更高效的数据训练方式。

Method: 提出ICRL框架，在强化学习的rollout阶段通过引入上下文示例指导模型使用工具，并随着训练逐步减少示例数量，最终实现零样本下的自主工具调用。

Result: 在多个推理和工具使用基准上进行了广泛实验，ICRL取得了最先进的性能，优于传统的SFT+RL方法。

Conclusion: ICRL是一种可扩展且数据高效的替代方案，能够有效训练大语言模型在无监督微调的情况下学会使用外部工具。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning abilities, their performance on complex tasks is often constrained by the limitations of their internal knowledge. A compelling approach to overcome this challenge is to augment these models with external tools -- such as Python interpreters for mathematical computations or search engines for retrieving factual information. However, enabling models to use these tools effectively remains a significant challenge. Existing methods typically rely on cold-start pipelines that begin with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), followed by reinforcement learning (RL). These approaches often require substantial amounts of labeled data for SFT, which is expensive to annotate or synthesize. In this work, we propose In-Context Reinforcement Learning (ICRL), an RL-only framework that eliminates the need for SFT by leveraging few-shot prompting during the rollout stage of RL. Specifically, ICRL introduces in-context examples within the rollout prompts to teach the model how to invoke external tools. Furthermore, as training progresses, the number of in-context examples is gradually reduced, eventually reaching a zero-shot setting where the model learns to call tools independently. We conduct extensive experiments across a range of reasoning and tool-use benchmarks. Results show that ICRL achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating its effectiveness as a scalable, data-efficient alternative to traditional SFT-based pipelines.

</details>


### [341] [UIS-Digger: Towards Comprehensive Research Agent Systems for Real-world Unindexed Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08117)
*Chang Liu,Chuqiao Kuang,Tianyi Zhuang,Yuxin Cheng,Huichi Zhou,Xiaoguang Li,Lifeng Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了未索引信息检索（UIS）问题，并发布了首个专用基准UIS-QA，揭示了现有LLM智能体在该任务上的性能显著下降；为此提出多智能体框架UIS-Digger，通过双模式浏览和并行解析网页与文件，在小规模模型上实现了超越大型模型的性能，推动了对全面信息检索系统的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的信息检索智能体严重依赖搜索引擎索引的知识，无法有效获取未被索引的内容（如动态页面、嵌入文件等），存在关键盲区，亟需研究和评估该类未索引信息检索（UIS）能力。

Method: 提出UIS-Digger多智能体框架，采用双模式浏览机制，支持同时进行网页搜索与文件解析，并基于约30B参数的骨干LLM，使用SFT和RFT训练策略进行优化。

Result: 在新构建的UIS-QA基准（含110个专家标注的问答对）上，现有先进智能体性能大幅下降（如GAIA上70.90降至UIS-QA上24.55）；而UIS-Digger达到27.27%的性能，优于集成O3和GPT-4.1等强模型的系统。

Conclusion: 当前信息检索智能体在未索引信息获取方面存在根本性局限，UIS-QA和UIS-Digger为该领域提供了首个工具包，定义了一个新的研究方向，强调主动交互未索引源对构建鲁棒信息检索系统的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in LLM-based information-seeking agents have achieved record-breaking performance on established benchmarks. However, these agents remain heavily reliant on search-engine-indexed knowledge, leaving a critical blind spot: Unindexed Information Seeking (UIS). This paper identifies and explores the UIS problem, where vital information is not captured by search engine crawlers, such as overlooked content, dynamic webpages, and embedded files. Despite its significance, UIS remains an underexplored challenge. To address this gap, we introduce UIS-QA, the first dedicated UIS benchmark, comprising 110 expert-annotated QA pairs. Notably, even state-of-the-art agents experience a drastic performance drop on UIS-QA (e.g., from 70.90 on GAIA and 46.70 on BrowseComp-zh to 24.55 on UIS-QA), underscoring the severity of the problem. To mitigate this, we propose UIS-Digger, a novel multi-agent framework that incorporates dual-mode browsing and enables simultaneous webpage searching and file parsing. With a relatively small $\sim$30B-parameter backbone LLM optimized using SFT and RFT training strategies, UIS-Digger sets a strong baseline at 27.27\%, outperforming systems integrating sophisticated LLMs such as O3 and GPT-4.1. This demonstrates the importance of proactive interaction with unindexed sources for effective and comprehensive information-seeking. Our work not only uncovers a fundamental limitation in current agent evaluation paradigms but also provides the first toolkit for advancing UIS research, defining a new and promising direction for robust information-seeking systems.

</details>


### [342] [Evidence-Driven Reasoning for Industrial Maintenance Using Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08171)
*Fearghal O'Donncha,Nianjun Zhou,Natalia Martinez,James T Rayfield,Fenno F. Heath,Abigail Langbridge,Roman Vaculin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Condition Insight Agent的决策支持框架，通过整合维护语言、操作数据的行为抽象和工程故障语义，实现基于证据的解释与建议，适用于工业维护中的条件性决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业维护中多源异构数据（如文本工单、传感器数据、结构化故障知识）常被孤立分析，难以支持基于资产历史与行为的条件性决策，亟需一种能融合多源证据并生成可解释建议的系统。

Method: 构建Condition Insight Agent框架，集成维护文本、操作数据行为抽象与工程故障语义，采用确定性证据构建和结构化故障知识约束推理过程，并引入基于规则的验证循环以抑制无根据结论。

Result: 在实际CMMS部署案例中验证了该系统能在异构且不完整数据下可靠运行，保持人类监督的同时提供可信的决策支持。

Conclusion: 受限的LLM推理结合规则验证可作为受控的工业维护决策支持层，提升运维决策的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: Industrial maintenance platforms contain rich but fragmented evidence, including free-text work orders, heterogeneous operational sensors or indicators, and structured failure knowledge. These sources are often analyzed in isolation, producing alerts or forecasts that do not support conditional decision-making: given this asset history and behavior, what is happening and what action is warranted? We present Condition Insight Agent, a deployed decision-support framework that integrates maintenance language, behavioral abstractions of operational data, and engineering failure semantics to produce evidence-grounded explanations and advisory actions. The system constrains reasoning through deterministic evidence construction and structured failure knowledge, and applies a rule-based verification loop to suppress unsupported conclusions. Case studies from production CMMS deployments show that this verification-first design operates reliably under heterogeneous and incomplete data while preserving human oversight. Our results demonstrate how constrained LLM-based reasoning can function as a governed decision-support layer for industrial maintenance.

</details>


### [343] [The Struggle Between Continuation and Refusal: A Mechanistic Analysis of the Continuation-Triggered Jailbreak in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08234)
*Yonghong Deng,Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Xinyue Zhang,Zhongbin Guo,Chengzhi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中由续写触发的越狱现象，通过注意力头层面的机制可解释性分析，揭示了模型内在续写驱动力与对齐训练获得的安全防御之间的竞争关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型尽管经过安全对齐，仍易受越狱攻击，其根本原因尚不清楚，因此需要深入探究越狱机制以提升模型安全性。

Method: 采用因果干预和激活缩放方法，在注意力头层面进行机制可解释性分析，并对识别出的安全关键注意力头进行行为分析。

Result: 发现续写触发的指令后缀位置变化可显著提高越狱成功率，且该行为源于模型内在续写驱动与安全防御机制之间的竞争；不同架构模型中的安全注意力头在功能和行为上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解大语言模型的越狱行为提供了新的机制性视角，对改进模型安全具有理论意义和实践价值。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), the safety of LLMs has become a critical concern. Despite significant efforts in safety alignment, current LLMs remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks. However, the root causes of such vulnerabilities are still poorly understood, necessitating a rigorous investigation into jailbreak mechanisms across both academic and industrial communities. In this work, we focus on a continuation-triggered jailbreak phenomenon, whereby simply relocating a continuation-triggered instruction suffix can substantially increase jailbreak success rates. To uncover the intrinsic mechanisms of this phenomenon, we conduct a comprehensive mechanistic interpretability analysis at the level of attention heads. Through causal interventions and activation scaling, we show that this jailbreak behavior primarily arises from an inherent competition between the model's intrinsic continuation drive and the safety defenses acquired through alignment training. Furthermore, we perform a detailed behavioral analysis of the identified safety-critical attention heads, revealing notable differences in the functions and behaviors of safety heads across different model architectures. These findings provide a novel mechanistic perspective for understanding and interpreting jailbreak behaviors in LLMs, offering both theoretical insights and practical implications for improving model safety.

</details>


### [344] [Towards a more efficient bias detection in financial language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08267)
*Firas Hadj Kacem,Ahmed Khanfir,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了五种金融语言模型中的偏见问题，利用约17,000条真实金融新闻句子生成超过125,000个原始-变异句对，发现所有模型在单一和交叉属性下均存在偏见。研究还发现跨模型间偏见输入具有相似模式，可利用其他模型的输出指导偏见检测，显著降低计算成本，例如使用DistilRoBERTa的特征可仅用20%的输入对揭示FinMA 73%的偏见行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融语言模型中的偏见阻碍其实际应用，而现有偏见检测方法因需大量文本变异和成对预测分析导致计算成本高昂，尤其在大模型频繁更新场景下难以持续应用，因此需要更高效的检测方法。

Method: 构建包含17,000条真实金融新闻的大规模数据集，并通过引入受保护属性（如性别、种族）进行句子变异，形成125,000多个原始-变异句对；评估五个金融语言模型在原子和交叉属性设置下的预测一致性；分析不同模型间偏见输入的共性，探索跨模型引导的偏见检测策略。

Result: 所有五个金融语言模型均表现出显著偏见，原子偏见范围为0.58%-6.05%，交叉偏见为0.75%-5.97%；不同模型在偏见敏感输入上呈现高度一致的模式；利用一个模型识别出的关键输入可有效指导其他模型的偏见检测，最多可用20%的输入对发现73%的偏见行为，大幅减少计算开销。

Conclusion: 金融语言模型普遍存在与受保护属性相关的偏见，且跨模型间存在可复用的偏见触发输入模式，支持采用跨模型引导策略实现高效、低成本的持续偏见监测，为实际部署中的公平性保障提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Bias in financial language models constitutes a major obstacle to their adoption in real-world applications. Detecting such bias is challenging, as it requires identifying inputs whose predictions change when varying properties unrelated to the decision, such as demographic attributes. Existing approaches typically rely on exhaustive mutation and pairwise prediction analysis over large corpora, which is effective but computationally expensive-particularly for large language models and can become impractical in continuous retraining and releasing processes. Aiming at reducing this cost, we conduct a large-scale study of bias in five financial language models, examining similarities in their bias tendencies across protected attributes and exploring cross-model-guided bias detection to identify bias-revealing inputs earlier. Our study uses approximately 17k real financial news sentences, mutated to construct over 125k original-mutant pairs. Results show that all models exhibit bias under both atomic (0.58\%-6.05\%) and intersectional (0.75\%-5.97\%) settings. Moreover, we observe consistent patterns in bias-revealing inputs across models, enabling substantial reuse and cost reduction in bias detection. For example, up to 73\% of FinMA's biased behaviours can be uncovered using only 20\% of the input pairs when guided by properties derived from DistilRoBERTa outputs.

</details>


### [345] [Deconstructing Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning: Towards a Unified Perception-Alignment-Reasoning Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08291)
*Tianyu Yang,Sihong Wu,Yilun Zhao,Zhenwen Liang,Lisen Dai,Chen Zhao,Minhao Cheng,Arman Cohan,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了多模态数学推理（MMR）的最新进展，提出了四个核心问题以系统性地分析现有方法，并讨论了未来研究的挑战与方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MMR模型在处理现实世界中的视觉数学任务时存在对图表误解、图文信息对齐困难和推理不一致等问题，且现有评估方法多关注最终答案而非推理过程的正确性。因此需要系统性框架来指导和比较不同方法。

Method: 通过提出四个基本问题——从多模态输入中提取什么、如何表示与对齐图文信息、如何进行推理、如何评估推理过程的正确性——对现有MMR方法进行系统性研究和分类。

Result: 建立了一个统一的分析框架，用于理解和比较不同的MMR方法，总结了当前整合结构化感知、显式对齐和可验证推理的趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架为MMR领域提供了清晰的发展路线图，揭示了当前存在的开放性挑战，并指出了未来有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning (MMR) has recently attracted increasing attention for its capability to solve mathematical problems that involve both textual and visual modalities. However, current models still face significant challenges in real-world visual math tasks. They often misinterpret diagrams, fail to align mathematical symbols with visual evidence, and produce inconsistent reasoning steps. Moreover, existing evaluations mainly focus on checking final answers rather than verifying the correctness or executability of each intermediate step. To address these limitations, a growing body of recent research addresses these issues by integrating structured perception, explicit alignment, and verifiable reasoning within unified frameworks. To establish a clear roadmap for understanding and comparing different MMR approaches, we systematically study them around four fundamental questions: (1) What to extract from multimodal inputs, (2) How to represent and align textual and visual information, (3) How to perform the reasoning, and (4) How to evaluate the correctness of the overall reasoning process. Finally, we discuss open challenges and offer perspectives on promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [346] [CORE-Acu: Structured Reasoning Traces and Knowledge Graph Safety Verification for Acupuncture Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08321)
*Liuyi Xu,Yun Guo,Ming Chen,Zihan Dun,Yining Qian,An-Yang Lu,Shuang Li,Lijun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CORE-Acu的神经符号框架，用于针灸临床决策支持，结合结构化思维链与知识图谱安全验证，提升大语言模型在针灸领域的可解释性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床决策支持中具有潜力，但其黑箱特性（如不可追溯的推理和概率性幻觉）在对可解释性和安全性要求极高的针灸领域构成严重挑战。

Method: 构建首个针灸结构化推理轨迹数据集和模式约束微调框架，采用结构化思维链（S-CoT）将中医隐式推理显式化；建立基于知识图谱和符号否决机制的“生成-验证-修正”闭环推理系统，并提出词汇匹配实体重加权损失（LMERL），以纠正微调过程中的术语漂移问题。

Result: 在1,000个保留病例上的实验表明，CORE-Acu在实体保真度和推理质量上表现优越，且观察到的安全违规率为0/1,000（95% CI: 0–0.37%），而GPT-4o在相同规则下的违规率为8.5%。

Conclusion: CORE-Acu是一种可靠的针灸临床决策支持神经符号框架，能够确保推理过程可审计和严格遵守安全规范。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show significant potential for clinical decision support (CDS), yet their black-box nature -- characterized by untraceable reasoning and probabilistic hallucinations -- poses severe challenges in acupuncture, a field demanding rigorous interpretability and safety. To address this, we propose CORE-Acu, a neuro-symbolic framework for acupuncture clinical decision support that integrates Structured Chain-of-Thought (S-CoT) with knowledge graph (KG) safety verification. First, we construct the first acupuncture Structured Reasoning Trace dataset and a schema-constrained fine-tuning framework. By enforcing an explicit causal chain from pattern identification to treatment principles, treatment plans, and acupoint selection, we transform implicit Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) reasoning into interpretable generation constraints, mitigating the opacity of LLM-based CDS. Furthermore, we construct a TCM safety knowledge graph and establish a ``Generate--Verify--Revise'' closed-loop inference system based on a Symbolic Veto Mechanism, employing deterministic rules to intercept hallucinations and enforce hard safety boundaries. Finally, we introduce the Lexicon-Matched Entity-Reweighted Loss (LMERL), which corrects terminology drift caused by the frequency--importance mismatch in general optimization by adaptively amplifying gradient contributions of high-risk entities during fine-tuning. Experiments on 1,000 held-out cases demonstrate CORE-Acu's superior entity fidelity and reasoning quality. Crucially, CORE-Acu achieved 0/1,000 observed safety violations (95\% CI: 0--0.37\%), whereas GPT-4o exhibited an 8.5\% violation rate under identical rules. These results establish CORE-Acu as a robust neuro-symbolic framework for acupuncture clinical decision support, guaranteeing both reasoning auditability and strict safety compliance.

</details>


### [347] [Agentic Neurosymbolic Collaboration for Mathematical Discovery: A Case Study in Combinatorial Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08322)
*Hai Xia,Carla P. Gomes,Bart Selman,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过神经符号推理，结合大语言模型、符号计算工具和人类指导，实现了组合设计理论中拉丁方不平衡性的一个紧下界的新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人工智能在纯数学发现中的作用，特别是在传统方法难以解决的问题上，借助LLM与符号系统协同推进研究。

Method: 采用神经符号系统框架，由大语言模型生成假设，符号计算工具（如计算机代数、约束求解器和模拟退火）进行验证与枚举，人类提供关键研究方向调整，并利用多模型讨论进行批评与纠错。

Result: 发现了当 $n \equiv 1 \pmod{3}$ 时拉丁方不平衡性的紧下界为 $4n(n{-}1)/9$，并通过Lean 4形式化验证；引入了一类新的近完美排列实现该界限。

Conclusion: 神经符号系统结合人类指导能够推动真正的数学发现，展示了AI在纯数学前沿问题中的潜力。

Abstract: We study mathematical discovery through the lens of neurosymbolic reasoning, where an AI agent powered by a large language model (LLM), coupled with symbolic computation tools, and human strategic direction, jointly produced a new result in combinatorial design theory. The main result of this human-AI collaboration is a tight lower bound on the imbalance of Latin squares for the notoriously difficult case $n \equiv 1 \pmod{3}$.
  We reconstruct the discovery process from detailed interaction logs spanning multiple sessions over several days and identify the distinct cognitive contributions of each component. The AI agent proved effective at uncovering hidden structure and generating hypotheses. The symbolic component consists of computer algebra, constraint solvers, and simulated annealing, which provides rigorous verification and exhaustive enumeration. Human steering supplied the critical research pivot that transformed a dead end into a productive inquiry. Our analysis reveals that multi-model deliberation among frontier LLMs proved reliable for criticism and error detection but unreliable for constructive claims.
  The resulting human-AI mathematical contribution, a tight lower bound of $4n(n{-}1)/9$, is achieved via a novel class of near-perfect permutations. The bound was formally verified in Lean 4. Our experiments show that neurosymbolic systems can indeed produce genuine discoveries in pure mathematics.

</details>


### [348] [M$^3$-ACE: Rectifying Visual Perception in Multimodal Math Reasoning via Multi-Agentic Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08369)
*Peijin Xie,Zhen Xu,Bingquan Liu,Baoxun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-ACE，一种多智能体上下文工程框架，通过解耦感知与推理、动态维护视觉证据列表，提升多模态数学推理中的视觉感知准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在视觉数学推理中表现受限，主要瓶颈在于视觉感知不准确，且模型对初始感知过于自信，难以通过常规方法纠正错误。

Method: 提出M3-ACE框架，采用多智能体协作机制，动态维护以视觉证据为中心的共享上下文；引入摘要工具和精炼工具，支持多轮稳定协作，整合互补观察并修正不一致或缺失的感知信息。

Result: 在MathVision、MathVista和MathVerse等多个基准上实现性能提升，其中MathVision达到89.1的新SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 感知为中心的多智能体协作对提升多模态推理系统至关重要，M3-ACE有效解决了视觉感知错误难以纠正的问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have recently shown promising progress in visual mathematical reasoning. However, their performance is often limited by a critical yet underexplored bottleneck: inaccurate visual perception. Through systematic analysis, we find that the most failures originate from incorrect or incomplete visual evidence extraction rather than deficiencies in reasoning capability. Moreover, models tend to remain overly confident in their initial perceptions, making standard strategies such as prompt engineering, multi-round self-reflection, or posterior guidance insufficient to reliably correct errors.
  To address this limitation, we propose M3-ACE, a multi-agentic context engineering framework designed to rectify visual perception in multimodal math reasoning. Instead of directly aggregating final answers, our approach decouples perception and reasoning by dynamically maintaining a shared context centered on visual evidence lists. Multiple agents collaboratively contribute complementary observations, enabling the system to expose inconsistencies and recover missing perceptual information. To support stable multi-turn collaboration, we further introduce two lightweight tools: a Summary Tool that organizes evidence from different agents into consistent, complementary, and conflicting components, and a Refine Tool that filters unreliable samples and guides iterative correction.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that M3-ACE substantially improves visual mathematical reasoning performance across multiple benchmarks. Our method establishes new state-of-the-art results 89.1 on the MathVision benchmark and achieves consistent improvements on other related datasets, including MathVista and MathVerse. These results highlight the importance of perception-centric multi-agent collaboration for advancing multimodal reasoning systems.

</details>


### [349] [A Hierarchical Error-Corrective Graph Framework for Autonomous Agents with LLM-Based Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08388)
*Cong Cao,Jingyao Zhang,Kun Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于基于大语言模型动作生成的自主智能体的分层纠错图框架（HECG），包含多维可迁移策略、错误矩阵分类和因果上下文图检索三项核心创新，旨在提升复杂任务中的策略选择、错误归因与上下文检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在策略迁移时缺乏多维对齐，错误分析依赖整体指标而无法定位根源，且检索机制难以捕捉动态任务中的因果结构，限制了智能体在复杂多步任务中的可靠性与适应性。

Method: 1) 多维可迁移策略（MDTS）：融合任务质量（Q）、置信度/成本（C）、奖励（R）和LLM语义评分（LLM-Score）实现策略的多维对齐；2) 错误矩阵分类（EMC）：将错误细分为十类并按严重性、可恢复性等维度结构化归因；3) 因果上下文图检索（CCGR）：构建包含状态、动作与事件因果关系的历史图谱，通过子图匹配获取与当前任务最相关的结构化上下文。

Result: 该框架能更精准地筛选高质量策略，降低负迁移风险；实现细粒度的错误诊断与归因，指导策略优化；并通过结构化因果图提升在动态环境中的检索准确性与执行可靠性。

Conclusion: HECG通过分层的多维对齐、结构化错误分析与因果图检索机制，显著增强了基于LLM的自主智能体在复杂、多步任务中的适应性、鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: We propose a Hierarchical Error-Corrective Graph FrameworkforAutonomousAgentswithLLM-BasedActionGeneration(HECG),whichincorporates three core innovations: (1) Multi-Dimensional Transferable Strategy (MDTS): by integrating task quality metrics (Q), confidence/cost metrics (C), reward metrics (R), and LLM-based semantic reasoning scores (LLM-Score), MDTS achieves multi-dimensional alignment between quantitative performance and semantic context, enabling more precise selection of high-quality candidate strate gies and effectively reducing the risk of negative transfer. (2) Error Matrix Classification (EMC): unlike simple confusion matrices or overall performance metrics, EMC provides structured attribution of task failures by categorizing errors into ten types, such as Strategy Errors (Strategy Whe) and Script Parsing Errors (Script-Parsing-Error), and decomposing them according to severity, typical actions, error descriptions, and recoverability. This allows precise analysis of the root causes of task failures, offering clear guidance for subsequent error correction and strategy optimization rather than relying solely on overall success rates or single performance metrics. (3) Causal-Context Graph Retrieval (CCGR): to enhance agent retrieval capabilities in dynamic task environments, we construct graphs from historical states, actions, and event sequences, where nodes store executed actions, next-step actions, execution states, transferable strategies, and other relevant information, and edges represent causal dependencies such as preconditions for transitions between nodes. CCGR identifies subgraphs most relevant to the current task context, effectively capturing structural relationships beyond vector similarity, allowing agents to fully leverage contextual information, accelerate strategy adaptation, and improve execution reliability in complex, multi-step tasks.

</details>


### [350] [Efficient Policy Learning with Hybrid Evaluation-Based Genetic Programming for Uncertain Agile Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08447)
*Junhua Xue,Yuning Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合评估的遗传规划方法（HE-GP）来求解不确定敏捷地球观测卫星调度问题（UAEOSSP），通过结合精确与近似评估模式，显著降低计算开销并保持优异调度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAEOSSP中的不确定性使得传统预规划调度易失效，且现有遗传规划超启发式方法因高计算成本和评估依赖性局部最优问题难以高效求解该问题。

Method: 设计了一种混合评估机制（HE），集成到基于策略的在线调度算法（OSA）中，包含精确（严格约束验证）和近似（简化逻辑）两种过滤模式，并根据进化状态动态切换；在此基础上构建HE-GP框架以演化调度策略。

Result: 在16个模拟实例集上实验表明，HE-GP相比仅使用精确评估的GP方法平均训练时间减少17.77%，生成的最优策略在所有场景下均获得最高平均排名，显著优于手工启发式和其他单评估GPHH方法。

Conclusion: HE-GP通过动态混合评估机制有效平衡了评估精度与效率，在保证调度质量的同时显著降低计算成本，为解决UAEOSSP提供了高效且实用的超启发式求解框架。

Abstract: The Uncertain Agile Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling Problem (UAEOSSP) is a novel combinatorial optimization problem and a practical engineering challenge that aligns with the current demands of space technology development. It incorporates uncertainties in profit, resource consumption, and visibility, which may render pre-planned schedules suboptimal or even infeasible. Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristic (GPHH) shows promise for evolving interpretable scheduling policies; however, their simulation-based evaluation incurs high computational costs. Moreover, the design of the constructive method, denoted as Online Scheduling Algorithm (OSA), directly affects fitness assessment, resulting in evaluation-dependent local optima within the policy space. To address these issues, this paper proposes a Hybrid Evaluation-based Genetic Programming (HE-GP) for effectively solving UAEOSSP. A Hybrid Evaluation (HE) mechanism is integrated into the policy-driven OSA, combining exact and approximate filtering modes: exact mode ensures evaluation accuracy through elaborately designed constraint verification modules, while approximate mode reduces computational overhead via simplified logic. HE-GP dynamically switches between evaluation models based on real-time evolutionary state information. Experiments on 16 simulated instance sets demonstrate that HE-GP significantly outperforms handcrafted heuristics and single-evaluation based GPHH, achieving substantial reductions in computational cost while maintaining excellent scheduling performance across diverse scenarios. Specifically, the average training time of HE-GP was reduced by 17.77\% compared to GP employing exclusively exact evaluation, while the optimal policy generated by HE-GP achieved the highest average ranks across all scenarios.

</details>


### [351] [CoCo: Code as CoT for Text-to-Image Preview and Rare Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08652)
*Haodong Li,Chunmei Qing,Huanyu Zhang,Dongzhi Jiang,Yihang Zou,Hongbo Peng,Dingming Li,Yuhong Dai,ZePeng Lin,Juanxi Tian,Yi Zhou,Siqi Dai,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoCo（Code-as-CoT）的代码驱动推理框架，通过将推理过程表示为可执行代码，提升文本到图像生成在复杂布局和结构化内容上的精确性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于自然语言思维链（CoT）的文本到图像生成方法在处理复杂空间布局、结构化视觉元素和密集文本时缺乏精度，因此需要更精确的中间规划方式。

Method: CoCo框架首先根据文本提示生成指定场景结构布局的可执行代码，然后在沙箱环境中执行该代码生成确定性的草图图像，再通过细粒度图像编辑对草图进行精修以获得最终高保真图像；同时构建了包含1万组草图-终图配对的CoCo-10K数据集用于训练。

Result: 在StructT2IBench、OneIG-Bench和LongText-Bench上的实验表明，CoCo相比直接生成方法分别提升了+68.83%、+54.8%和+41.23%，且优于其他结合CoT的生成方法。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是一种有效且可靠的推理范式，能够实现更精确、可控和结构化的文本到图像生成。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have significantly advanced text-to-image (T2I) generation, particularly through the integration of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, existing CoT-based T2I methods largely rely on abstract natural-language planning, which lacks the precision required for complex spatial layouts, structured visual elements, and dense textual content. In this work, we propose CoCo (Code-as-CoT), a code-driven reasoning framework that represents the reasoning process as executable code, enabling explicit and verifiable intermediate planning for image generation. Given a text prompt, CoCo first generates executable code that specifies the structural layout of the scene, which is then executed in a sandboxed environment to render a deterministic draft image. The model subsequently refines this draft through fine-grained image editing to produce the final high-fidelity result. To support this training paradigm, we construct CoCo-10K, a curated dataset containing structured draft-final image pairs designed to teach both structured draft construction and corrective visual refinement. Empirical evaluations on StructT2IBench, OneIG-Bench, and LongText-Bench show that CoCo achieves improvements of +68.83%, +54.8%, and +41.23% over direct generation, while also outperforming other generation methods empowered by CoT. These results demonstrate that executable code is an effective and reliable reasoning paradigm for precise, controllable, and structured text-to-image generation. The code is available at: https://github.com/micky-li-hd/CoCo

</details>


### [352] [A Multi-Objective Optimization Approach for Sustainable AI-Driven Entrepreneurship in Resilient Economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08692)
*Anas ALsobeh,Raneem Alkurdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出EcoAI-Resilience框架，通过多目标优化平衡人工智能部署的可持续性收益、经济韧性与环境成本，基于多源数据验证其在53国14个行业中的卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能发展带来经济与环境双重挑战，需在提升可持续性与经济韧性的同时，降低其高能耗与环境代价。

Method: 采用多目标数学优化方法，整合能源消耗、可持续性指标、经济表现和创业成果等数据，构建EcoAI-Resilience框架，并在53个国家和14个行业中进行实证分析。

Result: 模型各组件R值均超过0.99，优于多种基线模型；识别出100%可再生能源集成、80%能效提升和人均202.48美元最优投资等策略；发现经济复杂性与韧性（r=0.82）、可再生能源与可持续性（r=0.71）间强相关性，且全球AI准备度与可再生能源 adoption 持续提升。

Conclusion: EcoAI-Resilience框架有效协调AI发展与可持续发展目标，为政策制定和技术部署提供科学依据，推动绿色智能经济转型。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) technologies presents both unprecedented opportunities and significant challenges for sustainable economic development. While AI offers transformative potential for addressing environmental challenges and enhancing economic resilience, its deployment often involves substantial energy consumption and environmental costs. This research introduces the EcoAI-Resilience framework, a multi-objective optimization approach designed to maximize the sustainability benefits of AI deployment while minimizing environmental costs and enhancing economic resilience. The framework addresses three critical objectives through mathematical optimization: sustainability impact maximization, economic resilience enhancement, and environmental cost minimization. The methodology integrates diverse data sources, including energy consumption metrics, sustainability indicators, economic performance data, and entrepreneurship outcomes across 53 countries and 14 sectors from 2015-2024. Our experimental validation demonstrates exceptional performance with R scores exceeding 0.99 across all model components, significantly outperforming baseline methods, including Linear Regression (R = 0.943), Random Forest (R = 0.957), and Gradient Boosting (R = 0.989). The framework successfully identifies optimal AI deployment strategies featuring 100\% renewable energy integration, 80% efficiency improvement targets, and optimal investment levels of $202.48 per capita. Key findings reveal strong correlations between economic complexity and resilience (r = 0.82), renewable energy adoption and sustainability outcomes (r = 0.71), and demonstrate significant temporal improvements in AI readiness (+1.12 points/year) and renewable energy adoption (+0.67 year) globally.

</details>


### [353] [Evaluating Financial Intelligence in Large Language Models: Benchmarking SuperInvesting AI with LLM Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08704)
*Akshay Gulati,Kanha Singhania,Tushar Banga,Parth Arora,Anshul Verma,Vaibhav Kumar Singh,Agyapal Digra,Jayant Singh Bisht,Danish Sharma,Varun Singla,Shubh Garg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI金融智能基准（AFIB），用于多维度评估大型语言模型在财务分析中的表现，涵盖事实准确性、分析完整性、数据时效性、模型一致性及错误模式五个方面。研究评估了GPT、Gemini、Perplexity、Claude和SuperInvesting五种系统，在95多个真实股权研究问题上进行测试。结果显示SuperInvesting整体表现最佳，事实准确性和分析完整性最高，且幻觉率最低；而具备检索能力的Perplexity在数据时效性上表现好，但分析综合与一致性较弱。研究表明，结合结构化财务数据访问与推理能力的系统在投资研究中更为可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型广泛应用于金融分析，但缺乏系统性评估其金融推理能力的方法，因此需要构建一个多维度的评估框架来全面衡量不同模型在真实金融任务中的表现。

Method: 提出AI金融智能基准（AFIB），包含五个评估维度：事实准确性、分析完整性、数据时效性、模型一致性和失败模式。使用95多个来自实际股权研究任务的结构化财务问题，对GPT、Gemini、Perplexity、Claude和SuperInvesting五种AI系统进行评测。

Result: SuperInvesting在综合表现上最优，平均事实准确得分为8.96/10，分析完整性为56.65/70，且幻觉率最低；Perplexity因具备实时信息检索能力，在数据时效性任务中表现强，但在分析合成与一致性方面较弱；各模型在不同维度上表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 金融智能具有多维性，仅依赖语言模型本身不足以胜任复杂投资研究任务；结合结构化财务数据访问与深度分析推理能力的系统（如SuperInvesting）能提供更可靠的结果，未来应注重多模态、多能力协同的金融AI系统设计。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for financial analysis and investment research, yet systematic evaluation of their financial reasoning capabilities remains limited. In this work, we introduce the AI Financial Intelligence Benchmark (AFIB), a multi-dimensional evaluation framework designed to assess financial analysis capabilities across five dimensions: factual accuracy, analytical completeness, data recency, model consistency, and failure patterns. We evaluate five AI systems: GPT, Gemini, Perplexity, Claude, and SuperInvesting, using a dataset of 95+ structured financial analysis questions derived from real-world equity research tasks. The results reveal substantial differences in performance across models. Within this benchmark setting, SuperInvesting achieves the highest aggregate performance, with an average factual accuracy score of 8.96/10 and the highest completeness score of 56.65/70, while also demonstrating the lowest hallucination rate among evaluated systems. Retrieval-oriented systems such as Perplexity perform strongly on data recency tasks due to live information access but exhibit weaker analytical synthesis and consistency. Overall, the results highlight that financial intelligence in large language models is inherently multi-dimensional, and systems that combine structured financial data access with analytical reasoning capabilities provide the most reliable performance for complex investment research workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [354] [A Pivot-Based Kirigami Utensil for Hand-Held and Robot-Assisted Feeding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06716)
*Keone Leao,Grace Brotherson,Iain Mischel,Sagar Parekh,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为kiri-spoon的新型餐具设计，适用于手部有障碍的用户，可通过挤压手柄实现类似钳子的功能，有效防止食物掉落，并可用于手动或机器人辅助进食。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为帮助患有震颤或其他行动受限的成年人更方便地进食，解决传统餐具难以操控、易洒落食物的问题。

Method: 提出一种基于枢轴结构的kiri-spoon设计，通过手柄挤压改变形状以夹取食物，可作为勺子或叉子使用；该设计由四个3D打印部件组成，也可加装伺服电机用于机器人辅助喂食。

Result: 实验表明，残疾成人和帕金森病老年用户认为kiri-spoon比现有工具更有效防止溢出，更能满足其进食需求。

Conclusion: kiri-spoon是一种低成本、易组装、多功能的餐具解决方案，适用于不同使用场景（手动或机器人辅助），提升了行动不便用户的进食独立性和体验。

Abstract: Eating is a daily challenge for over 60 million adults with essential tremors and other mobility limitations. For these users, traditional utensils like forks or spoons are difficult to manipulate -- resulting in accidental spills and restricting the types of food that can be consumed. Prior work has developed rigid, hand-held utensils that often fail to secure food, as well as soft, shape-changing utensils made strictly for robot-assisted feeding. To assist a broader range of users, we introduce a re-designed kiri-spoon that can be leveraged as either a hand-held utensil or a robot-mounted attachment. Our key idea -- developed in collaboration with stakeholders -- is a pivot-based design. With this design the kiri-spoon behaves like a pair of pliers: users squeeze the handles to change the shape of the utensil and enclose food morsels. In practice, users can apply this kiri-spoon as either a spoon (that scoops food) or as a fork (that pinches food); when the handles are closed, the utensil wraps around the morsel and prevents it from accidentally falling. We characterize the amount of force required to open or close the kiri-spoon, and show how designers can modify this force through kinematic or material changes. A highlight of our design is its accessibility: the hand-held version consists of just four 3D printed parts that snap together. By adding a servo motor, we can extend this same kinematic structure to robot-assisted feeding. Across our user studies, adults with disabilities and elderly adults with Parkinson's reported that the kiri-spoon better met their needs and provided a more effective means of spill prevention than existing alternatives. See a video of our kiri-spoon here: https://youtu.be/FFIomm5RL98

</details>


### [355] [Dynamic Targeting of Satellite Observations Using Supplemental Geostationary Satellite Data and Hierarchical Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06719)
*Akseli Kangaslahti,Itai Zilberstein,Alberto Candela,Steve Chien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层规划方法，利用地球静止卫星的远期数据与星载传感器的短期数据结合，提升动态目标观测任务的性能，尤其在稀疏分布且动态性强的目标场景中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态目标观测依赖星载前视传感器，但其前视时间短（仅1分钟），难以应对复杂环境变化；同时，完全依赖大量地球静止卫星数据会导致规划搜索空间爆炸。因此需要一种高效融合多源数据的规划方法。

Method: 提出一种分层规划方法：首先使用地球静止卫星提供的长达35分钟的前视数据，在多项式时间内制定长期观测蓝图；然后利用星载传感器的高精度短时前视数据（1分钟）对短期观测进行精细化调整。

Result: 在四种不同任务场景（三种云规避变体和一个风暴追踪场景）中，所提方法相比传统仅依赖星载数据的动态目标规划器性能最高提升41%；分析表明，该方法在目标稀疏且动态性强的环境中优势最明显。

Conclusion: 融合地球静止卫星的广域前视数据与星载传感器的精确短时数据，通过分层规划可有效克服传统动态目标任务中的局限性，显著提升科学观测效益，尤其适用于目标稀疏、变化快的场景。

Abstract: The Dynamic Targeting (DT) mission concept is an approach to satellite observation in which a lookahead sensor gathers information about the upcoming environment and uses this information to intelligently plan observations. Previous work has shown that DT has the potential to increase the science return across applications. However, DT mission concepts must address challenges, such as the limited spatial extent of onboard lookahead data and instrument mobility, data throughput, and onboard computation constraints. In this work, we show how the performance of DT systems can be improved by using supplementary data streamed from geostationary satellites that provide lookahead information up to 35 minutes ahead of time rather than the 1 minute latency from an onboard lookahead sensor. While there is a greater volume of geostationary data, the search space for observation planning explodes exponentially with the size of the horizon. To address this, we introduce a hierarchical planning approach in which the geostationary data is used to plan a long-term observation blueprint in polynomial time, then the onboard lookahead data is leveraged to refine that plan over short-term horizons. We compare the performance of our approach to that of traditional DT planners relying on onboard lookahead data across four different problem instances: three cloud avoidance variations and a storm hunting scenario. We show that our hierarchical planner outperforms the traditional DT planners by up to 41% and examine the features of the scenarios that affect the performance of our approach. We demonstrate that incorporating geostationary satellite data is most effective for dynamic problem instances in which the targets of interest are sparsely distributed throughout the overflight.

</details>


### [356] [Robotic Foundation Models for Industrial Control: A Comprehensive Survey and Readiness Assessment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06749)
*David Kube,Simon Hadwiger,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器人基础模型（RFMs）在工业应用中的现状与挑战，提出了涵盖149项具体标准的评估框架，并对324种具备操作能力的RFM进行了系统评估，结果表明当前模型在安全性、实时性、感知鲁棒性和系统集成等方面仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RFMs在指令和示范驱动的机器人控制中展现出潜力，但其在工业环境中的适用性尚缺乏深入分析，因此需要系统评估其在实际工业场景中的成熟度与差距。

Method: 基于工业领域和应用场景的具体影响，提炼出11项相互关联的工业部署要求，并构建包含149项标准的评估框架；采用保守的LLM辅助评估流程，结合专家判断验证，对324个RFM进行共计48,276项标准级评估决策。

Result: 评估结果显示，即使评分最高的RFM也仅满足部分标准，表现出特定需求上的局部优势而非全面覆盖，整体工业成熟度有限且不均衡。

Conclusion: 实现工业级RFM的关键不在于孤立的基准性能突破，而在于将安全性、实时可行性、鲁棒感知、人机交互和成本效益的系统集成等要素系统化地纳入可审计的部署架构中。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models (RFMs) are emerging as a promising route towards flexible, instruction- and demonstration-driven robot control, however, a critical investigation of their industrial applicability is still lacking. This survey gives an extensive overview over the RFM-landscape and analyses, driven by concrete implications, how industrial domains and use cases shape the requirements of RFMs, with particular focus on collaborative robot platforms, heterogeneous sensing and actuation, edge-computing constraints, and safety-critical operation. We synthesise industrial deployment perspectives into eleven interdependent implications and operationalise them into an assessment framework comprising a catalogue of 149 concrete criteria, spanning both model capabilities and ecosystem requirements. Using this framework, we evaluate 324 manipulation-capable RFMs via 48,276 criterion-level decisions obtained via a conservative LLM-assisted evaluation pipeline, validated against expert judgements. The results indicate that industrial maturity is limited and uneven: even the highest-rated models satisfy only a fraction of criteria and typically exhibit narrow implication-specific peaks rather than integrated coverage. We conclude that progress towards industry-grade RFMs depends less on isolated benchmark successes than on systematic incorporation of safety, real-time feasibility, robust perception, interaction, and cost-effective system integration into auditable deployment stacks.

</details>


### [357] [Stability-Guided Exploration for Diverse Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06773)
*Eckart Cobo-Briesewitz,Tilman Burghoff,Denis Shcherba,Armand Jordana,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合RRT搜索与基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）的新方法，通过在稳定状态流形上采样，在黑盒仿真中实现多样化的长视界操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据收集是机器人学习中的瓶颈，现有方法依赖人类示范或局部轨迹优化，缺乏多样性与泛化能力。

Method: 结合RRT-style搜索与采样型MPC，引入一种新的采样策略，在稳定状态流形上采样，并在仿真中直接构建搜索树，允许非完全稳定运动的探索。

Result: 该方法能在无任务特定指导的情况下，发现包括推、抓取、旋转、投掷和工具使用在内的多样化操作策略，适用于不同机器人形态。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效生成多样化的长时程操作序列，突破了传统数据收集的局限，为机器人学习提供了更具扩展性的合成数据生成途径。

Abstract: Scaling up datasets is highly effective in improving the performance of deep learning models, including in the field of robot learning. However, data collection still proves to be a bottleneck. Approaches relying on collecting human demonstrations are labor-intensive and inherently limited: they tend to be narrow, task-specific, and fail to adequately explore the full space of feasible states. Synthetic data generation could remedy this, but current techniques mostly rely on local trajectory optimization and fail to find diverse solutions. In this work, we propose a novel method capable of finding diverse long-horizon manipulations through black-box simulation. We achieve this by combining an RRT-style search with sampling-based MPC, together with a novel sampling scheme that guides the exploration toward stable configurations. Specifically, we sample from a manifold of stable states while growing a search tree directly through simulation, without restricting the planner to purely stable motions. We demonstrate the method's ability to discover diverse manipulation strategies, including pushing, grasping, pivoting, throwing, and tool use, across different robot morphologies, without task-specific guidance.

</details>


### [358] [HybridMimic: Hybrid RL-Centroidal Control for Humanoid Motion Mimicking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06775)
*Ludwig Chee-Ying Tay,I-Chia Chang,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HybridMimic的混合框架，通过动态调节基于质心模型的控制器来实现人形机器人复杂任务的学习，结合了运动模仿与基于模型的方法，在硬件实验中表现出优于现有强化学习方法的鲁棒性和跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在部署时通常忽略对机器人动力学的显式推理，导致在分布外环境中可能出现物理上不可行的控制指令；而现有混合方法依赖预定义的接触时序，限制了通用性。因此需要一种更具适应性且动力学感知的控制框架。

Method: 提出HybridMimic框架，利用学习策略动态预测连续接触状态和期望的质心速度，调节基于质心动力学的控制器，并通过物理信息奖励训练策略输出精确的控制目标和前馈力矩。

Result: 在Booster T1人形机器人上的硬件实验表明，相比最先进的强化学习基线，HybridMimic将平均基座位置跟踪误差降低了13%。

Conclusion: HybridMimic通过融合运动模仿与基于质心动力学的控制，实现了在域偏移下仍具物理可行性的控制输出，提升了控制策略在真实硬件上的鲁棒性与部署性能。

Abstract: Motion mimicking, i.e., encouraging the control policy to mimic human motion, facilitates the learning of complex tasks via reinforcement learning (RL) for humanoid robots. Although standard RL frameworks demonstrate impressive locomotion agility, they often bypass explicit reasoning about robot dynamics during deployment, which is a design choice that can lead to physically infeasible commands when the robot encounters out-of-distribution environments. By integrating model-based principles, hybrid approaches can improve performance; however, existing methods typically rely on predefined contact timing, limiting their versatility. This paper introduces HybridMimic, a framework in which a learned policy dynamically modulates a centroidal-model-based controller by predicting continuous contact states and desired centroidal velocities. This architecture exploits the physical grounding of centroidal dynamics to generate feedforward torques that remain feasible even under domain shift. Using physics-informed rewards, the policy is trained to efficiently utilize the centroidal controller's optimization by outputting precise control targets and reference torques. Through hardware experiments on the Booster T1 humanoid, HybridMimic reduces the average base position tracking error by 13\% compared to a state-of-the-art RL baseline, demonstrating the robustness of dynamics-aware deployment.

</details>


### [359] [A Multi-Layer Sim-to-Real Framework for Gaze-Driven Assistive Neck Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06779)
*Colin Rubow,Eric Brewer,Ian Bales,Haohan Zhang,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究利用虚拟现实（VR）技术收集健康个体的眼动与头部运动数据，开发基于眼动预测头部运动的模型，并提出多层控制器选择框架，用于加速直觉化、安全且个性化的辅助颈部外骨骼系统的研发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于神经性疾病导致的颈部肌肉无力引发垂头综合征，严重影响患者生活质量，当前关键挑战在于如何准确预测用户意图的头部运动以实现自然辅助。

Method: 采用虚拟现实环境采集健康受试者的眼-头协同运动数据，训练仅基于眼动预测头部运动的模型；设计一个多层级抽象的控制器评估框架，从仿真、VR到物理外骨骼逐步筛选高性能控制策略。

Result: 成功识别出两种新型眼动驱动模型，在物理外骨骼上表现出良好性能；结果表明不同个体对控制器偏好不同，个性化控制至关重要。

Conclusion: VR结合多层评估框架可有效加速辅助机器人中直观、安全和个性化控制策略的开发，为未来面向用户的康复外骨骼设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dropped head syndrome, caused by neck muscle weakness from neurological diseases, severely impairs an individual's ability to support and move their head, causing pain and making everyday tasks challenging. Our long-term goal is to develop an assistive powered neck exoskeleton that restores natural movement. However, predicting a user's intended head movement remains a key challenge. We leverage virtual reality (VR) to collect coupled eye and head movement data from healthy individuals to train models capable of predicting head movement based solely on eye gaze. We also propose a novel multi-layer controller selection framework, where head control strategies are evaluated across decreasing levels of abstraction -- from simulation and VR to a physical neck exoskeleton. This pipeline effectively rejects poor-performing controllers early, identifying two novel gaze-driven models that achieve strong performance when deployed on the physical exoskeleton. Our results reveal that no single controller is universally preferred, highlighting the necessity for personalization in gaze-driven assistive control. Our work demonstrates the utility of VR-based evaluation for accelerating the development of intuitive, safe, and personalized assistive robots.

</details>


### [360] [Learning-Based Robust Control: Unifying Exploration and Distributional Robustness for Reliable Robotics via Free Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06831)
*Hozefa Jesawada,Giovanni Russo,Abdalla Swikir,Fares Abu-Dakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分布鲁棒自由能原理的策略计算模型，联合学习环境动态和奖励，并在仿真和真实世界实验中验证了其在连续控制任务中的有效性，缩小了仿真到现实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人控制中策略学习与部署时鲁棒性保障的挑战，特别是在存在认知不确定性的情况下。

Method: 基于计算神经科学中的自由能原理，提出了最大扩散学习框架的改进方法，明确表征了策略对环境和奖励中认知不确定性的鲁棒性。

Result: 在连续控制基准上通过仿真和使用Franka Research 3机械臂的真实操作实验验证了方法的有效性，实现了无需任务特定微调的可重复桌面操作。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效联合学习环境动态和奖励，并保证对认知不确定性的鲁棒性，显著缩小仿真到现实的差距，适用于实际机器人控制任务。

Abstract: A key challenge towards reliable robotic control is devising computational models that can both learn policies and guarantee robustness when deployed in the field. Inspired by the free energy principle in computational neuroscience, to address these challenges, we propose a model for policy computation that jointly learns environment dynamics and rewards, while ensuring robustness to epistemic uncertainties. Expounding a distributionally robust free energy principle, we propose a modification to the maximum diffusion learning framework. After explicitly characterizing robustness of our policies to epistemic uncertainties in both environment and reward, we validate their effectiveness on continuous-control benchmarks, via both simulations and real-world experiments involving manipulation with a Franka Research~3 arm. Across simulation and zero-shot deployment, our approach narrows the sim-to-real gap, and enables repeatable tabletop manipulation without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [361] [Receding-Horizon Nullspace Optimization for Actuation-Aware Control Allocation in Omnidirectional UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06832)
*Riccardo Pretto,Mahmoud Hamandi,Abdullah Mohamed Ali,Gokhan Alcan,Anthony Tzes,Fares Abu-Dakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种考虑执行器动态特性的预测控制分配方法，用于全驱动全向无人机，有效抑制电机指令振荡并提升轨迹跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制分配方法忽略了执行器的非对称动力学特性，导致动态机动中出现振荡性电机指令，影响轨迹跟踪精度。

Method: 采用滚动时域优化框架，结合闭环系统前向仿真与约束迭代LQR，在线求解包含非对称电机动力学的最优控制分配问题，并利用冗余执行器通过零空间优化平滑电机指令。

Result: 在OmniOcta平台上仿真表明，相比传统的单步二次规划分配器，该方法显著减少了电机指令的振荡，提升了位置和姿态的轨迹跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的执行器感知的控制分配策略能有效应对全向无人机中的执行器动态非对称问题，通过预测性优化实现更平稳的控制输入和更优的跟踪性能。

Abstract: Fully actuated omnidirectional UAVs enable independent control of forces and torques along all six degrees of freedom, broadening the operational envelope for agile flight and aerial interaction tasks. However, conventional control allocation methods neglect the asymmetric dynamics of the onboard actuators, which can induce oscillatory motor commands and degrade trajectory tracking during dynamic maneuvers. This work proposes a receding-horizon, actuation-aware allocation strategy that explicitly incorporates asymmetric motor dynamics and exploits the redundancy of over-actuated platforms through nullspace optimization. By forward-simulating the closed-loop system over a prediction horizon, the method anticipates actuator-induced oscillations and suppresses them through smooth redistribution of motor commands, while preserving the desired body wrench exactly. The approach is formulated as a constrained optimal control problem solved online via Constrained iterative LQR. Simulation results on the OmniOcta platform demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces motor command oscillations compared to a conventional single-step quadratic programming allocator, yielding improved trajectory tracking in both position and orientation.

</details>


### [362] [RoboCritics: Enabling Reliable End-to-End LLM Robot Programming through Expert-Informed Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06842)
*Callie Y. Kim,Nathan Thomas White,Evan He,Frederic Sala,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboCritics，通过结合大语言模型（LLM）与专家驱动的运动级批评机制，提升面向终端用户的机器人编程安全性与可靠性。系统以透明反馈和一键修复功能帮助用户检测并修正程序中的安全问题，实验表明其在减少安全违规、提高执行质量方面优于传统LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的机器人编程方法生成的代码为“黑箱”，难以验证和调试，存在安全隐患，尤其对新手用户不友好。因此需要一种更透明、可靠且用户可参与的编程辅助机制。

Method: 提出RoboCritics方法，引入基于专家知识的运动级批评模块，分析机器人执行轨迹中的潜在问题（如速度超限、碰撞、末端姿态不安全等），发现问题后向用户提供建议性反馈和一键修复，并将结构化信息反馈给LLM以实现迭代优化。系统集成于一个连接UR3e机器人的网页界面中，并通过一项18人参与的被试间实验进行评估。

Result: 与仅使用LLM的基线系统相比，RoboCritics显著减少了程序执行中的安全违规行为，提升了任务执行质量，并改变了用户验证和修改程序的方式，使编程过程更具可解释性和可控性。

Conclusion: RoboCritics通过融合专家知识与LLM能力，实现了更安全、可靠且以用户为中心的端到端机器人编程，为降低非专业用户使用机器人技术的门槛提供了有效路径。

Abstract: End-user robot programming grants users the flexibility to re-task robots in situ, yet it remains challenging for novices due to the need for specialized robotics knowledge. Large Language Models (LLMs) hold the potential to lower the barrier to robot programming by enabling task specification through natural language. However, current LLM-based approaches generate opaque, "black-box" code that is difficult to verify or debug, creating tangible safety and reliability risks in physical systems. We present RoboCritics, an approach that augments LLM-based robot programming with expert-informed motion-level critics. These critics encode robotics expertise to analyze motion-level execution traces for issues such as joint speed violations, collisions, and unsafe end-effector poses. When violations are detected, critics surface transparent feedback and offer one-click fixes that forward structured messages back to the LLM, enabling iterative refinement while keeping users in the loop. We instantiated RoboCritics in a web-based interface connected to a UR3e robot and evaluated it in a between-subjects user study (n=18). Compared to a baseline LLM interface, RoboCritics reduced safety violations, improved execution quality, and shaped how participants verified and refined their programs. Our findings demonstrate that RoboCritics enables more reliable and user-centered end-to-end robot programming with LLMs.

</details>


### [363] [Robodimm: A Physics-Grounded Framework for Automated Actuator Sizing in Scalable Modular Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06864)
*J. L. Torres,M. Munoz,J. D. Alvarez,J. L. Blanco,A. Gimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robodimm是一个用于可扩展机器人架构中自动执行器选型的软件框架，支持模块化机器人的电机-减速器组合优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有闭合运动链的模块化机器人中，关节力矩耦合且执行器惯性会影响整体性能，因此选择合适的电机-齿轮箱组合尤为困难。

Method: 利用Pinocchio进行动力学计算，Pink进行逆运动学求解，并采用Karush-Kuhn-Tucker（KKT）公式处理约束逆动力学问题，结合参数化缩放、交互式轨迹编程和两轮验证流程。

Result: 实现了对执行器尺寸的自动化优化，并考虑了执行器自重对系统的影响，提升了设计效率与准确性。

Conclusion: Robodimm为复杂机器人结构提供了高效、可扩展的执行器选型解决方案，有助于降低设计成本并提升动态性能。

Abstract: Selecting an appropriate motor-gearbox combination is a critical design task in robotics because it directly affects cost, mass, and dynamic performance. This process is especially challenging in modular robots with closed kinematic chains, where joint torques are coupled and actuator inertia propagates through the mechanism. We present Robodimm, a software framework for automated actuator sizing in scalable robot architectures. By leveraging Pinocchio for dynamics and Pink for inverse kinematics, Robodimm uses a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) formulation for constrained inverse dynamics. The platform supports parametric scaling, interactive trajectory programming through jog modes, and a two-round validation workflow that addresses actuator self-weight effects.

</details>


### [364] [CAR: Cross-Vehicle Kinodynamics Adaptation via Mobility Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06866)
*Tong Xu,Chenhui Pan,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAR框架，通过Transformer编码器和自适应层归一化将车辆轨迹与物理配置嵌入共享的移动性潜在空间，实现跨车辆的动力学快速迁移，在仅需一分钟新数据的情况下显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖大量特定平台数据或简化模型，难以在多样化、异构的无人车平台上扩展，限制了越野自主移动的发展。

Method: 提出Cross-vehicle kinodynamics Adaptation via mobility Representation（CAR）框架，使用带自适应层归一化的Transformer编码器，将不同车辆的轨迹转移和物理配置映射到共享的移动性潜在空间，并通过最近邻提取共性以实现快速适应。

Result: 在基于Chrono引擎的Verti-Bench仿真器上评估，并在Verti-4-Wheeler平台的四种不同物理配置上验证，仅用一分钟新轨迹数据，相比直接邻近迁移，预测误差最多降低67.2%。

Conclusion: CAR实现了跨异构车辆平台的高效移动性知识迁移，显著减少数据需求和计算开销，有效提升模型在新平台上的适应能力。

Abstract: Developing autonomous off-road mobility typically requires either extensive, platform-specific data collection or relies on simplified abstractions, such as unicycle or bicycle models, that fail to capture the complex kinodynamics of diverse platforms, ranging from wheeled to tracked vehicles. This limitation hinders scalability across evolving heterogeneous autonomous robot fleets. To address this challenge, we propose Cross-vehicle kinodynamics Adaptation via mobility Representation (CAR), a novel framework that enables rapid mobility transfer to new vehicles. CAR employs a Transformer encoder with Adaptive Layer Normalization to embed vehicle trajectory transitions and physical configurations into a shared mobility latent space. By identifying and extracting commonality from nearest neighbors within this latent space, our approach enables rapid kinodynamics adaptation to novel platforms with minimal data collection and computational overhead. We evaluate CAR using the Verti-Bench simulator, built on the Chrono multi-physics engine, and validate its performance on four distinct physical configurations of the Verti-4-Wheeler platform. With only one minute of new trajectory data, CAR achieves up to 67.2% reduction in prediction error compared to direct neighbor transfer across diverse unseen vehicle configurations, demonstrating the effectiveness of cross-vehicle mobility knowledge transfer in both simulated and real-world environments.

</details>


### [365] [Material Driven HRI Design: Aesthetics as Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06879)
*Natalie Friedman,Kevin Weatherwax,Chengchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器人设计中美学的重要性，提出设计师应借鉴时尚领域的理念，将颜色、纹理和材料选择作为交互信号，以引导用户对机器人角色和能力的合理预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上机器人设计注重功能而忽视美学，导致用户对机器人产生误解。作者希望通过对材料和外观的设计提升机器人的可解释性和用户体验。

Method: 提出一个框架，用于分析材料如何通过传递交互、任务和环境的预期来增强机器人的可解释性，并对6种机器人进行了内容分析。

Result: 研究表明， thoughtful 的美学设计可以作为交互信号，影响用户对机器人功能的认知；不当的设计则可能导致错误预期。

Conclusion: 美学不应被视为装饰，而应成为机器人设计中的功能性元素，有助于建立用户与机器人之间的清晰沟通。

Abstract: Aesthetics - often treated as secondary to function-guides how people interpret robots' roles. A great deal of robot designs - both real and fictitious - use sleek industrial aesthetics. These feature hard glossy plastics, hiding as much of the underlying mechanical and electrical components as possible, resembling something akin to a nude humanoid figure. This leaves robots as something of a blank slate to which end-users apply coverings to, often based on media of fiction and non-fiction alike. We argue that designers can take cues from fashion to design interaction and set appropriate expectations. Rather than viewing appearance as decoration, we propose that color, texture, and material choices function as interaction signals. These signals can invite or discourage touch, clarify a robot's role, and help align user expectations with a robot's actual capabilities. When done thoughtfully, such cues can create familiarity and legibility; when done poorly, they can lead to wrong expectations. This preliminary paper proposes a framework describing how materials can create explainability by signaling expectations for interaction, task, and environment. We use this framework to do a content analysis of 6 robots.

</details>


### [366] [VertiAdaptor: Online Kinodynamics Adaptation for Vertically Challenging Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06887)
*Tong Xu,Chenhui Pan,Aniket Datar,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VertiAdaptor（VA）的在线自适应框架，通过融合高程与语义嵌入信息，实现对非结构化地形环境下的车辆动力学建模与规划，显著提升了预测精度和自适应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非公路环境中地形复杂多变，传统运动动力学模型难以泛化，缺乏实时适应能力，限制了自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Method: VA利用函数编码器将高程与语义嵌入结合，在离线训练后通过一组神经常微分方程基函数构建动力学空间，并使用高效的最小二乘法在新环境中快速识别动力学特征，实现在线自适应。

Result: 在基于Chrono物理引擎构建的Verti-Bench仿真器及实物Verti-4-Wheeler平台上验证表明，VA相较基线方法提升了最高23.9%的预测精度，并实现了5倍的更快自适应速度。

Conclusion: VertiAdaptor有效增强了自动驾驶系统在复杂、动态非公路环境中的适应能力，推动了无人系统在非结构化地形中导航的鲁棒性与实用性发展。

Abstract: Autonomous driving in off-road environments presents significant challenges due to the dynamic and unpredictable nature of unstructured terrain. Traditional kinodynamic models often struggle to generalize across diverse geometric and semantic terrain types, underscoring the need for real-time adaptation to ensure safe and reliable navigation. We propose VertiAdaptor (VA), a novel online adaptation framework that efficiently integrates elevation with semantic embeddings to enable terrain-aware kinodynamic modeling and planning via function encoders. VA learns a kinodynamic space spanned by a set of neural ordinary differential equation basis functions, capturing complex vehicle-terrain interactions across varied environments. After offline training, the proposed approach can rapidly adapt to new, unseen environments by identifying kinodynamics in the learned space through a computationally efficient least-squares calculation. We evaluate VA within the Verti-Bench simulator, built on the Chrono multi-physics engine, and validate its performance both in simulation and on a physical Verti-4-Wheeler platform. Our results demonstrate that VA improves prediction accuracy by up to 23.9% and achieves a 5X faster adaptation time, advancing the robustness and reliability of autonomous robots in complex and evolving off-road environments.

</details>


### [367] [Collaborative Planning with Concurrent Synchronization for Operationally Constrained UAV-UGV Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06898)
*Zihao Deng,Qianhuang Li,Peng Gao,Maggie Wigness,John Rogers,Donghyun Kim,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoPCS的基于学习的协同规划方法，用于异构无人机（UAV）和地面车辆（UGV）团队在能量和地形约束下的同步并发协同规划，通过异构图变换器与统一模仿学习框架实现高效协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机续航受限、地面车移动受限，单独作业难以完成复杂任务，需实现能源与路径约束下的高效协同规划。

Method: 提出CoPCS框架，结合异构图变换器进行任务编码，利用变换器解码器实现联合同步协同规划，并在统一的模仿学习范式下进行端到端训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上验证了CoPCS的有效性，实现了同步并发协同规划的新能力，显著提升了团队任务性能。

Conclusion: CoPCS为异构机器人团队提供了高效的协同规划能力，能够在实际复杂任务中实现跨平台资源互补与时间同步的协同操作。

Abstract: Collaborative planning under operational constraints is an essential capability for heterogeneous robot teams tackling complex large-scale real-world tasks. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer rapid environmental coverage, but flight time is often limited by energy constraints, whereas Unmanned Ground Vehicles (UGVs) have greater energy capacity to support long-duration missions, but movement is constrained by traversable terrain. Individually, neither can complete tasks such as environmental monitoring. Effective UAV-UGV collaboration therefore requires energy-constrained multi-UAV task planning, traversability-constrained multi-UGV path planning, and crucially, synchronized concurrent co-planning to ensure timely in-mission recharging.
