To enable these capabilities, we propose Collaborative Planning with Concurrent Synchronization (CoPCS), a learning-based approach that integrates a heterogeneous graph transformer for operationally constrained task encoding with a transformer decoder for joint, synchronized co-planning that enables UAVs and UGVs to act concurrently in a coordinated manner. CoPCS is trained end-to-end under a unified imitation learning paradigm. We conducted extensive experiments to evaluate CoPCS in both robotic simulations and physical robot teams. Experimental results demonstrate that our method provides the novel multi-robot capability of synchronized concurrent co-planning and substantially improves team performance. More details of this work are available on the project website: https://hcrlab.gitlab.io/project/CoPCS.

</details>


### [368] [SysNav: Multi-Level Systematic Cooperation Enables Real-World, Cross-Embodiment Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06914)
*Haokun Zhu,Zongtai Li,Zihan Liu,Kevin Guo,Zhengzhi Lin,Yuxin Cai,Guofei Chen,Chen Lv,Wenshan Wang,Jean Oh,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SysNav的三层次系统，用于解决现实世界中的物体导航（ObjectNav）问题，通过解耦语义推理、导航规划和运动控制，在多种实体平台上实现了高效且可靠的长距离复杂环境导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的物体导航面临空间结构复杂、长视野规划和语义理解等多重挑战，现有方法难以有效整合视觉语言模型（VLMs）的能力并保证系统的鲁棒性和通用性。

Method: 将现实世界ObjectNav建模为系统级问题，设计包含高层语义引导、中层基于房间的分层导航策略和低层具身特定运动控制的三级系统；利用VLM进行场景理解和语义指导，并在三个不同实体（轮式机器人、四足机器人和人形机器人）上部署与验证。

Result: 在190次真实世界实验中，SysNav显著提升了任务成功率和导航效率，是首个能在建筑尺度复杂环境中可靠完成长距离物体导航的系统，并在四个仿真基准上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SysNav通过模块化解耦和分层设计，成功整合了VLM的语义能力与实际导航需求，实现了跨具身平台的高效、通用且稳健的现实世界物体导航。

Abstract: Object navigation (ObjectNav) in real-world environments is a complex problem that requires simultaneously addressing multiple challenges, including complex spatial structure, long-horizon planning and semantic understanding. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer promising capabilities for semantic understanding, yet effectively integrating them into real-world navigation systems remains a non-trivial challenge. In this work, we formulate real-world ObjectNav as a system-level problem and introduce SysNav, a three-level ObjectNav system designed for real-world crossembodiment deployment. SysNav decouples semantic reasoning, navigation planning and motion control to ensure robustness and generalizability. At the high-level, we summarize the environment into a structured scene representation and leverage VLMs to provide semantic-grounded navigation guidance. At the mid-level, we introduce a hierarchical room-based navigation strategy that reserves VLM guidance for room-level decisions, which effectively utilizes its reasoning ability while ensuring system efficiency. At the low-level, planned waypoints are executed through different embodiment-specific motion control modules. We deploy our system on three embodiments, a custom-built wheeled robot, the Unitree Go2 quadruped and the Unitree G1 humanoid, and conduct 190 real-world experiments. Our system achieves substantial improvements in both success rate and navigation efficiency. To the best of our knowledge, SysNav is the first system capable of reliably and efficiently completing building-scale long-range object navigation in complex real-world environments. Furthermore, extensive experiments on four simulation benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. Project page is available at: https://cmu-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [369] [LIPP: Load-Aware Informative Path Planning with Physical Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06924)
*Hojune Kim,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了负载感知信息路径规划（LIPP），扩展了传统信息路径规划（C-IPP），通过联合优化路径、访问顺序和采样数量，显式建模采样质量对移动能耗的影响，在能量受限下实现更高的信息获取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息路径规划忽略采样带来的负载增加对后续运动能耗的影响，导致在需要物理采样的任务中能量利用不优，采样效率低下。

Method: 将LIPP建模为混合整数二次规划（MIQP），联合优化路径、访问顺序和每个位置的采样次数，并引入与负载相关的能耗模型。

Result: 理论表明LIPP是C-IPP的严格推广，当样本质量趋近于零时退化为C-IPP；仿真显示随着样本质量增加，LIPP在单位能量的信息减少不确定性方面显著优于C-IPP。

Conclusion: LIPP通过考虑采样质量与能耗的耦合关系，实现了更节能高效的路径规划，特别适用于需物理采样的机器人任务。

Abstract: In classical Informative Path Planning (C-IPP), robots are typically modeled as mobile sensors that acquire digital measurements such as images or radiation levels. In this model - since making a measurement leaves the robot's physical state unchanged - traversal costs are determined solely by the path taken. This is a natural assumption for many missions, but does not extend to settings involving physical sample collection, where each collected sample adds mass and increases the energy cost of all subsequent motion. As a result, IPP formulations that ignore this coupling between information gain and load-dependent traversal cost can produce plans that are distance-efficient but energy-suboptimal, collecting fewer samples and less data than the energy budget would permit. In this paper, we introduce Load-aware Informative Path Planning (LIPP ), a generalization of C-IPP that explicitly models this coupling and the resulting order-dependent traversal costs. We formulate LIPP as a Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) that jointly optimizes routing, visitation order, and per-location sampling count under an energy budget. We show that LIPP strictly generalizes C-IPP: as sample unit mass $λ\to 0$, the load-dependent energy model reduces exactly to the classical distance budget constraint, recovering C-IPP as a special case. We further derive theoretical bounds on the path-length increase of LIPP relative to C-IPP, characterizing the trade-off for improved energy efficiency. Finally, through extensive simulations across 2000 diverse mission scenarios, we demonstrate that LIPP matches the behavior of C-IPP at zero sample mass and progressively achieves higher uncertainty reduction per unit energy as sample mass increases.

</details>


### [370] [A Contrastive Fewshot RGBD Traversability Segmentation Framework for Indoor Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06927)
*Qiyuan An,Tuan Dang,Fillia Makedon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合RGB图像和稀疏1D激光深度信息的多模态分割框架，用于室内可通行区域分割，并引入负对比学习分支和两阶段注意力深度模块，在少样本设置下显著提升了对细小障碍物的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉模型难以检测如椅腿等细小障碍物，存在安全隐患，且依赖大量标注数据；现有少样本分割方法易过拟合支持集，泛化能力差。

Method: 提出多模态分割框架，融合RGB图像与稀疏1D深度信息；设计负对比学习（NCL）分支利用负原型（障碍物）优化自由空间预测；构建两阶段注意力深度模块实现1D深度向量与RGB图像在水平和垂直方向上的对齐。

Result: 在自建室内RGB-D可通行性数据集上实验表明，该方法在1-shot和5-shot设置下mIoU最高提升9%，优于当前少样本分割和RGB-D分割基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用负原型和稀疏深度信息能有效提升少样本条件下室内可通行区域分割的鲁棒性和准确性，尤其改善了对挑战性障碍物的检测能力。

Abstract: Indoor traversability segmentation aims to identify safe, navigable free space for autonomous agents, which is critical for robotic navigation. Pure vision-based models often fail to detect thin obstacles, such as chair legs, which can pose serious safety risks. We propose a multi-modal segmentation framework that leverages RGB images and sparse 1D laser depth information to capture geometric interactions and improve the detection of challenging obstacles. To reduce the reliance on large labeled datasets, we adopt the few-shot segmentation (FSS) paradigm, enabling the model to generalize from limited annotated examples. Traditional FSS methods focus solely on positive prototypes, often leading to overfitting to the support set and poor generalization. To address this, we introduce a negative contrastive learning (NCL) branch that leverages negative prototypes (obstacles) to refine free-space predictions. Additionally, we design a two-stage attention depth module to align 1D depth vectors with RGB images both horizontally and vertically. Extensive experiments on our custom-collected indoor RGB-D traversability dataset demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art FSS and RGB-D segmentation baselines, achieving up to 9\% higher mIoU under both 1-shot and 5-shot settings. These results highlight the effectiveness of leveraging negative prototypes and sparse depth for robust and efficient traversability segmentation.

</details>


### [371] [Failure Mechanisms and Risk Estimation for Legged Robot Locomotion on Granular Slopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06928)
*Xingjue Liao,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过六足机器人在可倾斜的颗粒床面上实验，测量了斜坡依赖的法向和剪切颗粒阻力，并基于这些测量结果开发了一个简单的机器人-地形相互作用模型，用于预测在不同地形强度和坡度角下的锚定时机、步长和机器人速度。该模型揭示了斜坡导致性能下降主要由锚定延迟和后滑增加引起，而非过度沉陷。通过将模型扩展到广义地形条件，构建了失效相图以识别沉陷和打滑引起的失效区域，从而实现对颗粒斜坡上运动的风险定量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决腿式机器人在沙丘等颗粒斜坡上移动时面临的挑战，因为颗粒介质的剪切强度降低以及重力引起的各向异性屈服使得行走变得困难。

Method: 使用六足机器人在可调节倾斜角度的颗粒床面上进行系统性实验，测量机器人的移动速度及颗粒介质的法向和剪切阻力；基于实验数据建立机器人-地形相互作用模型，并进一步推广至一般化地形条件，构建失效相图。

Result: 发现随着坡度增加，剪切阻力显著下降而法向阻力基本不变；模型表明性能下降主要源于锚定延迟和后滑加剧，而非沉陷；成功构建了可区分沉陷与打滑失效区域的相图，可用于风险评估。

Conclusion: 该物理引导的框架能够预测地形依赖的失效机制，为提高机器人在可变形斜坡上的运动安全性与鲁棒性提供了理论支持和实践指导。

Abstract: Locomotion on granular slopes such as sand dunes remains a fundamental challenge for legged robots due to reduced shear strength and gravity-induced anisotropic yielding of granular media. Using a hexapedal robot on a tiltable granular bed, we systematically measure locomotion speed together with slope-dependent normal and shear granular resistive forces. While normal penetration resistance remains nearly unchanged with inclination, shear resistance decreases substantially as slope angle increases. Guided by these measurements, we develop a simple robot-terrain interaction model that predicts anchoring timing, step length, and resulting robot speed, as functions of terrain strength and slope angle. The model reveals that slope-induced performance loss is primarily governed by delayed anchoring and increased backward slip rather than excessive sinkage. By extending the model to generalized terrain conditions, we construct failure phase diagrams that identify sinkage- and slippage-induced failure regimes, enabling quantitative risk estimation for locomotion on granular slopes. This physics-informed framework provides predictive insight into terrain-dependent failure mechanisms and offers guidance for safer and more robust robot operation on deformable inclines.

</details>


### [372] [Feasibility Restoration under Conflicting STL Specifications with Pareto-Optimal Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06947)
*Tianhao Wu,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的两阶段框架，用于处理信号时序逻辑（STL）在安全关键应用中因规范冲突导致的不可行性问题，通过最小松弛恢复可行性，并以多目标优化实现可解释的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中，STL规范可能因安全规则、交通法规和任务目标之间的冲突而无法同时满足，导致传统STL约束的模型预测控制（MPC）失效并采取保守行为（如冻结），增加安全风险。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过最小松弛恢复可行性；第二阶段将问题建模为价值感知的多目标优化问题，并使用ε-约束法逼近帕累托前沿，以分析不同目标间的权衡和反事实行为。

Result: 该方法避免了在冲突STL规范下的死锁问题，并通过自动驾驶案例研究展示了其在安全关键场景中实现可解释决策的能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了STL约束下MPC的不可行性问题，支持在复杂现实环境中进行灵活且可解释的决策，提升了系统在安全关键应用中的鲁棒性与适应性。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is expressive formal language that specifies spatio-temporal requirements in robotics. Its quantitative robustness semantics can be easily integrated with optimization-based control frameworks. However, STL specifications may become conflicting in real-world applications, where safety rules, traffic regulations, and task objectives can be cannot be satisfied together. In these situations, traditional STL-constrained Model Predictive Control (MPC) becomes infeasible and default to conservative behaviors such as freezing, which can largely increase risks in safety-critical scenarios. In this paper, we proposes a unified two-stage framework that first restores feasibility via minimal relaxation, then refine the feasible solution by formulating it as a value-aware multi-objective optimization problem. Using $\varepsilon$-constraint method, we approximate the Pareto front of the multi-objective optimization, which allows analysis of tradeoffs among competing objectives and counterfactual analysis of alternative actions. We demonstrate that the proposed approach avoids deadlock under conflicting STL specifications and enables interpretable decision-making in safety-critical applications by conducting a case study in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [373] [Is Your Safe Controller Actually Safe? A Critical Review of CBF Tautologies and Hidden Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06954)
*Taekyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文批判性地回顾了控制屏障函数（CBFs）在机器人安全中的实际应用，指出了理论假设与实际实现之间的差距，特别是在输入受限系统中。强调了候选CBF与有效CBF的区别，并通过简单低维示例说明了CBF的有效性和常见误用情况，提供了无被动安全系统的可实现安全论证的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CBFs的理论基础已很成熟，但在具有输入约束的系统中，安全控制器的存在性假设与其实际构造之间存在持续的差距。此外，一些所谓的安全机器人策略或控制器仅限于被动安全系统，这限制了其广泛应用。因此，需要更深入分析CBFs的实际有效性并提供实用指导。

Method: 通过分析系统动力学、执行限制和类-K函数之间的相互作用，区分候选CBF与有效CBF；重新审视简单的低维例子，揭示CBF公式何时能提供有效的安全保障以及何时因常见误用而失败；提供构建无被动安全系统中可实现安全论证的实用指南。

Result: 明确了CBF在不同类型系统中的适用条件，揭示了一些现有研究中CBF应用的局限性，尤其是在非被动安全系统中的不足；开发了一个开源交互式网页演示工具，直观展示这些概念。

Conclusion: 本教程旨在弥合理论保障与实际实施之间的差距，强调正确使用CBF的重要性，并为缺乏被动安全特性的系统提供可靠的安全设计方法。

Abstract: This tutorial provides a critical review of the practical application of Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) in robotic safety. While the theoretical foundations of CBFs are well-established, I identify a recurring gap between the mathematical assumption of a safe controller's existence and its constructive realization in systems with input constraints. I highlight the distinction between candidate and valid CBFs by analyzing the interplay of system dynamics, actuation limits, and class-K functions. I further show that some purported demonstrations of safe robot policies or controllers are limited to passively safe systems, such as single integrators or kinematic manipulators, where safety is already inherited from the underlying physics and even naive geometric hard constraints suffice to prevent collisions. By revisiting simple low-dimensional examples, I show when CBF formulations provide valid safety guarantees and when they fail due to common misuses. I then provide practical guidelines for constructing realizable safety arguments for systems without such passive safety. The goal of this tutorial is to bridge the gap between theoretical guarantees and actual implementation, supported by an open-source interactive web demonstration that visualizes these concepts intuitively.

</details>


### [374] [Energy-Efficient Collaborative Transport of Tether-Suspended Payloads via Rotating Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06955)
*Eric Foss,Andrew Tai,Carlo Bosio,Mark W. Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过旋转平衡状态进行协作空中运输系绳负载的方法，利用离心力提供水平张力，使无人机能够产生纯垂直推力，从而降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态平衡方法存在能量损耗大、飞行器间距要求严格等问题，限制了协同空中运输的应用。

Method: 采用稳态圆周运动的旋转平衡状态，利用离心力维持系绳的水平张力，使每个四旋翼无人机仅需产生垂直推力。

Result: 相比静态提升方式，旋转平衡可将功耗降低高达20%，并支持更广泛的系绳配置。

Conclusion: 旋转平衡方法显著提高了协同空中运输的能效和实用性，扩展了其应用场景。

Abstract: Collaborative aerial transportation of tethered payloads is fundamentally limited by space, power, and weight constraints. Conventional approaches rely on static equilibrium conditions, where each vehicle tilts to generate the forces that ensure they maintain a formation geometry that avoids aerodynamic interactions and collision. This horizontal thrust component represents a significant energy penalty compared to the ideal case in which each vehicle produces purely vertical thrust to lift the payload. Operating in tighter tether configurations can minimize this effect, but at the cost of either having to fly the vehicles in closer proximity, which risks collision, or significantly increasing the length of the tether, which increases complexity and reduces potential use-cases. We propose operating the tether-suspended flying system at a rotating equilibrium. By maintaining steady circular motion, centrifugal forces provide the necessary horizontal tether tension, allowing each quadrotor to generate purely vertical thrust and thus reducing the total force (and power) required compared to an equilibrium where the thrusts are not vertical. It also allows for a wider range of tether configurations to be used without sacrificing efficiency. Results demonstrate that rotating equilibria can reduce power consumption relative to static lifting by up to 20%, making collaborative aerial solutions more practically relevant.

</details>


### [375] [VSL-Skin: Individually Addressable Phase-Change Voxel Skin for Variable-Stiffness and Virtual Joints Bridging Soft and Rigid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06979)
*Zihan Oliver Zeng,Jiajun An,Preston Luk,Upinder Kaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为可变刚度晶格皮肤（VSL-Skin）的新型软体机器人系统，首次实现了厘米级精度的体素级形态控制，具备高刚度调节范围、轴向压缩能力、自主自修复功能，并支持虚拟关节的编程化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变刚度系统通常局限于整体段或区域级调控，难以实现精细的刚度分布与虚拟关节定位，限制了软体机器人的适应性与结构强度的平衡。

Method: 提出Variable Stiffness Lattice Skin（VSL-Skin），采用相变材料与行-列寻址架构，结合闭式设计模型与有限元分析，实现体素级刚度控制与预测性刚度模式合成，并通过热循环实现自主组件级自修复。

Result: 系统在轴向、剪切、弯曲和扭转模式下实现近两个数量级的刚度调节；达到30%轴向压缩并保持结构完整性；支持75秒热切换周期与裁剪后仍保持可寻址性；验证了六种典型虚拟关节类型与可编程顺应性。

Conclusion: VSL-Skin实现了体素级可编程刚度控制与结构智能，推动了自主可重构机器人系统的发展，为多尺度机器人平台提供了通用部署方案。

Abstract: Soft robots are compliant but often cannot support loads or hold their shape, while rigid robots provide structural strength but are less adaptable. Existing variable-stiffness systems usually operate at the scale of whole segments or patches, which limits precise control over stiffness distribution and virtual joint placement. This paper presents the Variable Stiffness Lattice Skin (VSL-Skin), the first system to enable individually addressable voxel-level morphological control with centimeter-scale precision. The system provides three main capabilities: nearly two orders of magnitude stiffness modulation across axial (15-1200 N/mm), shear (45-850 N/mm), bending (8*10^2 - 3*10^4 N/deg), and torsional modes with centimeter-scale spatial control; the first demonstrated 30% axial compression in phase-change systems while maintaining structural integrity; and autonomous component-level self-repair through thermal cycling, which eliminates fatigue accumulation and enables programmable sacrificial joints for predictable failure management. Selective voxel activation creates six canonical virtual joint types with programmable compliance while preserving structural integrity in non-activated regions. The platform incorporates closed-form design models and finite element analysis for predictive synthesis of stiffness patterns and joint placement. Experimental validation demonstrates 30% axial contraction, thermal switching in 75-second cycles, and cut-to-fit integration that preserves addressability after trimming. The row-column architecture enables platform-agnostic deployment across diverse robotic systems without specialized infrastructure. This framework establishes morphological intelligence as an engineerable system property and advances autonomous reconfigurable robotics.

</details>


### [376] [Foundational World Models Accurately Detect Bimanual Manipulator Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06987)
*Isaac R. Ward,Michelle Ho,Houjun Liu,Aaron Feldman,Joseph Vincent,Liam Kruse,Sean Cheong,Duncan Eddy,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率性世界模型和共形预测框架的运行时监控方法，用于检测双手机器人在高维感知状态空间中的异常故障，利用预训练视觉基础模型的压缩潜在空间，并在新引入的Bimanual Cable Manipulation数据集上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于双手机器人具有高维图像和本体感觉信号构成的巨大状态空间，显式定义其故障模式不可行，且大规模部署中异常故障可能导致性能下降或安全隐患，因此需要一种有效的异常检测方法。

Method: 在预训练视觉基础模型（NVIDIA Cosmos Tokenizer）的压缩潜在空间中训练一个基于历史信息的概率性世界模型，利用其预测中的不确定性估计作为非一致性分数，结合共形预测框架构建运行时监控器，以识别与异常故障相关的高不确定性时段。

Result: 在模拟Push-T环境和新提出的Bimanual Cable Manipulation数据集上测试，该方法显著优于现有的异常检测和分布外检测基线方法，故障检测率提高3.8%，且所需可训练参数仅为次优学习方法的二十分之一。

Conclusion: 所提方法为在现实世界中安全可靠地部署操作机器人提供了可行路径，尤其适用于对可靠性要求极高的应用场景。

Abstract: Deploying visuomotor robots at scale is challenging due to the potential for anomalous failures to degrade performance, cause damage, or endanger human life. Bimanual manipulators are no exception; these robots have vast state spaces comprised of high-dimensional images and proprioceptive signals. Explicitly defining failure modes within such state spaces is infeasible. In this work, we overcome these challenges by training a probabilistic, history informed, world model within the compressed latent space of a pretrained vision foundation model (NVIDIA's Cosmos Tokenizer). The model outputs uncertainty estimates alongside its predictions that serve as non-conformity scores within a conformal prediction framework. We use these scores to develop a runtime monitor, correlating periods of high uncertainty with anomalous failures. To test these methods, we use the simulated Push-T environment and the Bimanual Cable Manipulation dataset, the latter of which we introduce in this work. This new dataset features trajectories with multiple synchronized camera views, proprioceptive signals, and annotated failures from a challenging data center maintenance task. We benchmark our methods against baselines from the anomaly detection and out-of-distribution detection literature, and show that our approach considerably outperforms statistical techniques. Furthermore, we show that our approach requires approximately one twentieth of the trainable parameters as the next-best learning-based approach, yet outperforms it by 3.8% in terms of failure detection rate, paving the way toward safely deploying manipulator robots in real-world environments where reliability is non-negotiable.

</details>


### [377] [Two-Stage Path Following for Mobile Manipulators via Dimensionality-Reduced Graph Search and Numerical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07003)
*Fuyu Guo,Yuting Mei,Yuyao Zhang,Qian Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于移动机械臂路径跟随的两阶段配置规划框架，通过解耦高维问题实现高效、精确且满足运动学约束的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂在高维构型空间和运动学约束下难以实现高效的路径跟随，现有方法在计算效率与轨迹精度之间存在权衡。

Method: 采用两阶段规划方法：第一阶段基于IRM将任务空间路径离散化为多层图，并利用Dijkstra算法提取初始可行路径；第二阶段将可行基座区域转化为凸包，通过L-BFGS算法进行连续优化以提升平滑性并严格满足可达性约束。

Result: 仿真结果实现了亚毫米级的运动学精度，物理实验验证了该框架在全向移动机械臂上的鲁棒性和实际应用能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了高维构型空间下的路径跟踪难题，在保证计算效率的同时实现了高精度与强约束满足能力，适用于实际移动操作任务。

Abstract: Efficient path following for mobile manipulators is often hindered by high-dimensional configuration spaces and kinematic constraints. This paper presents a robust two-stage configuration planning framework that decouples the 8-DoF planning problem into a tractable 2-DoF base optimization under a yaw-fixed base planning assumption. In the first stage, the proposed approach utilizes IRM to discretize the task-space path into a multi-layer graph, where an initial feasible path is extracted via a Dijkstra-based dynamic programming approach to ensure computational efficiency and global optimality within the discretized graph. In the second stage, to overcome discrete search quantization, feasible base regions are transformed into convex hulls, enabling subsequent continuous refinement via the L-BFGS algorithm to maximize trajectory smoothness while strictly enforcing reachability constraints. Simulation results demonstrate the theoretical precision of the proposed method by achieving sub-millimeter kinematic accuracy in simulation, and physical experiments on an omnidirectional mobile manipulator further validate the framework's robustness and practical applicability.

</details>


### [378] [SSP: Safety-guaranteed Surgical Policy via Joint Optimization of Behavioral and Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07032)
*Jianshu Hu,ZhiYuan Guan,Lei Song,Kantaphat Leelakunwet,Hesheng Wang,Wei Xiao,Qi Dou,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为安全保证外科策略（SSP）的框架，结合数据驱动方法与形式化安全控制，利用神经ODE建模不确定性动态，并通过鲁棒控制屏障函数（CBF）确保手术策略的安全性，在仿真和真实机器人平台上验证了其接近零约束违反率且保持高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习或模仿学习的机器人辅助手术策略缺乏形式化的安全保证，难以满足临床应用的安全需求，因此需要一种既能保持数据驱动策略通用性又能提供严格安全保障的方法。

Method: 提出SSP框架，使用神经常微分方程（Neural ODEs）从示范数据中学习具有不确定性感知的动力学模型，并基于该模型构建鲁棒的控制屏障函数（CBF）安全控制器，以最小干预方式修正外科策略动作，确保在不确定环境下的安全性；同时考虑行为约束和空间约束两类限制条件。

Result: 在SurRoL仿真环境和da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上的实验表明，该方法相较于无约束基线显著降低了约束违反率，接近零违规，同时保持了高任务成功率。

Conclusion: SSP框架有效弥合了数据驱动策略的通用性与手术应用所需的形式化安全之间的鸿沟，为实现安全可靠的自主外科手术提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The paradigm of robot-assisted surgery is shifting toward data-driven autonomy, where policies learned via Reinforcement Learning (RL) or Imitation Learning (IL) enable the execution of complex tasks. However, these ``black-box" policies often lack formal safety guarantees, a critical requirement for clinical deployment. In this paper, we propose the Safety-guaranteed Surgical Policy (SSP) framework to bridge the gap between data-driven generality and formal safety. We utilize Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (Neural ODEs) to learn an uncertainty-aware dynamics model from demonstration data. This learned model underpins a robust Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety controller, which minimally alters the actions of a surgical policy to ensure strict safety under uncertainty. Our controller enforces two constraint categories: behavioral constraints (restricting the task space of the agent) and spatial constraints (defining surgical no-go zones). We instantiate the SSP framework with surgical policies derived from RL, IL and Control Lyapunov Functions (CLF). Validation on in both the SurRoL simulation and da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) demonstrates that our method achieves a near-zero constraint violation rate while maintaining high task success rates compared to unconstrained baselines.

</details>


### [379] [TacDexGrasp: Compliant and Robust Dexterous Grasping with Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07040)
*Yubin Ke,Jiayi Chen,Hang Lv,Xiao Zhou,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于触觉反馈和二阶锥规划（SOCP）的控制器，用于多指手抓取未知物体时的力控制，无需显式建模扭矩或检测打滑，通过约束每个接触点的切向与法向力比值来防止平移和旋转打滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手在抓取未知物体时具有柔顺性和鲁棒性潜力，但其高维力控制面临挑战，尤其是如何分配接触力以抵抗重力并防止因物体质心偏移导致的旋转打滑。

Method: 利用触觉反馈和基于二阶锥规划（SOCP）的控制器，通过主动限制每个手指接触点处的切向与法向力之比低于估计的摩擦系数，从而维持抓取稳定性，且不依赖显式的扭矩建模或打滑检测。

Result: 实验证明该方法在12种不同物体上均能有效防止旋转和 translational 打滑，表现出良好的鲁棒性和顺应性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过简单的力比约束实现了对多指抓取中复杂不稳定问题的有效控制，提升了抓取系统的通用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-fingered hands offer great potential for compliant and robust grasping of unknown objects, yet their high-dimensional force control presents a significant challenge. This work addresses two key problems: (1) distributing forces across multiple contacts to counteract an object's weight, and (2) preventing rotational slip caused by gravitational torque when a grasp is distant from the object's center of mass. We address these challenges via tactile feedback and a Second-Order Cone Programming (SOCP)-based controller, without explicit torque modeling or slip detection. Our key insights are (1) rotational slip inevitably induces translational slip at some contact points for a multi-fingered grasp, and (2) the ratio of tangential to normal force at each contact is an effective early stability indicator. By actively constraining this ratio for each finger below the estimated friction coefficient, our controller maintains grasp stability against both translational and rotational slip. Real-world experiments on 12 diverse objects demonstrate the robustness and compliance of our approach.

</details>


### [380] [GuideTWSI: A Diverse Tactile Walking Surface Indicator Dataset from Synthetic and Real-World Images for Blind and Low-Vision Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07060)
*Hochul Hwang,Soowan Yang,Anh N. H. Nguyen,Parth Goel,Krisha Adhikari,Sunghoon I. Lee,Joydeep Biswas,Nicholas A. Giudice,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文强调了触觉步行表面指示器（TWSIs）对盲人和低视力（BLV）行人的关键作用，并指出现有数据集在地理和形态上的局限性，尤其是缺乏对北美洲和欧洲常用的截断圆顶型TWSI的覆盖，导致模型泛化能力差。为提升导航辅助系统的可靠性，亟需包含多样化TWSI类型和视角的大规模标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有城市感知数据集中TWSIs样本严重不足，且现有专用数据集存在地理偏倚（主要集中于东亚的条形导向铺面），忽略了北美和欧洲广泛使用的截断圆顶型铺面，导致基于这些数据训练的模型难以泛化到不同类型的TWSI，影响BLV个体的安全导航。

Method: 通过与BLV导盲犬使用者、训练师及定向移动（O&M）专家的观察会话，确认准确分割TWSIs对导航辅助的重要性，并分析当前数据集在视角（如自我中心或俯视视角）和地理代表性方面的局限性。

Result: 发现仅基于条形导向铺面数据训练的模型在识别截断圆顶类警告时表现不佳，易出现漏检和误停，暴露了现有方法在跨区域和跨形态场景下的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 为了实现可靠的导航辅助，必须构建包含多种TWSI类型（特别是截断圆顶）和多视角（如机器人相关的自我中心视角）的大规模标注数据集，以提升模型在真实世界中的泛化能力和安全性。

Abstract: Tactile Walking Surface Indicators (TWSIs) are safety-critical landmarks that blind and low-vision (BLV) pedestrians use to locate crossings and hazard zones. From our observation sessions with BLV guide dog handlers, trainers, and an O&M specialist, we confirmed the critical importance of reliable and accurate TWSI segmentation for navigation assistance of BLV individuals. Achieving such reliability requires large-scale annotated data. However, TWSIs are severely underrepresented in existing urban perception datasets, and even existing dedicated paving datasets are limited: they lack robot-relevant viewpoints (e.g., egocentric or top-down) and are geographically biased toward East Asian directional bars - raised parallel strips used for continuous guidance along sidewalks. This narrow focus overlooks truncated domes - rows of round bumps used primarily in North America and Europe as detectable warnings at curbs, crossings, and platform edges. As a result, models trained only on bar-centric data struggle to generalize to dome-based warnings, leading to missed detections and false stops in safety-critical environments.

</details>


### [381] [Morphology-Independent Facial Expression Imitation for Human-Face Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07068)
*Xu Chen,Rui Gao,Che Sun,Zhehang Liu,Yuwei Wu,Shuo Yang,Yunde Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形态无关的面部表情模仿方法，通过解耦表情与面部形态来实现更逼真的类人机器人面部表情生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将2D面部关键点映射到机器人执行器命令时，易受面部形态干扰，导致表情模仿性能下降。

Method: 构建了一个自监督的表情解耦模块以分离表情表征与形态表征，并设计了基于感知表情误差的学习目标的表情迁移模块，将表征映射到机器人的执行器命令。

Result: 在自主研发的高度拟人化机器人Pengrui上进行了广泛实验，验证了该方法能有效复现多种类人表情。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够消除面部形态影响，实现更准确、自然的面部表情模仿，提升人机交互体验。

Abstract: Accurate facial expression imitation on human-face robots is crucial for achieving natural human-robot interaction. Most existing methods have achieved photorealistic expression imitation through mapping 2D facial landmarks to a robot's actuator commands. Their imitation of landmark trajectories is susceptible to interference from facial morphology, which would lead to a performance drop. In this paper, we propose a morphology-independent expression imitation method that decouples expressions from facial morphology to eliminate morphological influence and produce more realistic expressions for human-face robots. Specifically, we construct an expression decoupling module to learn expression semantics by disentangling the expression representation from the morphology representation in a self-supervised manner. We devise an expression transfer module to map the representations to the robot's actuator commands through a learning objective of perceiving expression errors, producing accurate facial expressions based on the learned expression semantics. To support experimental validation, a custom-designed and highly expressive human-face robot, namely Pengrui, is developed to serve as an experimental platform for realistic expression imitation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method enables the human-face robot to reproduce a wide range of human-like expressions effectively. All code and implementation details of the robot will be released.

</details>


### [382] [VLN-Cache: Enabling Token Caching for VLN Models with Visual/Semantic Dynamics Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07080)
*Zihao Zheng,Zhihao Mao,Xingyue Zhou,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Zhaobo Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Donggang Cao,Hong Mei,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN-Cache是一种面向视觉与语义动态变化的缓存框架，通过视图对齐重映射和任务相关性显著性过滤，在保证导航性能的同时实现高效推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token缓存的方法在视觉语言导航（VLN）中因假设相机静止和语义焦点固定而失效，无法应对视角变化和任务阶段演进带来的视觉与语义动态问题。

Method: 提出VLN-Cache框架，引入视图对齐重映射以恢复几何对应关系，设计任务相关性显著性滤波器来抑制语义转换时的缓存复用，并采用层自适应熵策略平衡各层的复用预算。

Result: 在R2R-CE仿真基准上的实验表明，相比基线模型，VLN-Cache最高可实现1.52倍的推理加速，同时保持具有竞争力的导航成功率。

Conclusion: VLN-Cache有效解决了VLN中因视觉和语义动态导致的缓存失效问题，为大模型在实时导航任务中的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) increasingly relies on large vision-language models, but their inference cost conflicts with real-time deployment. Token caching is a promising training-free strategy that avoids redundant computation by reusing stable visual tokens across frames. However, existing methods assume a static camera and fixed semantic focus, assumptions that VLN fundamentally violates. We identify two failure modes: (1) visual dynamics, where viewpoint shift displaces token positions across frames, causing position-wise matching to pair misaligned content; (2) semantic dynamics, where token relevance shifts across task stages as navigation progresses, making cached states stale. We propose VLN-Cache, a visual-dynamic-aware and semantic-dynamic-aware caching framework that introduces view-aligned remapping to recover geometric correspondences and a task-relevance saliency filter to veto reuse at semantic transitions. A layer-adaptive entropy policy further balances the per-layer reuse budget. Experiments on the R2R-CE simulation benchmark show up to 1.52x speedup while maintaining competitive navigation success rates.

</details>


### [383] [ACLM: ADMM-Based Distributed Model Predictive Control for Collaborative Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07095)
*Ziyi Zhou,Pengyuan Shu,Ruize Cao,Yuntian Zhao,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ADMM的分布式模型预测控制框架，用于四足机器人团队在复杂环境中协同搬运重物，具有良好的可扩展性、实时性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式规划方法难以扩展，而分层或完全去中心化的方法常忽略动力学耦合，导致行为保守，因此需要一种兼顾动态耦合与实时性能的协同控制方法。

Method: 采用基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的分布式模型预测控制，将全局优化问题分解为多个机器人节点的子问题，并通过共识约束实现协调；结合滑动时域规划与力矩感知的全身控制器，实现运动与交互力矩的跟踪。

Result: 在最多四个机器人的仿真中验证了该方法具备良好的可扩展性、实时性能（每周期仅需几次ADMM迭代）和对模型不确定性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式框架有效平衡了动态耦合建模与计算效率，适用于多机器人协同操作任务，展现出在复杂环境中实际部署的潜力。

Abstract: Collaborative transportation of heavy payloads via loco-manipulation is a challenging yet essential capability for legged robots operating in complex, unstructured environments. Centralized planning methods, e.g., holistic trajectory optimization, capture dynamic coupling among robots and payloads but scale poorly with system size, limiting real-time applicability. In contrast, hierarchical and fully decentralized approaches often neglect force and dynamic interactions, leading to conservative behavior. This study proposes an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM)-based distributed model predictive control framework for collaborative loco-manipulation with a team of quadruped robots with manipulators. By exploiting the payload-induced coupling structure, the global optimal control problem is decomposed into parallel individual-robot-level subproblems with consensus constraints. The distributed planner operates in a receding-horizon fashion and achieves fast convergence, requiring only a few ADMM iterations per planning cycle. A wrench-aware whole-body controller executes the planned trajectories, tracking both motion and interaction wrenches. Extensive simulations with up to four robots demonstrate scalability, real-time performance, and robustness to model uncertainty.

</details>


### [384] [Towards Scalable Probabilistic Human Motion Prediction with Gaussian Processes for Safe Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07096)
*Jinger Chong,Xiaotong Zhang,Kamal Youcef-Toumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于结构化多任务变分高斯过程的全身体人类运动预测框架，具有良好的不确定性校准能力、低参数量和实时部署潜力，在Human3.6M数据集上表现出优异的概率预测性能和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作中，机器人需要准确且可靠地预测人类运动以确保安全，现有方法在不确定性估计、模型复杂度或运动学一致性方面存在不足。

Method: 采用结构化多任务变分高斯过程（GP）框架，利用时序相关性和关节维度分解实现可扩展性，并引入连续6D旋转表示以保持运动学一致性。

Result: 在Human3.6M数据集上，KDE NLL比强基线低50倍，CRPS为0.021 m，确定性MAE略高于深度学习方法3-18%；模型仅需0.24-0.35M参数，推理时间短，具备实时性；置信区间实证覆盖分析显示良好的校准性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于高斯过程的可扩展模型能在保持高预测精度的同时提供可靠、可解释的不确定性估计，适用于运动规划和避障等机器人下游任务。

Abstract: Accurate human motion prediction with well-calibrated uncertainty is critical for safe human-robot collaboration (HRC), where robots must anticipate and react to human movements in real time. We propose a structured multitask variational Gaussian Process (GP) framework for full-body human motion prediction that captures temporal correlations and leverages joint-dimension-level factorization for scalability, while using a continuous 6D rotation representation to preserve kinematic consistency. Evaluated on Human3.6M (H3.6M), our model achieves up to 50 lower kernel density estimate negative log-likelihood (KDE NLL) than strong baselines, a mean continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) of 0.021 m, and deterministic mean angle error (MAE) that is 3-18% higher than competitive deep learning methods. Empirical coverage analysis shows that the fraction of ground-truth outcomes contained within predicted confidence intervals gradually decreases with horizon, remaining conservative for lower-confidence intervals and near-nominal for higher-confidence intervals, with only modest calibration drift at longer horizons. Despite its probabilistic formulation, our model requires only 0.24-0.35 M parameters, roughly eight times fewer than comparable approaches, and exhibits modest inference times, indicating suitability for real-time deployment. Extensive ablation studies further validated the choice of 6D rotation representation and Matern 3/2 + Linear kernel, and guided the selection of the number of inducing points and latent dimensionality. These results demonstrate that scalable GP-based models can deliver competitive accuracy together with reliable and interpretable uncertainty estimates for downstream robotics tasks such as motion planning and collision avoidance.

</details>


### [385] [Learning From Failures: Efficient Reinforcement Learning Control with Episodic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07110)
*Chenyang Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FEMA的技术，通过存储失败经验来避免机器人在强化学习中反复进入不稳定状态，从而提高样本效率和长视野探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有挑战性的探索和接触丰富的动态环境中，早期训练常因碰撞和跌倒等提前终止而主导，导致学习被短视距、低回报的轨迹所淹没，阻碍收敛并限制长视距探索。

Method: 提出Failure Episodic Memory Alert (FEMA) 技术，利用情景记忆模块显式存储短视距的失败经验，并在交互过程中检索相似的失败经验，防止机器人反复陷入不稳定状态，引导策略走向更具长期价值的长视距轨迹。

Result: FEMA与无模型强化学习算法结合后，在MuJoCo任务上多个经典RL算法中实现了33.11%的样本效率提升，并在并行PPO训练框架中验证了其在真实双足机器人任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: FEMA能有效缓解早期训练中的频繁失败问题，促进长视野探索，显著提升样本效率，适用于模拟和真实机器人任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in robot learning. However, under challenging exploration and contact-rich dynamics, early-stage training is frequently dominated by premature terminations such as collisions and falls. As a result, learning is overwhelmed by short-horizon, low-return trajectories, which hinder convergence and limit long-horizon exploration. To alleviate this issue, we propose a technique called Failure Episodic Memory Alert (FEMA). FEMA explicitly stores short-horizon failure experiences through an episodic memory module. During interactions, it retrieves similar failure experiences and prevents the robot from recurrently relapsing into unstable states, guiding the policy toward long-horizon trajectories with greater long-term value. FEMA can be combined easily with model-free reinforcement learning algorithms, and yields a substantial sample-efficiency improvement of 33.11% on MuJoCo tasks across several classical RL algorithms. Furthermore, integrating FEMA into a parallelized PPO training pipeline demonstrates its effectiveness on a real-world bipedal robot task.

</details>


### [386] [DexKnot: Generalizable Visuomotor Policy Learning for Dexterous Bag-Knotting Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07136)
*Jiayuan Zhang,Ruihai Wu,Haojun Chen,Yuran Wang,Yifan Zhong,Ceyao Zhang,Yaodong Yang,Yuanpei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexKnot框架，结合关键点效用与扩散策略，实现机器人对未见塑料袋形变的可泛化打结操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在面对未见过的塑料袋实例或复杂形变时泛化能力差，难以应对高自由度和动态变化的柔性物体操作。

Method: 通过真实手动变形数据学习形状无关的关键点表征，并利用关键点匹配确定未知袋形的关键位置；将这些关键点输入基于扩散变换器的策略模型，结合少量人类演示生成机器人动作。

Result: DexKnot在多种未见袋型和变形下均实现了稳定且一致的打结性能，显著提升了策略的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DexKnot通过降维至稀疏关键点表示，有效解决了柔性物体操作中的泛化难题，为机器人处理高自由度日常任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Knotting plastic bags is a common task in daily life, yet it is challenging for robots due to the bags' infinite degrees of freedom and complex physical dynamics. Existing methods often struggle in generalization to unseen bag instances or deformations. To address this, we present DexKnot, a framework that combines keypoint affordance with diffusion policy to learn a generalizable bag-knotting policy. Our approach learns a shape-agnostic representation of bags from keypoint correspondence data collected through real-world manual deformation. For an unseen bag configuration, the keypoints can be identified by matching the representation to a reference. These keypoints are then provided to a diffusion transformer, which generates robot action based on a small number of human demonstrations. DexKnot enables effective policy generalization by reducing the dimensionality of observation space into a sparse set of keypoints. Experiments show that DexKnot achieves reliable and consistent knotting performance across a variety of previously unseen instances and deformations.

</details>


### [387] [Model-based thermal drift compensation for high-precision hexapod robot actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07141)
*Clément Robert,Alain Vissiere,Olivier Company,Pierre Noire,Thierry Roux,Sébastien Krut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于表面温度点建模的方法，用于预测和校正六足机器人中精密机电执行器的热漂移，实验验证可减少超过80%的热致膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热膨胀是高精度六足机器人中定位误差的重要来源，影响机器人的精度和重复性，需通过有效方法进行热漂移补偿。

Method: 建立执行器膨胀状态与其表面若干关键点温度之间的理论模型，并在专用试验台上通过高精度干涉测量系统进行实验标定与验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能将热引起的执行器膨胀减少超过80%。

Conclusion: 该方法为六足机器人及其他类似机器人部件的完整热漂移校正提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Thermal expansion is a significant source of positioning error in high-precision hexapod robots (Gough-Stewart platforms). Any variation in the temperature of the hexapod's parts induces expansion, which alters their kinematic model and reduces the robot's accuracy and repeatability. These variations may arise from internal heat sources (such as motors, encoders, and electronics) or from environmental changes. In this study, a method is proposed to anticipate and therefore correct the thermal drift of one of the hexapod precision electro-mechanical actuators. This method is based on determining a model that links the expansion state of the actuator at any given moment to the temperature of some well-chosen points on its surface. This model was initially developed theoretically. Its coefficients were then adjusted experimentally on a specific test-bench, based on a rigorous measurement campaign of actuator expansion using a high-precision interferometric measurement system. Experimental validation demonstrates a reduction of thermally induced expansion by more than 80%. This paves the way for thermal drift correction across the entire robot or similar robotics parts.

</details>


### [388] [Tutorial on Aided Inertial Navigation Systems: A Modern Treatment Using Lie-Group Theoretical Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07143)
*Soulaimane Berkane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于李群SE_2(3)的辅助惯性导航系统，强调了不变性和对称性在多传感器融合中的作用，提供了一个面向控制且便于实现的几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更清晰地理解和实现现代辅助惯性导航系统，需要一个具有明确几何结构和对称性特性的理论框架。

Method: 采用以扩展特殊欧几里得群SE_2(3)为中心的李群方法，构建具有不变性的状态估计框架，并讨论高阶表示、同步观测器与等变滤波等扩展方法。

Result: 提出了一种统一的、系统理论化的几何框架，能够有效融合惯性测量与辅助信息，提升导航系统的鲁棒性与精度。

Conclusion: 该方法为现代辅助惯性导航提供了清晰、可实现的理论基础，有助于推动其在实际工程中的应用。

Abstract: This tutorial presents a control-oriented introduction to aided inertial navigation systems using a Lie-group formulation centered on the extended Special Euclidean group SE_2(3). The focus is on developing a clear and implementation-oriented geometric framework for fusing inertial measurements with aiding information, while making the role of invariance and symmetry explicit. Recent extensions, including higher-order state representations, synchronous observer designs, and equivariant filtering methods, are discussed as natural continuations of the same underlying principles. The goal is to provide readers with a coherent system-theoretic perspective that supports both understanding and practical use of modern aided inertial navigation methods.

</details>


### [389] [RoTri-Diff: A Spatial Robot-Object Triadic Interaction-Guided Diffusion Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07165)
*Zixuan Chen,Nga Teng Chan,Yiwen Hou,Chenrui Tie,Zixuan Liu,Haonan Chen,Junting Chen,Jieqi Shi,Yang Gao,Jing Huo,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的双臂操作中机器人-物体三元交互（RoTri）表示方法，并结合扩散模型实现了更稳定的模仿学习框架RoTri-Diff，显著提升了双臂协调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂操作的模仿学习方法忽略了两个机械臂与物体之间的动态几何关系，导致协作不稳定、抓取失败和碰撞等问题。

Method: 通过建模机器人-物体三元交互（RoTri），编码双臂与物体之间的相对6D位姿，建立连续的三角几何约束，并在此基础上提出RoTri-Diff，一种分层扩散模型，融合RoTri约束、机器人关键姿态和物体运动进行轨迹生成。

Result: 在11个RLBench2任务上比现有最先进方法平均提升10.2%，并在4个具有挑战性的现实世界双臂任务中实现了稳定表现。

Conclusion: 显式建模双臂与物体之间的三元几何关系对于实现稳定、协调的双臂操作至关重要，RoTri-Diff为复杂双臂任务提供了一个鲁棒且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation is a fundamental robotic skill that requires continuous and precise coordination between two arms. While imitation learning (IL) is the dominant paradigm for acquiring this capability, existing approaches, whether robot-centric or object-centric, often overlook the dynamic geometric relationship among the two arms and the manipulated object. This limitation frequently leads to inter-arm collisions, unstable grasps, and degraded performance in complex tasks. To address this, in this paper we explicitly models the Robot-Object Triadic Interaction (RoTri) representation in bimanual systems, by encoding the relative 6D poses between the two arms and the object to capture their spatial triadic relationship and establish continuous triangular geometric constraints. Building on this, we further introduce RoTri-Diff, a diffusion-based imitation learning framework that combines RoTri constraints with robot keyposes and object motion in a hierarchical diffusion process. This enables the generation of stable, coordinated trajectories and robust execution across different modes of bimanual manipulation. Extensive experiments show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 10.2% on 11 representative RLBench2 tasks and achieves stable performance on 4 challenging real-world bimanual tasks. Project website: https://rotri-diff.github.io/.

</details>


### [390] [Vision-Guided MPPI for Agile Drone Racing: Navigating Arbitrary Gate Poses via Neural Signed Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07199)
*Fangguo Zhao,Hanbing Zhang,Zhouheng Li,Xin Guan,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Gate-SDF和MPPI的机载视觉引导最优控制框架，实现无需参考轨迹的自主无人机竞速飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预计算轨迹或精确门位姿估计，对环境扰动和传感器噪声敏感，且难以泛化；端到端学习易过拟合赛道布局。

Method: 构建Gate-SDF神经符号距离场，直接从原始深度图学习连续空间场，提供避障排斥力与几何引导，并将其嵌入基于采样的MPPI控制器中，利用GPU并行实现实时轨迹优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中成功实现高速穿越任意放置和朝向的门，具备强鲁棒性，可应对严重遮挡和未建模的门位置姿态变化。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了不依赖参考轨迹的敏捷飞行，提升了无人机在动态复杂环境下的自主导航能力。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing requires the tight coupling of perception, planning, and control under extreme agility. However, recent approaches typically rely on precomputed spatial reference trajectories or explicit 6-DoF gate pose estimation, rendering them brittle to spatial perturbations, unmodeled track changes, and sensor noise. Conversely, end-to-end learning policies frequently overfit to specific track layouts and struggle with zero-shot generalization. To address these fundamental limitations, we propose a fully onboard, vision guided optimal control framework that enables reference-free agile flight through arbitrarily placed and oriented gates. Central to our approach is Gate-SDF, a novel, implicitly learned neural signed distance field. Gate-SDF directly processes raw, noisy depth images to predict a continuous spatial field that provides both collision repulsion and active geometric guidance toward the valid traversal area. We seamlessly integrate this representation into a sampling-based Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. By fully exploiting GPU parallelism, the framework evaluates these continuous spatial constraints across thousands of simulated trajectory rollouts simultaneously in real time. Furthermore, our formulation inherently maintains spatial consistency, ensuring robust navigation even under severe visual occlusion during aggressive maneuvers. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed system achieves high-speed agile flight and successfully navigates unseen tracks subject to severe unmodeled gate displacements and orientation perturbations. Videos are available at https://zhaofangguo.github.io/vision_guided_mppi/

</details>


### [391] [Soft Rigid Hybrid Gripper with Inflatable Silicone Pockets for Tunable Frictional Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07308)
*Hoang Hiep Ly,Cong-Nhat Nguyen,Doan-Quang Tran,Quoc-Khanh Dang,Ngoc Duy Tran,Thi Thoa Mac,Anh Nguyen,Xuan-Thuan Nguyen,Tung D. Ta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种软硬混合夹持器手指，通过调节硅胶气囊的内部气压来主动调节表面摩擦力，从而在不增加夹持力的情况下稳定抓取各种物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性夹持器依赖增大正压力来固定物体，容易损坏脆弱物品，因此需要一种能减少损伤又能可靠抓取多种材质物体的解决方案。

Method: 设计并集成具有刚性结构外壳和可充气硅胶软袋的混合夹持指，通过改变气囊内压调节表面摩擦力，并进行基础实验验证压力与摩擦系数的关系及抓取性能。

Result: 实验证明，增加气囊内压可提高有效摩擦系数，夹持器能稳定提起重物、易滑物体以及鸡蛋、豆腐、水果和纸杯等易碎变形物体，且损伤极小。

Conclusion: 该混合夹持指通过调控摩擦力而非增加夹持力，实现了对多样机械特性物体的安全可靠抓取，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Grasping objects with diverse mechanical properties, such as heavy, slippery, or fragile items, remains a significant challenge in robotics. Conventional rigid grippers typically rely on increasing the normal forces to secure an object, however, this can cause damage to fragile objects due to excessive force. To address this limitation, we propose a soft rigid hybrid gripper finger that combines rigid structural shells with soft, inflatable silicone pockets, which could be integrated into a conventional gripper. The hybrid gripper can actively modulate its surface friction by varying the internal air pressure of the silicone pockets, enabling the gripper to securely grasp objects without increasing the gripping force. This is demonstrated by fundamental experimental results, in which an increase in internal pressure leads to a proportional increase in the effective coefficient of friction. The gripping experiments also show that the integrated gripper can stably lift heavy and slippery objects or fragile, deformable objects, such as eggs, tofu, fruits, and paper cups, with minimal damage by increasing friction rather than applying high force.

</details>


### [392] [Underwater Embodied Intelligence for Autonomous Robots: A Constraint-Coupled Perspective on Planning, Control, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07393)
*Jingzehua Xu,Guanwen Xie,Jiwei Tang,Shuai Zhang,Xiaofan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向水下自主机器人的“约束耦合”视角，强调感知、规划、控制与通信、能源等物理限制在真实海洋环境中的紧密耦合关系，综述了强化学习、信念感知规划等技术进展，并提出跨层失效分类体系，指出了通向可验证、鲁棒水下智能的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人在真实海洋环境中面临水动力不确定性、观测不完整、通信带宽受限和能源稀缺等多重耦合约束，传统将这些视为独立扰动的方法难以实现可靠自主，亟需一种系统性框架来理解并应对这些交织挑战。

Method: 本文采用“具身智能”的视角，将水下自主性视为感知-规划-控制闭环中多层约束的耦合结果，综合分析强化学习、混合控制、多机器人协同等技术在典型应用场景中的表现，提出一个涵盖认知、动态与协同失效的跨层故障分类体系，并分析不确定性下错误在各层间的传播机制。

Result: 揭示了环境监测、检测、探索和协同任务中不同的约束压力特征；提出了统一的跨层失效分类法，阐明了错误如何在感知、规划与控制层间级联放大；识别出若干关键研究方向，包括基于物理规律的世界模型、可验证的学习型控制、通信感知协同机制和部署导向的系统设计。

Conclusion: 通过将约束耦合内化为系统设计的核心要素而非外部干扰，水下具身智能有望从性能驱动的适应性行为，演进为在真实海洋条件下具备韧性、可扩展且可验证的自主能力。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots are increasingly deployed for environmental monitoring, infrastructure inspection, subsea resource exploration, and long-horizon exploration. Yet, despite rapid advances in learning-based planning and control, reliable autonomy in real ocean environments remains fundamentally constrained by tightly coupled physical limits. Hydrodynamic uncertainty, partial observability, bandwidth-limited communication, and energy scarcity are not independent challenges; they interact within the closed perception-planning-control loop and often amplify one another over time. This Review develops a constraint-coupled perspective on underwater embodied intelligence, arguing that planning and control must be understood within tightly coupled sensing, communication, coordination, and resource constraints in real ocean environments. We synthesize recent progress in reinforcement learning, belief-aware planning, hybrid control, multi-robot coordination, and foundation-model integration through this embodied perspective. Across representative application domains, we show how environmental monitoring, inspection, exploration, and cooperative missions expose distinct stress profiles of cross-layer coupling. To unify these observations, we introduce a cross-layer failure taxonomy spanning epistemic, dynamic, and coordination breakdowns, and analyze how errors cascade across autonomy layers under uncertainty. Building on this structure, we outline research directions toward physics-grounded world models, certifiable learning-enabled control, communication-aware coordination, and deployment-aware system design. By internalizing constraint coupling rather than treating it as an external disturbance, underwater embodied intelligence may evolve from performance-driven adaptation toward resilient, scalable, and verifiable autonomy under real ocean conditions.

</details>


### [393] [Perceptive Variable-Timing Footstep Planning for Humanoid Locomotion on Disconnected Footholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07400)
*Zhaoyang Xiang,Upama Pant,Ayonga Hereid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合整数模型预测控制的足式机器人步态规划框架，结合感知信息实现动态一致、安全且自适应的足部放置与步长时间联合规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含障碍物和不安全地面的真实行走场景中，需要在离散可踩区域上进行安全稳定的步态规划，传统方法难以兼顾动态一致性与实时性。

Method: 采用机载感知的深度图像构建概率局部高度图，提取凸形可踏区域并用二元变量在混合整数二次规划中约束区域隶属关系；利用DCM动力学联合规划落脚点与步长时间，并嵌入捕获性边界保证安全性，通过反向传播实测DCM实现步内重规划。

Result: 在Digit机器人仿真中验证了方法的有效性，能够在随机分布的 stepping-stone 场景中生成地形感知、动态一致的步序，具备抗外力扰动能力，求解时间达毫秒级。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了复杂地形下高效、安全、鲁棒的步态规划，为双足机器人在非连续地面上的行走提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Many real-world walking scenarios contain obstacles and unsafe ground patches (e.g., slippery or cluttered areas), leaving a disconnected set of admissible footholds that can be modeled as stepping-stone-like regions. We propose an onboard, perceptive mixed-integer model predictive control framework that jointly plans foot placement and step duration using step-to-step Divergent Component of Motion (DCM) dynamics. Ego-centric depth images are fused into a probabilistic local heightmap, from which we extract a union of convex steppable regions. Region membership is enforced with binary variables in a mixed-integer quadratic program (MIQP). To keep the optimization tractable while certifying safety, we embed capturability bounds in the DCM space: a lateral one-step condition (preventing leg crossing) and a sagittal infinite-step bound that limits unstable growth. We further re-plan within the step by back-propagating the measured instantaneous DCM to update the initial DCM, improving robustness to model mismatch and external disturbances. We evaluate the approach in simulation on Digit on randomized stepping-stone fields, including external pushes. The planner generates terrain-aware, dynamically consistent footstep sequences with adaptive timing and millisecond-level solve times.

</details>


### [394] [Unifying Sidewinding and Rolling: A Wave-Based Framework for Self-Righting in Elongated Limbless and Multi-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07417)
*Hangjun Liu,Jiarui Geng,Jinxuan Ding,Gengzhi He,Xiyuan Wang,Melisa Arukgoda,Joe DiGennaro,George Ubertalli,Grigoriy Blekherman,Baxi Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过生物力学与机器人物理实验，探讨了蜈蚣状机器人在翻倒后自主复位的有效策略，揭示了腿长、腿数等形态参数对复位能力的影响，并发现了过长肢体导致复位困难的临界阈值，为狭小空间作业机器人设计提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜈蚣状机器人虽擅长进入狭窄空间，但在攀爬障碍时易倾覆，可靠的自主复位能力对其野外应用至关重要，但当前对此类长条形多足系统的复位策略理解不足。

Method: 结合比较生物力学与机器人物理方法，研究两种典型蜈蚣（短腿的Scolopendra subspinipes和长腿的家屋蜈蚣Scutigera coleoptrata）的复位行为，并据此构建可调腿长的仿生机器人，系统测试不同形态下复位策略的有效性。

Result: 短腿蜈蚣能可靠地通过空中和地面辅助完成复位，而长腿家屋蜈蚣主要依赖空中翻转，在接触地面时难以产生有效复位力矩；实验发现增加腿长需调整控制策略以避免中部关节扭矩集中，并识别出一个关键腿长阈值，超过后复位变得困难。

Conclusion: 形态与复位策略之间存在强耦合关系，研究提出了适用于细长多足机器人的复位设计原则，并为未来在复杂环境中运行的蜈蚣式机器人提供了优化方向。

Abstract: Centipede-like robots offer unique locomotion advantages due to their small cross-sectional area for accessing confined spaces, and their redundant legs enhance robustness in cluttered environments such as search-and-rescue and pipe inspection. However, elongated robots are particularly vulnerable to tipping over when climbing large obstacles, making reliable self-righting essential for field deployment. Self-righting strategies for elongate, multi-legged systems remain poorly understood. In this study, we conduct a comparative biomechanics and robophysical investigation to address three key questions: (1) What self-righting strategies are effective for elongate, many-legged systems? (2) How should these strategies depend on morphological parameters such as leg length and leg number? (3) Is there a morphological limit beyond which reliable self-righting becomes infeasible? We compare two biological exemplars: Scolopendra subspinipes (short legs) and Scutigera coleoptrata (house centipedes with long legs). Scolopendra subspinipes reliably self-rights both during aerial phases and through ground-assisted self-righting, whereas house centipedes rely predominantly on aerial reorientation and struggle to generate effective self-righting torques during ground contact. Motivated by these observations, we construct a parameterized space of bio-inspired self-righting strategies and develop an elongate robot with adjustable leg lengths. Systematic experiments reveal that increasing leg length necessitates a shift in control strategy to prevent torque over-concentration in mid-body actuators, and we identify a critical limb-length threshold above which robust self-righting becomes challenging. These results establish morphology-strategy coupling principles for self-righting in elongate robots and provide design guidelines for centipede-like systems operating in uncertain terrain.

</details>


### [395] [Cable-driven Continuum Robotics: Proprioception via Proximal-integrated Force Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07426)
*Gang Zhang,Junyan Yan,Jibiao Chen,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于近端集成力感知的新型本体感知方法，用于解决微型连续体机器人的三维接触力感知难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型连续体机器人在结构微型化、非线性力学和传感器集成方面面临挑战，难以实现三维接触力感知。

Method: 结合仿生设计原理与非线性建模，利用腱-关节协同感知机制，融合缆索张力与六轴力/扭矩传感器进行多模态感知，并将复杂关系建模为优化问题。

Result: 实现了对三维接触力及接触点位置的感知，同时完成机器人形状估计，实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为开发更安全、智能的连续体机器人奠定了基础，有助于其在复杂临床环境中更广泛应用。

Abstract: Micro-scale continuum robots face significant limitations in achieving three-dimensional contact force perception, primarily due to structural miniaturization, nonlinear mechanical, and sensor integration. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces a novel proprioception method for cable-driven continuum robots based on proximal-integrated force sensing (i.e., cable tension and six-axis force/torque (F/T) sensor), inspired by the tendon-joint collaborative sensing mechanism of the finger. By integrating biomechanically inspired design principles with nonlinear modeling, the proposed method addresses the challenge of force perception (including the three-dimensional contact force and the location of the contact point) and shape estimation in micro-scale continuum robots. First, a quasi-bionic mapping between human tissues/organs and robot components is established, enabling the transfer of the integrated sensing strategy of tendons, joints, and neural feedback to the robotic system. Second, a multimodal perception strategy is developed based on the structural constraints inherent to continuum robots. The complex relationships among mechanical and material nonlinearities, robot motion states, and contact forces are formulated as an optimization problem to reduce the perception complexity. Finally, experimental validation demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed method. This work lays the foundation for developing safer and smarter continuum robots, enabling broader clinical adoption in complex environments.

</details>


### [396] [LITHE: Bridging Best-Effort Python and Real-Time C++ for Hot-Swapping Robotic Control Laws on Commodity Linux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07442)
*He Kai Lim,Tyler R. Clites*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LITHE是一种轻量级软件架构，将机器人控制的高层（Python）与底层（C++实时控制器）整合到单一硬件上，通过CPU隔离、无锁进程间通信和多线程动态链接，实现1kHz实时控制环路的动态逻辑更新，支持高层AI在线演化底层控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层控制架构中，高层难以修改底层固定的控制逻辑，限制了机器人在不同任务和环境中的自适应能力；现有方案依赖复杂中间件或专用硬件，且难以兼顾实时性。

Method: 提出LITHE架构，在树莓派4B上使用isolcpus实现CPU隔离，采用无锁IPC和流水线执行保证低抖动；引入多线程动态链接技术，使Python层能动态更新C++实时控制逻辑，保持1kHz控制频率。

Result: 系统最坏执行时间小于100微秒，最大释放抖动低于4微秒，在重负载下仍保持稳定；验证了大语言模型可在线进行系统辨识并动态更新实时控制器。

Conclusion: LITHE打破了传统实时控制系统中“不可变编译代码”的瓶颈，实现了高层AI与底层实时控制之间的安全、连续融合，为具身智能的实时演化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Modern robotic systems rely on hierarchical control, where a high-level "Brain" (Python) directs a lower-level "Spine" (C++ real-time controller). Despite its necessity, this hierarchy makes it difficult for the Brain to completely rewrite the Spine's immutable control logic, consequently inhibiting fundamental adaptation for different tasks and environments. Conventional approaches require complex middleware, proprietary hardware, or sacrifice real-time performance. We present LITHE (Linux Isolated Threading for Hierarchical Execution), a lightweight software architecture that collapses the robot control hierarchy onto a commodity single-board computer (Raspberry Pi 4B with pi3hat), while maintaining safe frequency decoupling between the Brain and Spine. LITHE integrates strict CPU isolation (isolcpus), lock-free inter-process communication (IPC), and pipelined execution to meet high-frequency deadlines with minimal jitter. By adding multi-threaded dynamic linking, LITHE enables a Python-based Brain to dynamically evolve the logic of a 1kHz C++ Spine without interruption. We validate "functional real-time" system performance with worst-case execution time (WCET) < 100 $μ$s and maximum release jitter (MRJ) < 4 $μ$s under heavy load. We demonstrate a novel application where a large language model (LLM) supervisor performs online system identification to evolve a real-time controller on-the-fly, without interrupting the 1 kHz control loop. In essence, LITHE eliminates the "immutable compiled code" bottleneck for best-effort Brains to synthesize and inject completely new control laws into the real-time Spine. This bridges a critical gap between high-level AI and low-level real-time control to unlock continuous real-time evolution of embodied intelligence in safe, human-in-the-loop systems.

</details>


### [397] [GSAT: Geometric Traversability Estimation using Self-supervised Learning with Anomaly Detection for Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07480)
*Dongjin Cho,Miryeong Park,Juhui Lee,Geonmo Yang,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GSAT的新方法，用于自主导航中的可通行性估计，通过在潜在空间中构建正类超球体进行异常检测，无需额外原型即可分类可通行区域，并结合异常分类与可通行性预测的联合学习，提升了机器人经验利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可通行性估计方法依赖人工定义的语义或几何阈值，主观性强且可靠性低；自监督方法虽能从经验中学习，但存在仅正样本学习的根本问题。

Method: 提出GSAT方法，在潜在空间中构造正类超球体，通过异常检测实现可通行区域分类，无需负样本或未标记样本；同时采用异常分类与可通行性预测的联合学习机制。

Result: 通过消融实验、异构真实机器人平台验证以及仿真环境中的自主导航演示，全面评估了该框架的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: GSAT能够有效解决正样本-无标签学习中的挑战，提升可通行性估计的可靠性与机器人经验利用效率，适用于多种实际机器人系统和仿真环境。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation requires reliable estimation of environmental traversability. Traditional methods have relied on semantic or geometry-based approaches with human-defined thresholds, but these methods often yield unreliable predictions due to the inherent subjectivity of human supervision. While self-supervised approaches enable robots to learn from their own experience, they still face a fundamental challenge: the positive-only learning problem. To address these limitations, recent studies have employed Positive-Unlabeled (PU) learning, where the core challenge is identifying positive samples without explicit negative supervision. In this work, we propose GSAT, which addresses these limitations by constructing a positive hypersphere in latent space to classify traversable regions through anomaly detection without requiring additional prototypes (e.g., unlabeled or negative). Furthermore, our approach employs joint learning of anomaly classification and traversability prediction to more efficiently utilize robot experience. We comprehensively evaluate the proposed framework through ablation studies, validation on heterogeneous real-world robotic platforms, and autonomous navigation demonstrations in simulation environments.

</details>


### [398] [HSC-VLA: Hierarchical Scene-Clearing for Robust Bimanual Manipulation in Dense Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07484)
*Zhen Liu,Xinyu Ning,Zhe Hu,XinXin Xie,Yitong Liu,Zhongzhu Pu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HSC-VLA是一种分层视觉-语言-动作框架，通过分离高层语义推理与低层运动控制，在高密度杂乱环境中显著提升长视野操作任务的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视觉-语言-动作模型在高密度杂乱环境中因视觉干扰导致注意力分散和语义错位，难以有效执行复杂长视野操作任务。

Method: 提出HSC-VLA框架，包含高层‘大脑’用于任务分解并生成任务相关场景掩码，以及低层‘小脑’（基于扩散策略）利用掩码过滤后的视觉输入和本体感知进行双臂操作。

Result: 在高度杂乱的超市货架环境中，HSC-VLA整体任务成功率高达86.7%，远超最佳基线模型（34.3%）；在分类整理和补货等长视野任务中分别达到72%和66%的成功率。

Conclusion: HSC-VLA通过显式的场景清理抽象和分层架构，有效缓解了视觉干扰问题，实现了更强的鲁棒性、故障恢复能力及在复杂环境下的高性能操作。

Abstract: Modern Vision--Language--Action models often suffer from critical instruction-following failures in high-density manipulation environments, where task-irrelevant visual clutter dilutes attention, corrupts grounding, and substantially degrades performance in complex long-horizon scenarios. To overcome the representation bottleneck of monolithic end-to-end architectures, we propose HSC-VLA, a hierarchical framework that decouples high-level visual-semantic reasoning from low-level, high-frequency sensorimotor execution through an explicit scene-clearing abstraction. HSC-VLA employs a high-level Brain to decompose long-horizon tasks and to generate task-specific scene masks that preserve task-relevant geometry while suppressing distractors. The filtered observations are then passed to a low-level Cerebellum, a diffusion-based policy that performs bimanual manipulation using only mask-filtered vision and proprioception. Extensive experiments in densely cluttered supermarket shelves demonstrate that HSC-VLA achieves 86.7\% aggregate success under high-density clutter, surpassing the best monolithic baseline ($π_0$-Full FT at 34.3\%) by 52.4\%. HSC-VLA also exhibits strong long-horizon performance, reaching 72\% on clutter sorting and 66\% on restocking, demonstrating strong robustness and effective failure recovery in complex cluttered manipulation.

</details>


### [399] [InterReal: A Unified Physics-Based Imitation Framework for Learning Human-Object Interaction Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07516)
*Dayang Liang,Yuhang Lin,Xinzhe Liu,Jiyuan Shi,Yunlong Liu,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InterReal是一个基于物理的模仿学习框架，用于实现人形机器人在真实世界中的人-物交互控制，能够有效提升交互技能的学习与部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架大多关注非交互式的全身控制，限制了人形机器人在实际场景中的应用，缺乏对精细交互能力的支持。

Method: 提出InterReal框架，包含带手-物接触约束的HOI运动数据增强方法，以及由关键跟踪误差引导的自动奖励学习机制，以提升策略稳定性和学习效率。

Result: 在箱体抓取和推动任务中，InterReal在跟踪精度和任务成功率上均优于现有基线方法，并在真实机器人Unitree G1上成功验证。

Conclusion: InterReal能有效实现人形机器人在真实环境中的复杂交互控制，具备良好的鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: Interaction is one of the core abilities of humanoid robots. However, most existing frameworks focus on non-interactive whole-body control, which limits their practical applicability. In this work, we develop InterReal, a unified physics-based imitation learning framework for Real-world human-object Interaction (HOI) control. InterReal enables humanoid robots to track HOI reference motions, facilitating the learning of fine-grained interactive skills and their deployment in real-world settings. Within this framework, we first introduce a HOI motion data augmentation scheme with hand-object contact constraints, and utilize the augmented motions to improve policy stability under object perturbations. Second, we propose an automatic reward learner to address the challenge of large-scale reward shaping. A meta-policy guided by critical tracking error metrics explores and allocates reward signals to the low-level reinforcement learning objective, which enables more effective learning of interactive policies. Experiments on HOI tasks of box-picking and box-pushing demonstrate that InterReal achieves the best tracking accuracy and the highest task success rate compared to recent baselines. Furthermore, we validate the framework on the real-world robot Unitree G1, which demonstrates its practical effectiveness and robustness beyond simulation.

</details>


### [400] [ICLR: In-Context Imitation Learning with Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07530)
*Toan Nguyen,Weiduo Yuan,Songlin Wei,Hui Li,Daniel Seita,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ICLR的新型框架，通过在上下文模仿学习中引入视觉推理轨迹来增强机器人对新任务的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅依赖状态-动作轨迹，缺乏对任务意图的显式表征，导致在复杂或模糊任务中表现受限。

Method: ICLR框架利用结构化的视觉推理轨迹（在图像空间中表示预期的未来机器人轨迹）增强示范提示，并在一个统一的自回归Transformer模型中联合学习生成推理轨迹和低级动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的操作任务中，ICLR均表现出比其他方法更高的成功率，并在未见过的任务和新物体配置上展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将具身化的视觉推理融入上下文模仿学习是提升机器人系统鲁棒性和泛化能力的有效途径。

Abstract: In-context imitation learning enables robots to adapt to new tasks from a small number of demonstrations without additional training. However, existing approaches typically condition only on state-action trajectories and lack explicit representations of task intent. This limitation hinders performance in complex and ambiguous task settings where the same actions may be consistent with different objectives. To address this, we present In-Context Imitation Learning with Visual Reasoning (ICLR), a novel framework that augments demonstration prompts with structured visual reasoning traces representing anticipated future robot trajectories in image space. ICLR also jointly learns to generate reasoning traces and low-level actions within a unified autoregressive transformer, enabling the model to mimic not only action prediction but also the reasoning process that leads to those actions. We extensively evaluate ICLR in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks and demonstrate consistent improvements in success rates and generalization to unseen tasks and novel object configurations compared to other in-context imitation learning methods. These results suggest that incorporating embodied visual reasoning represents a promising direction for enhancing the robustness and generalization of robotic in-context learning systems.

</details>


### [401] [FeasibleCap: Real-Time Embodiment Constraint Guidance for In-the-Wild Robot Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07580)
*Zi Yin,Fanhong Li,Yun Gui,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FeasibleCap是一种用于“手握夹爪”数据采集的新系统，可在无机器人硬件的情况下实时提供可执行性反馈，通过视觉叠加和触觉提示帮助演示者在采集过程中纠正动作，从而提高轨迹可用性并降低采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在“手握夹爪”数据采集中，采集的轨迹是否可在目标机器人上执行通常需后续验证，失败演示显著增加了有效成本。现有方法依赖AR/VR设备、机器人硬件或学习模型，缺乏适用于该范式的实时反馈机制。

Method: FeasibleCap在每帧检查目标机器人模型的可达性、关节速率限制和碰撞情况，并通过设备上的视觉叠加和触觉反馈实现实时闭环指导，无需学习模型、头戴设备或机器人硬件。

Result: 在抓取放置和抛掷任务中，FeasibleCap提高了回攧行为的成功率，减少了不可行帧的比例，尤其在抛掷任务中提升最明显；仿真实验表明，采集时施加可执行约束不影响跨平台迁移能力。

Conclusion: FeasibleCap成功将实时可执行性反馈引入无机器人参与的“手握夹爪”数据采集，提升了数据质量与效率，且不牺牲跨机器人平台的泛化能力。

Abstract: Gripper-in-hand data collection decouples demonstration acquisition from robot hardware, but whether a trajectory is executable on the target robot remains unknown until a separate replay-and-validate stage. Failed demonstrations therefore inflate the effective cost per usable trajectory through repeated collection, diagnosis, and validation. Existing collection-time feedback systems mitigate this issue but rely on head-worn AR/VR displays, robot-in-the-loop hardware, or learned dynamics models; real-time executability feedback has not yet been integrated into the gripper-in-hand data collection paradigm. We present \textbf{FeasibleCap}, a gripper-in-hand data collection system that brings real-time executability guidance into robot-free capture. At each frame, FeasibleCap checks reachability, joint-rate limits, and collisions against a target robot model and closes the loop through on-device visual overlays and haptic cues, allowing demonstrators to correct motions during collection without learned models, headsets, or robot hardware. On pick-and-place and tossing tasks, FeasibleCap improves replay success and reduces the fraction of infeasible frames, with the largest gains on tossing. Simulation experiments further indicate that enforcing executability constraints during collection does not sacrifice cross-embodiment transfer across robot platforms. Hardware designs and software are available at https://github.com/aod321/FeasibleCap.

</details>


### [402] [GeoLoco: Leveraging 3D Geometric Priors from Visual Foundation Model for Robust RGB-Only Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07624)
*Yufei Liu,Xieyuanli Chen,Hainan Pan,Chenghao Shi,Yanjie Chen,Kaihong Huang,Zhiwen Zeng,Huimin Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GeoLoco的纯RGB驱动的人形机器人运动框架，利用视觉基础模型的几何先验，实现从单目图像到三维潜在表征的转化，通过动态注意力机制和双头辅助学习策略，实现了仿真到现实的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的感知型人形机器人运动方法依赖深度传感器，忽略了丰富的语义和外观信息，且端到端强化学习在从2D像素学习时存在样本效率低和仿真到现实迁移困难的问题。因此，需要一种仅基于RGB的方法来实现高效、鲁棒的运动控制。

Method: 提出GeoLoco框架，将单目RGB图像视为高维3D潜在表示，利用冻结的尺度感知视觉基础模型（VFM）提取几何先验；设计本体感知查询的多头交叉注意力机制，根据机器人实时步态阶段动态关注任务关键的拓扑特征；引入双头辅助学习方案，显式正则化潜在空间以对齐真实地形几何结构。

Result: 在纯仿真环境中训练的GeoLoco模型成功迁移到Unitree G1人形机器人上，能够在复杂地形中稳健行走，实现了零样本的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: GeoLoco证明了仅使用RGB输入即可实现鲁棒的人形机器人运动控制，通过融合视觉基础模型的几何先验与显式正则化策略，打破了传统深度感知与端到端学习的局限，推动了具身智能中低层控制与高层推理的融合。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm of perceptive humanoid locomotion relies heavily on active depth sensors. However, this depth-centric approach fundamentally discards the rich semantic and dense appearance cues of the visual world, severing low-level control from the high-level reasoning essential for general embodied intelligence. While monocular RGB offers a ubiquitous, information-dense alternative, end-to-end reinforcement learning from raw 2D pixels suffers from extreme sample inefficiency and catastrophic sim-to-real collapse due to the inherent loss of geometric scale. To break this deadlock, we propose GeoLoco, a purely RGB-driven locomotion framework that conceptualizes monocular images as high-dimensional 3D latent representations by harnessing the powerful geometric priors of a frozen, scale-aware Visual Foundation Model (VFM). Rather than naive feature concatenation, we design a proprioceptive-query multi-head cross-attention mechanism that dynamically attends to task-critical topological features conditioned on the robot's real-time gait phase. Crucially, to prevent the policy from overfitting to superficial textures, we introduce a dual-head auxiliary learning scheme. This explicit regularization forces the high-dimensional latent space to strictly align with the physical terrain geometry, ensuring robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. Trained exclusively in simulation, GeoLoco achieves robust zero-shot transfer to the Unitree G1 humanoid and successfully negotiates challenging terrains.

</details>


### [403] [Exoskeleton Control through Learning to Reduce Biological Joint Moments in Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07629)
*Zihang You,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的外骨骼辅助控制框架，并通过开源步态数据集验证了仿真训练控制器在真实数据上的扭矩预测一致性，展示了良好的髋关节扭矩时序匹配，但在高负荷条件下（如高速、陡坡）尤其是膝关节仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对外骨骼辅助扭矩预测器的定量验证及其对人体关节功率注入影响的系统评估，限制了仿真到现实迁移的应用。

Method: 构建了一个基于物理的强化学习框架，在仿真中训练多层感知机（MLP）控制器，将双侧髋膝运动学短时历史映射为归一化辅助扭矩；并通过公开步态数据集进行推理，与生物力学关节力矩对比验证。

Result: 仿真训练的控制器在不同速度和坡度下能保持任务强度趋势；髋关节扭矩预测与真实数据高度一致（相关系数最高达0.98），但膝关节在高速和陡坡条件下差异增大；功率分析显示延迟调优可提升正向功率输出。

Conclusion: 研究建立了仿真训练外骨骼控制器的定量验证框架，证实了扭矩层面良好的仿真到数据一致性，同时指出了在功率匹配和复杂工况下的迁移挑战。

Abstract: Data-driven joint-moment predictors offer a scalable alternative to laboratory-based inverse-dynamics pipelines for biomechanics estimation and exoskeleton control. Meanwhile, physics-based reinforcement learning (RL) enables simulation-trained controllers to learn dynamics-aware assistance strategies without extensive human experimentation. However, quantitative verification of simulation-trained exoskeleton torque predictors, and their impact on human joint power injection, remains limited. This paper presents (1) an RL framework to learn exoskeleton assistance policies that reduce biological joint moments, and (2) a validation pipeline that verifies the trained control networks using an open-source gait dataset through inference and comparison with biological joint moments. Simulation-trained multilayer perceptron (MLP) controllers are developed for level-ground and ramp walking, mapping short-horizon histories of bilateral hip and knee kinematics to normalized assistance torques. Results show that predicted assistance preserves task-intensity trends across speeds and inclines. Agreement is particularly strong at the hip, with cross-correlation coefficients reaching 0.94 at 1.8 m/s and 0.98 during 5° decline walking, demonstrating near-matched temporal structure. Discrepancies increase at higher speeds and steeper inclines, especially at the knee, and are more pronounced in joint power comparisons. Delay tuning biases assistance toward greater positive power injection; modest timing shifts increase positive power and improve agreement in specific gait intervals. Together, these results establish a quantitative validation framework for simulation-trained exoskeleton controllers, demonstrate strong sim-to-data consistency at the torque level, and highlight both the promise and the remaining challenges for sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [404] [PanoDP: Learning Collision-Free Navigation with Panoramic Depth and Differentiable Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07644)
*Hao Zhong,Pei Chi,Jiang Zhao,Shenghai Yuan,Xuyang Gao,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PanoDP是一种无需通信的学习框架，结合四视图全景深度感知与基于可微物理的训练信号，实现杂乱环境中的自主避障导航，表现出优于单视图和其他基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测且存在静态结构和动态障碍物的复杂环境中，实现安全、无碰撞的自主导航具有挑战性，需要更稳定的训练方法和对360度环境信息的有效利用。

Method: 提出PanoDP框架，采用轻量级CNN编码四视图全景深度信息，并在策略优化中引入密集的可微物理碰撞和运动可行性损失项，提升训练稳定性；通过环形到中心的基准测试和AirSim等外部仿真器进行系统性评估。

Result: PanoDP在多种设置下相比单视图和非物理引导基线显著提高了无碰撞率和任务完成率，消融实验验证了模型有效利用了360度视角信息，并展现出良好的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: PanoDP通过融合全景感知与可微物理训练信号，实现了更安全、高效的多智能体导航，证明了其在复杂动态环境中具备优越的性能和应用潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous collision-free navigation in cluttered environments requires safe decision-making under partial observability with both static structure and dynamic obstacles. We present \textbf{PanoDP}, a communication-free learning framework that combines four-view panoramic depth perception with differentiable-physics-based training signals. PanoDP encodes panoramic depth using a lightweight CNN and optimizes policies with dense differentiable collision and motion-feasibility terms, improving training stability beyond sparse terminal collisions. We evaluate PanoDP on a controlled ring-to-center benchmark with systematic sweeps over agent count, obstacle density/layout, and dynamic behaviors, and further test out-of-distribution generalization in an external simulator (e.g., AirSim).
