Across settings, PanoDP increases collision-free and completion rates over single-view and non-physics-guided baselines under matched training budgets, and ablations (view masking, rotation augmentation) confirm the policy leverages 360-degree information. Code will be open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [405] [TempoFit: Plug-and-Play Layer-Wise Temporal KV Memory for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07647)
*Jun Sun,Boyu Yang,Jiahao Zhang,Ning Ma,Chencheng Wu,Siqing Zhang,Yiou Huang,Qiufeng Wang,Shan Liang,Yaran Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoFit是一种无需训练的时间扩展方法，通过重用预训练视觉语言动作（VLA）模型中的prefix K/V状态实现历史信息记忆，在不增加新参数或视觉标记的情况下提升长视野操作任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练视觉语言动作策略在处理非马尔可夫性的长视野任务时表现脆弱，主要由于推理过程缺乏记忆；现有历史注入方法存在扩展性差、延迟高或需重新训练等问题。

Method: 提出TempoFit，利用模型原生的prefix attention K/V作为内容可寻址的运行时状态，跨时间步重用并在选定中间层使用FIFO机制存储层间K/V；引入无参数的K-to-K检索与帧间隙时间偏置（FGTB），并通过归一化保持的残差加载注入上下文以避免分布偏移。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG上，TempoFit将强基线模型的平均成功率提升了高达+4.0%，同时保持接近实时的推理延迟，并在CALVIN和真实机器人长视野任务中展现出良好迁移能力。

Conclusion: TempoFit为冻结的VLA模型提供了一种高效、即插即用的时间扩展方案，能够在不破坏原有单帧推理结构的前提下有效增强其对长视野任务的适应能力。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have achieved strong single-step manipulation, but their inference remains largely memoryless, which is brittle in non-Markovian long-horizon settings with occlusion, state aliasing, and subtle post-action changes. Prior approaches inject history either by stacking frames, which scales visual tokens and latency while adding near-duplicate pixels, or by learning additional temporal interfaces that require (re-)training and may break the original single-frame inference graph. We present TempoFit, a training-free temporal retrofit that upgrades frozen VLAs through state-level memory. Our key insight is that prefix attention K/V already form a model-native, content-addressable runtime state; reusing them across timesteps introduces history without new tokens or trainable modules. TempoFit stores layer-wise FIFO prefix K/V at selected intermediate layers, performs parameter-free K-to-K retrieval with Frame-Gap Temporal Bias (FGTB), a fixed recency bias inspired by positional biases in NLP, to keep decisions present-dominant, and injects the retrieved context via pre-attention residual loading with norm-preserving rescaling to avoid distribution shift under frozen weights. On LIBERO-LONG, TempoFit improves strong pretrained backbones by up to +4.0% average success rate while maintaining near-real-time latency, and it transfers consistently to CALVIN and real-robot long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [406] [AtomicVLA: Unlocking the Potential of Atomic Skill Learning in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07648)
*Likui Zhang,Tao Tang,Zhihao Zhan,Xiuwei Chen,Zisheng Chen,Jianhua Han,Jiangtong Zhu,Pei Xu,Hang Xu,Hefeng Wu,Liang Lin,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AtomicVLA的统一规划与执行框架，通过构建可扩展的原子技能库和动态专家组合，有效解决了现有视觉-语言-动作模型在长视野、多步骤机器人任务中的可扩展性和持续学习能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理现实世界中长视野、多步骤且需要持续技能获取的机器人任务时存在可扩展性差的问题，主要由于其采用单一动作解码器和聚合数据训练的方式。

Method: 提出AtomicVLA框架，结合任务级规划、原子技能抽象和细粒度动作生成；通过技能引导的混合专家（SG-MoE）构建可扩展的原子技能库，并设计灵活的路由编码器，实现新技能的自动专家分配与持续学习。

Result: 在模拟实验中，AtomicVLA在LIBERO上超越π₀达2.4%，在LIBERO-LONG上达10%，在CALVIN上的平均任务长度优于π₀和π₀.5分别为0.22和0.25；在真实场景的长视野和持续学习任务中分别超过基线18.3%和21%。

Conclusion: 原子技能抽象与动态专家组合能显著提升VLA模型在长视野和终身学习任务中的性能与可扩展性，为未来机器人持续自主学习提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in Visual-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising potential for robotic manipulation tasks. However, real-world robotic tasks often involve long-horizon, multi-step problem-solving and require generalization for continual skill acquisition, extending beyond single actions or skills. These challenges present significant barriers for existing VLA models, which use monolithic action decoders trained on aggregated data, resulting in poor scalability. To address these challenges, we propose AtomicVLA, a unified planning-and-execution framework that jointly generates task-level plans, atomic skill abstractions, and fine-grained actions. AtomicVLA constructs a scalable atomic skill library through a Skill-Guided Mixture-of-Experts (SG-MoE), where each expert specializes in mastering generic yet precise atomic skills. Furthermore, we introduce a flexible routing encoder that automatically assigns dedicated atomic experts to new skills, enabling continual learning. We validate our approach through extensive experiments. In simulation, AtomicVLA outperforms $π_{0}$ by 2.4\% on LIBERO, 10\% on LIBERO-LONG, and outperforms $π_{0}$ and $π_{0.5}$ by 0.22 and 0.25 in average task length on CALVIN. Additionally, our AtomicVLA consistently surpasses baselines by 18.3\% and 21\% in real-world long-horizon tasks and continual learning. These results highlight the effectiveness of atomic skill abstraction and dynamic expert composition for long-horizon and lifelong robotic tasks. The project page is \href{https://zhanglk9.github.io/atomicvla-web/}{here}.

</details>


### [407] [Multi-Agent Off-World Exploration for Sparse Evidence Discovery via Gaussian Belief Mapping and Dual-Domain Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07650)
*Zhuoran Qiao,Tianxin Hu,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯信念映射和双域覆盖的多机器人信息路径规划框架，用于月球等外星环境中的稀疏证据发现，能够在存在通信限制和地形风险的情况下实现高效、安全的协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对地外多机器人探索中存在的目标稀疏、感知受限、地形危险和通信受限等问题，现有方法依赖不完整或有偏见的兴趣区域定义，且对地形风险处理不足，难以避免不可恢复的危险区域。

Method: 该方法采用基于高斯过程的兴趣和风险信念建模，结合轨迹意图表示，支持多智能体间的协调序贯决策；通过双域覆盖机制，在优先搜索兴趣区域的同时保留对外部区域的有限探索，增强对兴趣区域偏差的鲁棒性，并在路径规划中显式考虑风险以平衡信息增益与安全性。

Result: 在模拟月球环境中进行实验，结果表明所提方法在不同预算和通信范围下均优于基于采样的和贪心基线方法，特别是在风险感知场景下具有更低的最终不确定性，并在通信受限时保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了多机器人在外星复杂环境下的协同探索效率与安全性，具备应对兴趣区域偏差、通信受限和地形风险的能力，适用于未来的地外科学探测任务。

Abstract: Off-world multi-robot exploration is challenged by sparse targets, limited sensing, hazardous terrain, and restricted communication. Many scientifically valuable clues are visually ambiguous and often require close-range observations, making efficient and safe informative path planning essential. Existing methods often rely on predefined areas of interest (AOIs), which may be incomplete or biased, and typically handle terrain risk only through soft penalties, which are insufficient for avoiding non-recoverable regions. To address these issues, we propose a multi-agent informative path planning framework for sparse evidence discovery based on Gaussian belief mapping and dual-domain coverage. The method maintains Gaussian-process-based interest and risk beliefs and combines them with trajectory-intent representations to support coordinated sequential decision-making among multiple agents. It further prioritizes search inside the AOI while preserving limited exploration outside it, thereby improving robustness to AOI bias. In addition, the risk-aware design helps agents balance information gain and operational safety in hazardous environments. Experimental results in simulated lunar environments show that the proposed method consistently outperforms sampling-based and greedy baselines under different budgets and communication ranges. In particular, it achieves lower final uncertainty in risk-aware settings and remains robust under limited communication, demonstrating its effectiveness for cooperative off-world robotic exploration.

</details>


### [408] [DAISS: Phase-Aware Imitation Learning for Dual-Arm Robotic Ultrasound-Guided Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07663)
*Feng Li,Pei Liu,Shiting Wang,Ning Wang,Zhongliang Jiang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于超声引导下介入手术的双臂机器人系统DAISS，通过相位感知的模仿学习实现精确的双手协调操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导下的穿刺手术需要医生同时操控超声探头和穿刺针，对手眼协调和双手配合要求极高，自动化此类不对称任务具有挑战性。

Method: 开发了基于NDI的遥操作平台DAISS，采集高质量双臂演示数据，并设计了一种轻量、数据高效的模仿策略，结合相位感知架构与动态掩码损失，融合实时超声与视觉信息生成协调运动。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能从少量演示中学习个性化专家策略，实现精准的双臂协同控制。

Conclusion: 相位感知的模仿学习在双臂医疗机器人中具有提升操作精度和降低认知负荷的潜力。

Abstract: Imitation learning has shown strong potential for automating complex robotic manipulation. In medical robotics, ultrasound-guided needle insertion demands precise bimanual coordination, as clinicians must simultaneously manipulate an ultrasound probe to maintain an optimal acoustic view while steering an interventional needle. Automating this asymmetric workflow -- and reliably transferring expert strategies to robots -- remains highly challenging. In this paper, we present the Dual-Arm Interventional Surgical System (DAISS), a teleoperated platform that collects high-fidelity dual-arm demonstrations and learns a phase-aware imitation policy for ultrasound-guided interventions. To avoid constraining the operator's natural behavior, DAISS uses a flexible NDI-based leader interface for teleoperating two coordinated follower arms. To support robust execution under real-time ultrasound feedback, we develop a lightweight, data-efficient imitation policy. Specifically, the policy incorporates a phase-aware architecture and a dynamic mask loss tailored to asymmetric bimanual control. Conditioned on a planned trajectory, the network fuses real-time ultrasound with external visual observations to generate smooth, coordinated dual-arm motions. Experimental results show that DAISS can learn personalized expert strategies from limited demonstrations. Overall, these findings highlight the promise of phase-aware imitation-learning-driven dual-arm robots for improving precision and reducing cognitive workload in image-guided interventions.

</details>


### [409] [Low-Cost Teleoperation Extension for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07672)
*Danil Belov,Artem Erkhov,Yaroslav Savotin,Tatiana Podladchikova,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于开源框架的移动双手机器人的遥操作方法，利用智能手机、主从手臂和脚踏板实现直观的全身控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有遥操作系统成本高、设备专用性强的问题，降低移动双手机器人遥操作的门槛。

Method: 结合智能手机的IMU进行头部跟踪（用于相机控制）、主从手臂进行双臂操作、脚踏板实现免手操控底座，构建模块化系统，并与XLeRobot框架集成。

Result: 用户实验表明，相比键盘控制，该方法在任务执行效率和认知负荷方面均有改善。

Conclusion: 所提出的开源遥操作框架可有效利用消费级硬件实现高性能遥操作，具有良好的通用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Teleoperation of mobile bimanual manipulators requires simultaneous control of high-dimensional systems, often necessitating expensive specialized equipment. We present an open-source teleoperation framework that enables intuitive whole body control using readily available commodity hardware. Our system combines smartphone-based head tracking for camera control, leader arms for bilateral manipulation, and foot pedals for hands-free base navigation. Using a standard smartphone with IMU and display, we eliminate the need for costly VR helmets while maintaining immersive visual feedback. The modular architecture integrates seamlessly with the XLeRobot framework, but can be easily adapted to other types of mobile manipulators. We validate our approach through user studies that demonstrate improved task performance and reduced cognitive load compared to keyboard-based control.

</details>


### [410] [RoboPCA: Pose-centered Affordance Learning from Human Demonstrations for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07691)
*Zhanqi Xiao,Ruiping Wang,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以姿态为中心的机器人操作对象交互预测框架RoboPCA，通过结合指令联合预测合适的接触区域和姿态，并利用Human2Afford数据生成流程从人类演示中自动提取三维空间信息与姿态中心的标注，提升了在图像数据集、仿真和真实机器人上的性能及跨任务、跨类别的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注预测接触区域，而将姿态估计分离处理，导致预测区域与实际操作姿态不一致，易引发任务失败。因此需要一种联合预测接触区域与姿态的方法以提高机器人操作的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RoboPCA框架，结合RGB-D编码器融合几何与外观特征，并引入掩码增强特征以突出任务相关区域；设计Human2Afford数据构建流程，从人类示范中自动恢复3D场景信息，通过追踪接触区对象点和分析手-物交互模式，建立从3D手部网格到机器人末端执行器姿态的映射，实现姿态中心的标注生成。

Result: RoboPCA在多个图像数据集、仿真环境和真实机器人平台上均优于基线方法，展现出更强的任务和类别泛化能力，能更准确地预测符合任务需求的接触姿态。

Conclusion: 通过以姿态为中心的联合预测与高质量数据构建流程，RoboPCA有效解决了传统方法中区域与姿态不一致的问题，显著提升了机器人在复杂操作任务中的性能与适应性。

Abstract: Understanding spatial affordances -- comprising the contact regions of object interaction and the corresponding contact poses -- is essential for robots to effectively manipulate objects and accomplish diverse tasks. However, existing spatial affordance prediction methods mainly focus on locating the contact regions while delegating the pose to independent pose estimation approaches, which can lead to task failures due to inconsistencies between predicted contact regions and candidate poses. In this work, we propose RoboPCA, a pose-centered affordance prediction framework that jointly predicts task-appropriate contact regions and poses conditioned on instructions. To enable scalable data collection for pose-centered affordance learning, we devise Human2Afford, a data curation pipeline that automatically recovers scene-level 3D information and infers pose-centered affordance annotations from human demonstrations. With Human2Afford, scene depth and the interaction object's mask are extracted to provide 3D context and object localization, while pose-centered affordance annotations are obtained by tracking object points within the contact region and analyzing hand-object interaction patterns to establish a mapping from the 3D hand mesh to the robot end-effector orientation. By integrating geometry-appearance cues through an RGB-D encoder and incorporating mask-enhanced features to emphasize task-relevant object regions into the diffusion-based framework, RoboPCA outperforms baseline methods on image datasets, simulation, and real robots, and exhibits strong generalization across tasks and categories.

</details>


### [411] [C$^2$-Explorer: Contiguity-Driven Task Allocation with Connectivity-Aware Task Representation for Decentralized Multi-UAV Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07699)
*Xinlu Yan,Mingjie Zhang,Yuhao Fang,Yanke Sun,Jun Ma,Youmin Gong,Boyu Zhou,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为C²-Explorer的去中心化框架，用于解决多无人机在通信受限条件下探索效率低下的问题。该方法通过构建连通性图分解未知区域，并引入基于邻域惩罚的连续性驱动任务分配机制，显著减少了探索时间和路径长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务表示和分配方法在通信开销、环境适应性和时空连续性方面存在不足，导致多无人机探索效率低下，尤其是在通信受限的情况下。

Method: 提出C²-Explorer框架：首先构建连通性图以将不连通的未知区域分解为独立任务单元；然后设计一种基于图的邻域惩罚机制，鼓励时间上连续的任务分配，避免非相邻区域的频繁切换。整个系统采用去中心化架构，适用于通信受限环境。

Result: 大量仿真结果表明，C²-Explorer相比现有最先进方法平均减少43.1%的探索时间与33.3%的路径长度；真实飞行实验验证了系统的可行性。

Conclusion: C²-Explorer通过改进任务表示与连续性感知的分配策略，有效提升了多无人机在通信受限下的协同探索效率，具备良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Efficient multi-UAV exploration under limited communication is severely bottlenecked by inadequate task representation and allocation. Previous task representations either impose heavy communication requirements for coordination or lack the flexibility to handle complex environments, often leading to inefficient traversal. Furthermore, short-horizon allocation strategies neglect spatiotemporal contiguity, causing non-contiguous assignments and frequent cross-region detours. To address this, we propose C$^2$-Explorer, a decentralized framework that constructs a connectivity graph to decompose disconnected unknown components into independent task units. We then introduce a contiguity-driven allocation formulation with a graph-based neighborhood penalty to discourage non-adjacent assignments, promoting more contiguous task sequences over time. Extensive simulation experiments show that C$^2$-Explorer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines, reducing average exploration time by 43.1\% and path length by 33.3\%. Real-world flights further demonstrate the system's feasibility. The code will be released at https://github.com/Robotics-STAR-Lab/C2-Explorer

</details>


### [412] [AeroPlace-Flow: Language-Grounded Object Placement for Aerial Manipulators via Visual Foresight and Object Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07744)
*Sarthak Mishra,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Naveen Nair,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroPlace-Flow 是一种无需训练的框架，通过语言引导实现无人机精确物体放置，结合视觉预测、3D几何推理与物体流，无需预定义姿态或专门训练即可生成可执行的放置目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中操作多依赖预定义坐标，难以适应用户通过自然语言表达的实际放置需求，缺乏对精确放置和语言交互的支持。

Method: 利用RGB-D观测和自然语言指令，通过图像编辑模型合成目标图像，结合深度对齐与以对象为中心的3D推理，推断出避障的物体流，指导无人机完成放置动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中验证了方法的有效性，硬件上的平均成功率达到75%，支持多样化场景下的语言条件物体放置。

Conclusion: AeroPlace-Flow 实现了无需训练的语言引导空中物体放置，通过融合视觉想象与显式3D推理，提升了人机交互自然性和任务通用性。

Abstract: Precise object placement remains underexplored in aerial manipulation, where most systems rely on predefined target coordinates and focus primarily on grasping and control. Specifying exact placement poses, however, is cumbersome in real-world settings, where users naturally communicate goals through language. In this work, we present AeroPlace-Flow, a training-free framework for language-grounded aerial object placement that unifies visual foresight with explicit 3D geometric reasoning and object flow. Given RGB-D observations of the object and the placement scene, along with a natural language instruction, AeroPlace-Flow first synthesizes a task-complete goal image using image editing models. The imagined configuration is then grounded into metric 3D space through depth alignment and object-centric reasoning, enabling the inference of a collision-aware object flow that transports the grasped object to a language and contact-consistent placement configuration. The resulting motion is executed via standard trajectory tracking for an aerial manipulator. AeroPlace-Flow produces executable placement targets without requiring predefined poses or task-specific training. We validate our approach through extensive simulation and real-world experiments, demonstrating reliable language-conditioned placement across diverse aerial scenarios with an average success rate of 75% on hardware.

</details>


### [413] [Residual Control for Fast Recovery from Dynamics Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07775)
*Nethmi Jayasinghe,Diana Gontero,Francesco Migliarba,Spencer T. Brown,Vinod K. Sangwan,Mark C. Hersam,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种稳定性对齐的残差控制架构，通过冻结主策略并利用受约束的残差通道实现机器人在遭遇未观测动力学变化时的快速恢复，显著减少恢复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中机器人常遭遇未观测的动力学变化，导致学习策略性能下降，现有方法难以在不重新训练的情况下快速恢复任务性能。

Method: 设计了一种稳定性对齐残差控制架构，包含一个固定的名义策略和一个受控的残差控制通道；引入稳定性对齐门（SAG）来调节修正量，确保方向一致性、幅值限制、性能触发和自适应增益调节。

Result: 在Go1四足、Cassie双足、H1人形和Scout轮式平台上测试，相比冻结策略基线平均恢复时间分别缩短87%、48%、30%和20%，且保持稳态性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能在无需重训练的前提下有效应对多种动力学扰动，实现快速恢复并保持闭环系统稳定性，适用于实际部署中的持续运行场景。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in real-world environments inevitably encounter unobserved dynamics shifts during continuous execution, including changes in actuation, mass distribution, or contact conditions. When such shifts occur mid-episode, even locally stabilizing learned policies can experience substantial transient performance degradation. While input-to-state stability guarantees bounded state deviation, it does not ensure rapid restoration of task-level performance. We address inference-time recovery under frozen policy parameters by casting adaptation as constrained disturbance shaping around a nominal stabilizing controller. We propose a stability-aligned residual control architecture in which a reinforcement learning policy trained under nominal dynamics remains fixed at deployment, and adaptation occurs exclusively through a bounded additive residual channel. A Stability Alignment Gate (SAG) regulates corrective authority through magnitude constraints, directional coherence with the nominal action, performance-conditioned activation, and adaptive gain modulation. These mechanisms preserve the nominal closed-loop structure while enabling rapid compensation for unobserved dynamics shifts without retraining or privileged disturbance information. Across mid-episode perturbations including actuator degradation, mass variation, and contact changes, the proposed method consistently reduces recovery time relative to frozen and online-adaptation baselines while maintaining near-nominal steady-state performance. Recovery time is reduced by \textbf{87\%} on the Go1 quadruped, \textbf{48\%} on the Cassie biped, \textbf{30\%} on the H1 humanoid, and \textbf{20\%} on the Scout wheeled platform on average across evaluated conditions relative to a frozen SAC policy.

</details>


### [414] [A Robust Antenna Provides Tactile Feedback in a Multi-legged Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07795)
*Zhaochen J. Xu,Juntao He,Delfin Aydan,Malaika Taylor,Tianyu Wang,Jianfeng Lin,Wesley Dyer,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多足细长机器人、受生物蜈蚣触角启发的触觉天线，通过机械调谐的柔性结构实现对周围环境几何形状的实时感知，结合简单的形变到碰撞状态映射，提升了机器人在复杂狭窄环境中的自主导航与避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受限空间中，由于机身与环境接触广泛且地形流变特性快速变化，现有机器人难以实现鲁棒导航，因此需要一种不依赖全局信息或实时视觉的局部感知解决方案。

Method: 设计具有梯度刚度（基部硬、尖端软）的触觉天线，通过物理实验建立天线曲率与接触力之间的关系，并将其简化为从形变到离散碰撞状态的映射，进而集成到基于碰撞状态选择运动策略的控制器中。

Result: 在富含障碍物和狭窄环境中的实验证明，该触觉反馈系统能有效实现可靠转向并帮助机器人摆脱近卡死状态，且无需全局环境信息或实时视觉。

Conclusion: 机械调优的触觉附属装置可通过简化感知方式显著增强细长多足机器人在受限空间中的自主性，为复杂地形下的无视觉导航提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-legged elongate robots hold promise for maneuvering through complex environments. Prior work has demonstrated that reliable locomotion can be achieved using open-loop body undulation and foot placement on rugose terrain. However, robust navigation through confined spaces remains challenging when body-environment contact is extensive and terrain rheology varies rapidly. To address this challenge, we develop a pair of tactile antennae for multi-legged robots that enable real-time sensing of surrounding geometry, modeling the morphology and function of biological centipede antennae. Each antenna features gradient compliance, with a stiff base and soft tip, allowing repeated deformation and elastic recovery. Robophysical experiments reveal a relationship between continuous antenna curvature and contact force, leading to a simplified mapping from antenna deformation to inferred discrete collision states. We incorporate this mapping into a controller that selects among a set of locomotor maneuvers based on the inferred collision state. Experiments in obstacle-rich and confined environments demonstrate that tactile feedback enables reliable steering and allows the robot to recover from near-stuck conditions without requiring global environmental information or real-time vision. These results highlight how mechanically tuned tactile appendages can simplify sensing and enhance autonomy in elongate multi-legged robots operating in constrained spaces.

</details>


### [415] [Inverse Resistive Force Theory (I-RFT): Learning granular properties through robot-terrain physical interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07796)
*Shipeng Liu,Feng Xue,Yifeng Zhang,Tarunika Ponnusamy,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为I-RFT的物理信息机器学习框架，结合颗粒阻力理论与高斯过程，可从任意步态轨迹下的接触力中推断松散地形的力学性质，实现高效、自适应的机器人地形感知与运动优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖特定脚部轨迹（如垂直压入或水平剪切）估计颗粒地形性质，限制了在自然运动中的应用，需发展适用于任意步态的通用估计方法。

Method: 将颗粒阻力理论（RFT）模型嵌入高斯过程回归中，构建物理一致的逆向模型I-RFT，利用本体感知的接触力数据，在任意步态和足形下反演地形力学参数。

Result: 实验表明I-RFT能在多种步态和足形下准确估计地形性质，且对地形阻力应力场的不确定性量化可用于优化足部设计与步态规划，提升信息采集效率。

Conclusion: I-RFT为复杂颗粒环境的高效建模提供了新基础，推动了可主动调整步态以适应未知地形的自主探索机器人发展。

Abstract: For robots to navigate safely and efficiently on soft, granular terrains, it is crucial to gather information about the terrain's mechanical properties, which directly affect locomotion performance. Recent research has developed robotic legs that can accurately sense ground reaction forces during locomotion. However, existing tests of granular property estimation often rely on specific foot trajectories, such as vertical penetration or horizontal shear, limiting their applicability during natural locomotion.
  To address this limitation, we introduce a physics-informed machine learning framework, Inverse Resistive Force Theory (I-RFT), which integrates the Granular Resistive Force Theory model with Gaussian Processes to infer terrain properties from proprioceptively measured contact forces under arbitrary gait trajectories. By embedding the granular force model within the learning process, I-RFT preserves physical consistency while enabling generalization across diverse motion primitives.
  Experimental results demonstrate that I-RFT accurately estimates terrain properties across multiple gait trajectories and toe shapes. Moreover, we show that the quantified uncertainty over the terrain resistance stress map could enable robots to optimize foot design and gait trajectories for efficient information gathering. This approach establishes a new foundation for data-efficient characterization of complex granular environments and opens new avenues for locomotion strategies that actively adapt gait for autonomous terrain exploration.

</details>


### [416] [Toward Global Intent Inference for Human Motion by Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07797)
*Sarmad Mehrdad,Maxime Sabbah,Vincent Bonnet,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究利用MO-IRL算法和七维候选成本项，探索是否可用单一、统一的成本函数来解释和预测人类伸手运动，结果表明时间变化的权重显著提高了轨迹重建精度，支持存在统一最优原则的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否存在一个通用的优化准则可以统一解释不同个体和姿势下的人类伸手动作，而非依赖于特定个体或姿势的优化标准。

Method: 采用Minimal Observation Inverse Reinforcement Learning (MO-IRL) 算法，结合七维候选成本项，估计平面伸手任务中的时变成本权重，并评估三种泛化水平下的表现。

Result: 时间变化的权重使轨迹重建误差平均降低27%（RMSE），推断出的成本显示关节加速度调节起主导作用，力矩变化平滑性贡献较小。

Conclusion: 一个不依赖于个体和姿势的统一时变成本函数能高精度预测人类伸手轨迹，支持此类运动受统一最优原则支配的假设。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether a single, unified cost function can explain and predict human reaching movements, in contrast with existing approaches that rely on subject- or posture-specific optimization criteria. Using the Minimal Observation Inverse Reinforcement Learning (MO-IRL) algorithm, together with a seven-dimensional set of candidate cost terms, we efficiently estimate time-varying cost weights for a standard planar reaching task. MO-IRL provides orders-of-magnitude faster convergence than bilevel formulations, while using only a fraction of the available data, enabling the practical exploration of time-varying cost structures. Three levels of generality are evaluated: Subject-Dependent Posture-Dependent, Subject-Dependent Posture-Independent, and Subject-Independent Posture-Independent. Across all cases, time-varying weights substantially improve trajectory reconstruction, yielding an average 27% reduction in RMSE compared to the baseline. The inferred costs consistently highlight a dominant role for joint-acceleration regulation, complemented by smaller contributions from torque-change smoothness. Overall, a single subject- and posture-agnostic time-varying cost function is shown to predict human reaching trajectories with high accuracy, supporting the existence of a unified optimality principle governing this class of movements.

</details>


### [417] [Preference-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning for Space-Time Efficient Online 3D Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07800)
*Nikita Sarawgi,Omey M. Manyar,Fan Wang,Thinh H. Nguyen,Daniel Seita,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STEP的时空高效装箱方法，通过在每次操作中权衡空间利用增益与操作时间，实现了在不降低装箱密度的情况下减少44%的操作时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人装箱系统需要在紧凑放置和快速执行之间进行权衡，选择不同物品或调整姿态虽可提升空间利用率但会增加时间开销，缺乏对这一时空权衡的显式建模。

Method: 提出一种基于选择的动作评估机制，使用偏好条件化的Transformer结构强化学习策略（STEP），在每一步评估多个候选动作，综合考虑预期装箱收益与估计操作时间，实现时间感知的决策，并支持在不同候选集大小上的泛化及与标准放置模块集成。

Result: 相比现有方法，在保持装箱密度的同时，操作时间减少了44%。

Conclusion: STEP通过显式建模空间与时间的权衡，显著提升了仓储自动化中装箱任务的效率，为实际应用提供了更优的时间-空间平衡方案。

Abstract: Robotic bin packing is widely deployed in warehouse automation, with current systems achieving robust performance through heuristic and learning-based strategies. These systems must balance compact placement with rapid execution, where selecting alternative items or reorienting them can improve space utilization but introduce additional time. We propose a selection-based formulation that explicitly reasons over this trade-off: at each step, the robot evaluates multiple candidate actions, weighing expected packing benefit against estimated operational time. This enables time-aware strategies that selectively accept increased operational time when it yields meaningful spatial improvements. Our method, STEP (Space-Time Efficient Packing), uses a preference-conditioned, Transformer-based reinforcement learning policy, and allows generalization across candidate set sizes and integration with standard placement modules. It achieves a 44% reduction in operational time without compromising packing density. Additional material is available at https://step-packing.github.io.

</details>


### [418] [Uncertainty Mitigation and Intent Inference: A Dual-Mode Human-Machine Joint Planning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07822)
*Zeyu Fang,Yuxin Lin,Cheng Liu,Beomyeol Yu,Zeyuan Yang,Rongqian Chen,Taeyoung Lee,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于开放世界环境中人机协作的统一联合规划系统，能够处理任务相关知识缺失和人类隐含意图两种不确定性，通过双向对话与实时意图感知显著降低交互成本与任务执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将人类视为被动监督者，限制了机器人作为主动、类人队友的能力；为此，本文旨在构建能主动建模人类行为、识别知识盲区并通过沟通消除不确定性的协作系统。

Method: 系统包含两个模块：一是基于LLM辅助主动询问机制与假设增强A^*搜索的不确定性缓解联合规划模块，结合动态规划计算最优查询策略；二是利用空间与方向线索维护人类意图概率信念的实时意图感知协作模块，实现无需显式通信的动态协调任务选择。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和真实无人机部署中验证了系统有效性，结果显示不确定性缓解规划使交互成本降低51.9%，意图感知协作使任务执行时间减少25.4%（相比基线）。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合规划系统有效提升了人机协作效率，通过主动沟通与意图推断实现了更自然、高效的开放环境人机协同。

Abstract: Effective human-robot collaboration in open-world environments requires joint planning under uncertain conditions. However, existing approaches often treat humans as passive supervisors, preventing autonomous agents from becoming human-like teammates that can actively model teammate behaviors, reason about knowledge gaps, query, and elicit responses through communication to resolve uncertainties. To address these limitations, we propose a unified human-robot joint planning system designed to tackle dual sources of uncertainty: task-relevant knowledge gaps and latent human intent. Our system operates in two complementary modes. First, an uncertainty-mitigation joint planning module enables two-way conversations to resolve semantic ambiguity and object uncertainty. It utilizes an LLM-assisted active elicitation mechanism and a hypothesis-augmented A^* search, subsequently computing an optimal querying policy via dynamic programming to minimize interaction and verification costs. Second, a real-time intent-aware collaboration module maintains a probabilistic belief over the human's latent task intent via spatial and directional cues, enabling dynamic, coordination-aware task selection for agents without explicit communication. We validate the proposed system in both Gazebo simulations and real-world UAV deployments integrated with a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based 3D semantic perception pipeline. Experimental results demonstrate that the system significantly cuts the interaction cost by 51.9% in uncertainty-mitigation planning and reduces the task execution time by 25.4% in intent-aware cooperation compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [419] [Reasoning Knowledge-Gap in Drone Planning via LLM-based Active Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07824)
*Zeyu Fang,Beomyeol Yu,Cheng Liu,Zeyuan Yang,Rongqian Chen,Yuxin Lin,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最小信息查询的神经符号树（MINT）框架，实现人类与AI在无人机任务中的高效协作，通过主动征询关键信息减少人机交互频率并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机人机协同依赖控制权切换应对环境不确定性，对非专业操作员而言效率低且认知负担重，亟需更高效的协作范式。

Method: 提出MINT框架，将知识缺口结构化为可查询的神经符号树，利用大语言模型生成最优二元问题，结合视觉-语言模型感知、语音接口和底层控制模块，在仿真与真实环境中实现主动信息征询。

Result: 在高保真仿真和真实部署中验证了方法有效性，相比穷举提问基线显著提高了复杂搜救任务的成功率，并大幅减少了人工干预次数。

Conclusion: MINT框架通过从控制接管向主动信息征询的范式转变，实现了更高效、低认知负荷的人机协同无人机操作。

Abstract: Human-AI joint planning in Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) typically relies on control handover when facing environmental uncertainties, which is often inefficient and cognitively demanding for non-expert operators. To address this, we propose a novel framework that shifts the collaboration paradigm from control takeover to active information elicitation. We introduce the Minimal Information Neuro-Symbolic Tree (MINT), a reasoning mechanism that explicitly structures knowledge gaps regarding obstacles and goals into a queryable format. By leveraging large language models, our system formulates optimal binary queries to resolve specific ambiguities with minimal human interaction. We demonstrate the efficacy of this approach through a comprehensive workflow integrating a vision-language model for perception, voice interfaces, and a low-level UAV control module in both high-fidelity NVIDIA Isaac simulations and real-world deployments. Experimental results show that our method achieves a significant improvement in the success rate for complex search-and-rescue tasks while significantly reducing the frequency of human interaction compared to exhaustive querying baselines.

</details>


### [420] [Physics-infused Learning for Aerial Manipulator in Winds and Near-Wall Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07826)
*Yiming Zhang,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理建模与学习方法的统一控制框架，用于提升无人机在复杂气动环境下的空中操作性能，通过仿真验证了其在近墙飞行和接触任务中具有更优的扰动估计与轨迹跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中操纵系统多基于简化推力模型，难以准确描述真实环境中风扰与结构附近气流变化，而高精度CFD方法无法实时应用，学习模型又泛化能力不足，因此需要一种兼具准确性与实时性的气动力建模与补偿方法。

Method: 将基于物理的桨叶元素模型与基于学习的残余力估计器相结合，并引入转子转速分配策略进行扰动补偿；桨叶模型提供风扰下的前馈气动力估计，学习模块预测未建模的残余力，通过在线自适应机制联合更新残余力预测与转速分配。

Result: 在模拟的近墙有风环境下进行了自由飞行与壁面接触跟踪任务测试，结果表明该方法相比传统方法具有更优的扰动估计精度和轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合建模与自适应控制框架能有效提升无人机在复杂气动环境中的操作鲁棒性，为实际空中操纵任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation (AM) expands UAV capabilities beyond passive observation to contact-based operations at high altitudes and in otherwise inaccessible environments. Although recent advances show promise, most AM systems are developed in controlled settings that overlook key aerodynamic effects. Simplified thrust models are often insufficient to capture the nonlinear wind disturbances and proximity-induced flow variations present in real-world environments near infrastructure, while high-fidelity CFD methods remain impractical for real-time use. Learning-based models are computationally efficient at inference, but often struggle to generalize to unseen condition. This paper combines both approaches by integrating a physics-based blade-element model with a learning-based residual force estimator, along with a rotor-speed allocation strategy for disturbance compensation, resulting in a unified control framework. The blade-element model computes per-rotor aerodynamic forces under wind and provides a refined feedforward disturbance estimate. A learning-based estimator then predicts the residual forces not captured by the model, enabling compensation for unmodeled aerodynamic effects. An online adaptation mechanism further updates the residual-force prediction and rotor-speed allocation jointly to reduce the mismatch between desired and realized thrust. We evaluate this framework in both free-flight and wall-contact tracking tasks in a simulated near-wall wind environment. Results demonstrate improved disturbance estimation and trajectory-tracking accuracy over conventional approaches, enabling robust wall-contact execution under challenging aerodynamic conditions.

</details>


### [421] [Relating Reinforcement Learning to Dynamic Programming-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07844)
*Filip V. Georgiev,Kalle G. Timperi,Başak Sakçak,Steven M. LaValle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了最优规划与强化学习之间的联系，提出了去随机化的强化学习方法，并分析了成本最小化与奖励最大化等条件下的等价性，提倡优化真实成本而非依赖任意参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合最优规划与强化学习之间的差距，探索两者在动态规划基础上的共性与差异。

Method: 提出并分析了一种去随机化的强化学习方法，通过数学分析探讨成本最小化与奖励最大化、目标终止与无限时域学习之间的等价条件，并比较价值迭代、Dijkstra算法与强化学习的性能。

Result: 揭示了成本最小化与奖励最大化在特定条件下的等价性，明确了折扣因子可能导致目标失败的条件，并在确定性和随机性场景下比较了不同算法的性能。

Conclusion: 应定义并优化真实成本函数，而不是依赖人为设定的参数（如学习率、贪婪因子或折扣因子）来引导决策过程，从而实现更优的规划与学习统一框架。

Abstract: This paper bridges some of the gap between optimal planning and reinforcement learning (RL), both of which share roots in dynamic programming applied to sequential decision making or optimal control. Whereas planning typically favors deterministic models, goal termination, and cost minimization, RL tends to favor stochastic models, infinite-horizon discounting, and reward maximization in addition to learning-related parameters such as the learning rate and greediness factor. A derandomized version of RL is developed, analyzed, and implemented to yield performance comparisons with value iteration and Dijkstra's algorithm using simple planning models. Next, mathematical analysis shows: 1) conditions under which cost minimization and reward maximization are equivalent, 2) conditions for equivalence of single-shot goal termination and infinite-horizon episodic learning, and 3) conditions under which discounting causes goal achievement to fail. The paper then advocates for defining and optimizing truecost, rather than inserting arbitrary parameters to guide operations. Performance studies are then extended to the stochastic case, using planning-oriented criteria and comparing value iteration to RL with learning rates and greediness factors.

</details>


### [422] [Viewpoint-Agnostic Grasp Pipeline using VLM and Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07866)
*Dilermando Almeida,Juliano Negri,Guilherme Lazzarini,Thiago H. Segreto,Ranulfo Bezerra,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的语言引导抓取方法，能够在杂乱环境中实现高成功率的鲁棒抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于遮挡导致观测不完整和深度估计不可靠，移动腿式机械臂在复杂环境中的稳定抓取仍具挑战性。

Method: 结合开放词汇检测与可提示实例分割定位目标，利用RGB-D生成以物体为中心的点云，并通过反投影深度补偿和两阶段点云补全提升几何可靠性；随后生成并筛选6自由度抓取候选，采用考虑可达性、接近可行性和避障的安全启发策略选择可执行的抓取动作。

Result: 在四足机器人平台上进行实验，于两种杂乱桌面场景中对比视图依赖基线方法，本方法整体成功率达90%（9/10），远高于基线的30%（3/10）。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著提升了在遮挡和部分观测条件下的抓取鲁棒性，实现了语言指令到安全抓取执行的有效闭环。

Abstract: Robust grasping in cluttered, unstructured environments remains challenging for mobile legged manipulators due to occlusions that lead to partial observations, unreliable depth estimates, and the need for collision-free, execution-feasible approaches. In this paper we present an end-to-end pipeline for language-guided grasping that bridges open-vocabulary target selection to safe grasp execution on a real robot. Given a natural-language command, the system grounds the target in RGB using open-vocabulary detection and promptable instance segmentation, extracts an object-centric point cloud from RGB-D, and improves geometric reliability under occlusion via back-projected depth compensation and two-stage point cloud completion. We then generate and collision-filter 6-DoF grasp candidates and select an executable grasp using safety-oriented heuristics that account for reachability, approach feasibility, and clearance. We evaluate the method on a quadruped robot with an arm in two cluttered tabletop scenarios, using paired trials against a view-dependent baseline. The proposed approach achieves a 90% overall success rate (9/10) against 30% (3/10) for the baseline, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to occlusions and partial observations in clutter.

</details>


### [423] [Choose What to Observe: Task-Aware Semantic-Geometric Representations for Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07875)
*Haoran Ding,Liang Ma,Yaxun Yang,Wen Yang,Tianyu Liu,Anqing Duan,Xiaodan Liang,Dezhen Song,Ivan Laptev,Yoshihiko Nakamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种任务感知的观察接口，通过将视觉输入标准化为共享表示来提升视觉运动策略在分布外外观变化下的鲁棒性，无需修改或微调策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于演示学习的视觉运动策略在原始RGB观测中对无关视觉因素过拟合的问题，导致在背景变化、物体重着色等外观变化下表现脆弱。

Method: 给定RGB图像和任务相关实体的开放词汇描述，使用SAM3分割目标物体和机器人/夹爪；构建L0观察：在恒定背景下用预定义语义颜色重绘分割实体；对于需要更强几何线索的任务，通过深度引导覆盖将Depth Anything 3的单目深度注入分割区域，生成统一的语义-几何观察L1，保持标准3通道类图像输入。

Result: 在RoboMimic（Lift）、ManiSkill YCB杂乱抓取、四个RLBench任务（受控外观变化）和两个真实世界Franka任务（ReachX和CloseCabinet）上进行评估，跨基准和策略主干（Flow Matching Policy和SmolVLA），该接口在保持分布内性能的同时显著提升了分布外视觉变化下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的任务感知观察接口有效增强了视觉运动政策对分布外外观变化的适应能力，且不牺牲原有任务性能，具有广泛适用性和实用性。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies learned from demonstrations often overfit to nuisance visual factors in raw RGB observations, resulting in brittle behavior under appearance shifts such as background changes and object recoloring. We propose a task-aware observation interface that canonicalizes visual input into a shared representation, improving robustness to out-of-distribution (OOD) appearance changes without modifying or fine-tuning the policy. Given an RGB image and an open-vocabulary specification of task-relevant entities, we use SAM3 to segment the target object and robot/gripper. We construct an L0 observation by repainting segmented entities with predefined semantic colors on a constant background. For tasks requiring stronger geometric cues, we further inject monocular depth from Depth Anything 3 into the segmented regions via depth-guided overwrite, yielding a unified semantic--geometric observation (L1) that remains a standard 3-channel, image-like input. We evaluate on RoboMimic (Lift), ManiSkill YCB grasping under clutter, four RLBench tasks under controlled appearance shifts, and two real-world Franka tasks (ReachX and CloseCabinet). Across benchmarks and policy backbones (Flow Matching Policy and SmolVLA), our interface preserves in-distribution performance while substantially improving robustness under OOD visual shifts.

</details>


### [424] [RoboRouter: Training-Free Policy Routing for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07892)
*Yiteng Chen,Zhe Cao,Hongjia Ren,Chenjie Yang,Wenbo Li,Shiyi Wang,Yemin Wang,Li Zhang,Yanming Shao,Zhenjun Zhao,Huiping Zhuang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RoboRouter，一种无需训练的智能策略路由框架，通过整合异构策略的优势，在仿真和真实世界任务中显著提升了机器人操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略在特定任务上表现良好但泛化能力有限，本文旨在通过智能路由机制结合多种策略的优势，提升整体性能和可扩展性。

Method: RoboRouter构建语义任务表示，检索历史相似任务记录，预测最优策略选择，并利用结构化反馈优化后续决策；新策略加入仅需轻量级评估，无需重新训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，RoboRouter比单一策略平均成功率分别提升超过3%和13%，同时保持高效执行。

Conclusion: 智能路由异构现成策略是一种实用且可扩展的方法，有助于构建更强大的机器人系统。

Abstract: Research on robotic manipulation has developed a diverse set of policy paradigms, including vision-language-action (VLA) models, vision-action (VA) policies, and code-based compositional approaches. Concrete policies typically attain high success rates on specific task distributions but lim-ited generalization beyond it. Rather than proposing an other monolithic policy, we propose to leverage the complementary strengths of existing approaches through intelligent policy routing. We introduce RoboRouter, a training-free framework that maintains a pool of heterogeneous policies and learns to select the best-performing policy for each task through accumulated execution experience. Given a new task, RoboRouter constructs a semantic task representation, retrieves historical records of similar tasks, predicts the optimal policy choice without requiring trial-and-error, and incorporates structured feedback to refine subsequent routing decisions. Integrating a new policy into the system requires only lightweight evaluation and incurs no training overhead. Across simulation benchmark and real-world evaluations, RoboRouter consistently outperforms than in-dividual policies, improving average success rate by more than 3% in simulation and over 13% in real-world settings, while preserving execution efficiency. Our results demonstrate that intelligent routing across heterogeneous, off-the-shelf policies provides a practical and scalable pathway toward building more capable robotic systems.

</details>


### [425] [NaviDriveVLM: Decoupling High-Level Reasoning and Motion Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07901)
*Ximeng Tao,Pardis Taghavi,Dimitar Filev,Reza Langari,Gaurav Pandey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NaviDriveVLM提出了一种解耦框架，通过分离视觉语言模型中的推理与动作生成，实现高效且精确的端到端自动驾驶运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动驾驶系统在高层推理与运动规划之间存在权衡：大模型语义理解强但难以适应精确控制，小模型易于微调但推理能力弱。

Method: 提出NaviDriveVLM，采用解耦设计，由大规模的Navigator负责高阶语义推理，轻量级可训练Driver负责动作生成，并引入明确的中间表征以支持下游规划。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上的实验表明，NaviDriveVLM在端到端运动规划任务中优于现有的大型VLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 该解耦架构有效平衡了推理能力与训练效率，提升了自动驾驶系统的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for end-to-end autonomous driving (AD) by jointly modeling visual observations, driving context, and language-based reasoning. However, existing VLM-based systems face a trade-off between high-level reasoning and motion planning: large models offer strong semantic understanding but are costly to adapt for precise control, whereas small VLM models can be fine-tuned efficiently but often exhibit weaker reasoning. We propose NaviDriveVLM, a decoupled framework that separates reasoning from action generation using a large-scale Navigator and a lightweight trainable Driver. This design preserves reasoning ability, reduces training cost, and provides an explicit interpretable intermediate representation for downstream planning. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that NaviDriveVLM outperforms large VLM baselines in end-to-end motion planning.

</details>


### [426] [Omnidirectional Humanoid Locomotion on Stairs via Unsafe Stepping Penalty and Sparse LiDAR Elevation Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07928)
*Yuzhi Jiang,Yujun Liang,Junhao Li,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单阶段训练框架，结合密集的不安全步态惩罚和滚动点云建图系统，实现人形机器人在楼梯等复杂地形上的安全全向行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖前向深度相机导致感知盲区，且稀疏的触地后惩罚机制导致学习效率低、策略次优，难以实现稳定安全的楼梯行走。

Method: 引入密集不安全步态惩罚以提供连续反馈；构建具有时空置信度衰减和自保护区域机制的滚动点云建图系统；采用Edge-Guided Asymmetric U-Net（EGAU）优化由稀疏LiDAR返回引起的楼梯重建失真。

Result: 仿真中接近100%的安全落脚率，真实机器人部署中保持高安全率，并成功完成复杂户外地形的长距离连续行走测试。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了高效的学习、稳定的建图与可靠的足部放置，在仿真和现实环境中均展现出优异的安全性与长期稳定性，具备良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots, characterized by numerous degrees of freedom and a high center of gravity, are inherently unstable. Safe omnidirectional locomotion on stairs requires both omnidirectional terrain perception and reliable foothold selection. Existing methods often rely on forward-facing depth cameras, which create blind zones that restrict omnidirectional mobility. Furthermore, sparse post-contact unsafe stepping penalties lead to low learning efficiency and suboptimal strategies. To realize safe stair-traversal gaits, this paper introduces a single-stage training framework incorporating a dense unsafe stepping penalty that provides continuous feedback as the foot approaches a hazardous placement. To obtain stable and reliable elevation maps, we build a rolling point-cloud mapping system with spatiotemporal confidence decay and a self-protection zone mechanism, producing temporally consistent local maps. These maps are further refined by an Edge-Guided Asymmetric U-Net (EGAU), which mitigates reconstruction distortion caused by sparse LiDAR returns on stair risers. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that the proposed method achieves a near-100\% safe stepping rate on stair terrains in simulation, while maintaining a remarkably high safe stepping rate in real-world deployments. Furthermore, it completes a continuous long-distance walking test on complex outdoor terrains, demonstrating reliable sim-to-real transfer and long-term stability.

</details>


### [427] [Unified Structural-Hydrodynamic Modeling of Underwater Underactuated Mechanisms and Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07939)
*Chenrui Zhang,Yiyuan Zhang,Yunfei Ye,Junkai Chen,Haozhe Wang,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于轨迹驱动的全局优化框架，用于水下多体系统的统一结构-水动力学建模，能够准确识别内部弹性、阻尼和分布水动力参数，实现了从欠驱动机构到柔性软体机器人系统的高保真模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下欠驱动和软体机器人系统的精确建模面临挑战，因其需要识别高维的内部结构和外部水动力参数。

Method: 受CMA-ES启发，通过仿真与实验运动在轨迹层面的匹配，同时识别耦合的内部弹性、阻尼及分布水动力参数。

Result: 在欠驱动多体机制上验证，末端执行器位置归一化误差低于5%；模型策略迁移到章鱼-inspired软臂及整体机器人时表现出强真实-仿真一致性，无需手动调参。

Conclusion: 所提结构-水动力学建模框架具有良好的可扩展性和跨系统迁移能力，适用于多种水下欠驱动与软体机器人系统。

Abstract: Underwater robots are widely deployed for ocean exploration and manipulation. Underactuated mechanisms are particularly advantageous in aquatic environments, as reducing actuator count lowers the risk of motor leakage while introducing inherent mechanical compliance. However, accurate modeling of underwater underactuated and soft robotic systems remains challenging because it requires identifying a high-dimensional set of internal structural and external hydrodynamic parameters. In this work, we propose a trajectory-driven global optimization framework for unified structural-hydrodynamic modeling of underwater multibody systems. Inspired by the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES), the proposed approach simultaneously identifies coupled internal elastic, damping, and distributed hydrodynamic parameters through trajectory-level matching between simulation and experimental motion. This enables high-fidelity reproduction of both underactuated mechanisms and compliant soft robotic systems in underwater environments. We first validate the framework on a link-by-link underactuated multibody mechanism, demonstrating accurate identification of distributed hydrodynamic coefficients, with a normalized end effector position error below 5% across multiple trajectories, varying initial conditions, and both active-passive and fully passive configurations. The identified modeling strategy is then transferred to a single octopus-inspired soft arm, showing strong real-to-sim consistency without manual retuning. Finally, eight identified arms are assembled into a swimming octopus robot, where the unified parameter set enables realistic whole body behavior without additional parameter calibration. These results demonstrate the scalability and transferability of the proposed structural-hydrodynamic modeling framework across underwater underactuated and soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [428] [VORL-EXPLORE: A Hybrid Learning Planning Approach to Multi-Robot Exploration in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07973)
*Ning Liu,Sen Shen,Zheng Li,Sheng Liu,Dongkun Han,Shangke Lyu,Thomas Braunl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VORL-EXPLORE是一种结合学习与规划的多机器人探索框架，通过引入执行保真度信号，提升密集动态环境下的探索效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层多机器人探索方法将前沿分配与局部导航解耦，导致在密集动态环境中系统脆弱，易出现机器人拥堵、反复重规划和冗余覆盖等问题。

Method: 提出VORL-EXPLORE框架，引入执行保真度作为局部可导航性的共享估计，将其融入基于Voronoi图的任务分配目标，并加入机器人间排斥机制以减少竞争；同时设计风险感知的自适应仲裁机制，结合全局A*引导与基于强化学习的局部反应策略；支持利用近期进展和安全结果生成伪标签，实现保真度模型的在线自监督再校准。

Result: 在随机网格和Gazebo工厂场景中进行大量实验，验证了该方法具有高任务成功率、更短路径、更低覆盖重叠和强碰撞规避能力，并在严重交通场景消融实验中展示了良好的适应性。

Conclusion: VORL-EXPLORE通过耦合任务分配与运动执行，显著提升了多机器人系统在复杂环境中的探索鲁棒性和效率，具备在线适应非稳态障碍的能力。

Abstract: Hierarchical multi-robot exploration commonly decouples frontier allocation from local navigation, which can make the system brittle in dense and dynamic environments. Because the allocator lacks direct awareness of execution difficulty, robots may cluster at bottlenecks, trigger oscillatory replanning, and generate redundant coverage. We propose VORL-EXPLORE, a hybrid learning and planning framework that addresses this limitation through execution fidelity, a shared estimate of local navigability that couples task allocation with motion execution. This fidelity signal is incorporated into a fidelity-coupled Voronoi objective with inter-robot repulsion to reduce contention before it emerges. It also drives a risk-aware adaptive arbitration mechanism between global A* guidance and a reactive reinforcement learning policy, balancing long-range efficiency with safe interaction in confined spaces. The framework further supports online self-supervised recalibration of the fidelity model using pseudo-labels derived from recent progress and safety outcomes, enabling adaptation to non-stationary obstacles without manual risk tuning. We evaluate this capability separately in a dedicated severe-traffic ablation. Extensive experiments in randomized grids and a Gazebo factory scenario show high success rates, shorter path length, lower overlap, and robust collision avoidance. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [429] [Aero-Promptness: Drag-Aware Aerodynamic Manipulability for Propeller-driven Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07998)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑气动阻力和电机扭矩限制的冗余多旋翼控制分配几何框架——Drag-Aware Aerodynamic Manipulability (DAAM)，通过在广义力空间中构建状态依赖的可操作性体积，并利用其对数行列式作为自然屏障函数来优化分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼控制分配方法常忽略气动阻力和电机饱和效应，导致高动态飞行中性能下降；本文旨在建立一个能显式反映这些物理约束的几何化分析框架，以提升冗余配置下的控制鲁棒性与效率。

Method: 在螺旋桨转速空间引入基于剩余对称加速度能力的黎曼度量，并结合非线性推力定律映射到广义力空间，形成状态依赖的可操作性体积；通过优化该体积的对数行列式实现冗余自由度的解析分配，同时保证坐标缩放不变性。

Result: 所提方法能够显式惩罚因气动阻力引起的电机饱和和低转速推力损失；理论证明最优分配在局部构成光滑嵌入流形，但也存在由执行器极限和转速符号切换引发的全局跳跃不连续性。

Conclusion: DAAM为冗余多旋翼系统提供了一个几何上一致且物理意义明确的控制分配框架，能够在复杂动力学条件下实现更安全、高效的飞行控制，具有较强的理论价值与应用潜力。

Abstract: This work introduces the Drag-Aware Aerodynamic Manipulability (DAAM), a geometric framework for control allocation in redundant multirotors. By equipping the propeller spin-rate space with a Riemannian metric based on the remaining symmetric acceleration capacity of each motor, the formulation explicitly accounts for motor torque limits and aerodynamic drag. Mapping this metric through the nonlinear thrust law to the generalized force space yields a state-dependent manipulability volume. The log-determinant of this volume acts as a natural barrier function, strictly penalizing drag-induced saturation and low-spin thrust loss. Optimizing this volume along the allocation fibers provides a redundancy resolution strategy inherently invariant to arbitrary coordinate scaling in the generalized-force space. Analytically, we prove that the resulting optimal allocations locally form smooth embedded manifolds, and we geometrically characterize the global jump discontinuities that inevitably arise from physical actuator limits and spin-rate sign transitions.

</details>


### [430] [Dual-Horizon Hybrid Internal Model for Low-Gravity Quadrupedal Jumping with Hardware-in-the-Loop Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07999)
*Haozhe Xu,Yifei Zhao,Wenhao Feng,Zhipeng Wang,Hongrui Sang,Cheng Cheng,Xiuxian Li,Zhen Yin,Bin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双时间尺度混合内部模型，结合虚拟现实测试平台MATRIX，在模拟月球重力环境下实现了四足机器人仅依靠本体感知的连续跳跃运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对平坦地面上的单次跳跃，缺乏在复杂地形下实现稳定连续跳跃的解决方案，且缺少真实硬件验证。

Method: 设计了双时间尺度混合内部模型，短时分支捕捉快速垂直动态并估计垂直速度，长时分支建模水平运动趋势和质心高度演化；通过MATRIX混合现实测试平台进行硬件在环验证，该平台利用滑轮配重系统抵消重力，并将虚幻引擎中的月球地形实时映射到运动平台和跑步机上。

Result: 在模拟月球重力和陨石坑类地形条件下，实现了四足机器人仅依赖本体感知的稳定连续跳跃。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在延长的空中相位下保持稳定跳跃控制，结合MATRIX平台为未来行星探索机器人提供了可扩展的测试与验证框架。

Abstract: Locomotion under reduced gravity is commonly realized through jumping, yet continuous pronking in lunar gravity remains challenging due to prolonged flight phases and sparse ground contact. The extended aerial duration increases landing impact sensitivity and makes stable attitude regulation over rough planetary terrain difficult. Existing approaches primarily address single jumps on flat surfaces and lack both continuous-terrain solutions and realistic hardware validation. This work presents a Dual-Horizon Hybrid Internal Model for continuous quadrupedal jumping under lunar gravity using proprioceptive sensing only. Two temporal encoders capture complementary time scales: a short-horizon branch models rapid vertical dynamics with explicit vertical velocity estimation, while a long-horizon branch models horizontal motion trends and center-of-mass height evolution across the jump cycle. The fused representation enables stable and continuous jumping under extended aerial phases characteristic of lunar gravity. To provide hardware-in-the-loop validation, we develop the MATRIX (Mixed-reality Adaptive Testbed for Robotic Integrated eXploration) platform, a digital-twin-driven system that offloads gravity through a pulley-counterweight mechanism and maps Unreal Engine lunar terrain to a motion platform and treadmill in real time. Using MATRIX, we demonstrate continuous jumping of a quadruped robot under lunar-gravity emulation across cratered lunar-like terrain.

</details>


### [431] [Vector Field Augmented Differentiable Policy Learning for Vision-Based Drone Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08019)
*Yang Su,Feng Yu,Yu Hu,Xinze Niu,Linzuo Zhang,Fangyu Sun,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DiffRacing的新型可微策略学习框架，结合向量场与可微损失，实现高效、稳定的自主无人机竞速训练，并在仿真和真实环境中验证了其优越的采样效率和飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微物理策略学习方法难以表达如穿越门框等非光滑、非线性的任务目标，限制了其在无人机竞速中的应用。

Method: 提出DiffRacing框架，融合可微损失与向量场增强的梯度信号，并引入可微Delta动作模型以补偿动力学差异，支持高效的仿真到现实迁移。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，DiffRacing展现出更高的样本效率、更快的收敛速度和鲁棒的高速飞行性能，成功完成复杂环境下的无人机竞速任务。

Conclusion: 向量场可作为任务特定的几何先验，有效增强基于梯度的策略学习，在保持高动态飞行的同时实现可靠的避障与精准过门。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing in complex environments requires agile, high-speed flight while maintaining reliable obstacle avoidance. Differentiable-physics-based policy learning has recently demonstrated high sample efficiency and remarkable performance across various tasks, including agile drone flight and quadruped locomotion. However, applying such methods to drone racing remains difficult, as key objective like gate traversal are inherently hard to express as smooth, differentiable losses. To address these challenges, we propose DiffRacing, a novel vector field-augmented differentiable policy learning framework. DiffRacing integrates differentiable losses and vector fields into the training process to provide continuous and stable gradient signals, balancing obstacle avoidance and high-speed gate traversal. In addition, a differentiable Delta Action Model compensates for dynamics mismatch, enabling efficient sim-to-real transfer without explicit system identification. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that DiffRacing achieves superior sample efficiency, faster convergence, and robust flight performance, thereby demonstrating that vector fields can augment traditional gradient-based policy learning with a task-specific geometric prior.

</details>


### [432] [AffordGrasp: Cross-Modal Diffusion for Affordance-Aware Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08021)
*Xiaofei Wu,Yi Zhang,Yumeng Liu,Yuexin Ma,Yujiao Shi,Xuming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AffordGrasp的扩散模型框架，用于生成兼具物理稳定性和语义一致性的高精度人体抓取姿态，通过构建细粒度语言标注管道和双条件扩散机制，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义的抓取生成方法在3D物体与文本指令之间存在较大模态差距，缺乏明确的空间或语义约束，导致生成的抓取姿态物理上不合理或语义上不一致。

Method: 提出了AffordGrasp框架：首先设计一个可扩展的自动标注流程，为手-物交互数据集添加细粒度结构化语言标签；然后采用感知功能的潜在表示结合双条件扩散过程，联合建模物体几何、空间功能和指令语义；并通过分布调整模块增强物理接触一致性和语义对齐。

Result: 在基于HO-3D、OakInk、GRAB和AffordPose构建的四个增强指令基准上评估，AffordGrasp在抓取质量、语义准确性和多样性方面均显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: AffordGrasp能有效桥接3D几何与自然语言指令，生成高质量、符合语义且物理合理的抓取姿态，推动了AR/VR和具身智能中自然人机交互的发展。

Abstract: Generating human grasping poses that accurately reflect both object geometry and user-specified interaction semantics is essential for natural hand-object interactions in AR/VR and embodied AI. However, existing semantic grasping approaches struggle with the large modality gap between 3D object representations and textual instructions, and often lack explicit spatial or semantic constraints, leading to physically invalid or semantically inconsistent grasps. In this work, we present AffordGrasp, a diffusion-based framework that produces physically stable and semantically faithful human grasps with high precision. We first introduce a scalable annotation pipeline that automatically enriches hand-object interaction datasets with fine-grained structured language labels capturing interaction intent. Building upon these annotations, AffordGrasp integrates an affordance-aware latent representation of hand poses with a dual-conditioning diffusion process, enabling the model to jointly reason over object geometry, spatial affordances, and instruction semantics. A distribution adjustment module further enforces physical contact consistency and semantic alignment. We evaluate AffordGrasp across four instruction-augmented benchmarks derived from HO-3D, OakInk, GRAB, and AffordPose, and observe substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods in grasp quality, semantic accuracy, and diversity.

</details>


### [433] [See and Switch: Vision-Based Branching for Interactive Robot-Skill Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08057)
*Petr Vanc,Jan Kristof Behrens,Václav Hlaváč,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为See & Switch的交互式机器人教学与执行框架，利用视觉驱动的切换器实现基于高维图像的技能分支选择和异常检测，支持多种教学模式，并在真实机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的示教编程（PbD）方法难以应对现实世界中的环境变化，尤其是在任务图执行过程中缺乏可靠的感知驱动分支选择机制。

Method: 提出See & Switch框架，将任务表示为可由用户扩展的技能部分图，通过决策状态连接；使用眼在手上的摄像头图像作为输入，训练视觉Switcher进行在线分支选择和分布外情境检测；集成示教、摇杆控制和手势操作，支持现场恢复演示。

Result: 在三个灵巧操作任务中进行了实验验证，对576次真实机器人运行测试显示，分支选择准确率达90.7%，异常检测准确率为87.9%；用户研究（8名参与者）表明该方法对新手用户友好且高效。

Conclusion: See & Switch框架通过视觉引导的分支选择和多模态教学集成，提升了示教编程在复杂动态环境中的适应性和鲁棒性，推动了条件任务图的实际应用。

Abstract: Programming robots by demonstration (PbD) is an intuitive concept, but scaling it to real-world variability remains a challenge for most current teaching frameworks. Conditional task graphs are very expressive and can be defined incrementally, which fits very well with the PbD idea. However, acting using conditional task graphs requires reliable perception-grounded online branch selection. In this paper, we present See & Switch, an interactive teaching-and-execution framework that represents tasks as user-extendable graphs of skill parts connected via decision states (DS), enabling conditional branching during replay. Unlike prior approaches that rely on manual branching or low-dimensional signals (e.g., proprioception), our vision-based Switcher uses eye-in-hand images (high-dimensional) to select among competing successor skill parts and to detect out-of-distribution contexts that require new demonstrations. We integrate kinesthetic teaching, joystick control, and hand gestures via an input-modality-abstraction layer and demonstrate that our proposed method is teaching modality-independent, enabling efficient in-situ recovery demonstrations. The system is validated in experiments on three challenging dexterous manipulation tasks. We evaluate our method under diverse conditions and furthermore conduct user studies with 8 participants. We show that the proposed method reliably performs branch selection and anomaly detection for novice users, achieving 90.7 % and 87.9 % accuracy, respectively, across 576 real-robot rollouts. We provide all code and data required to reproduce our experiments at http://imitrob.ciirc.cvut.cz/publications/seeandswitch.

</details>


### [434] [Adaptive Vision-Based Control of Redundant Robots with Null-Space Interaction for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08089)
*Xiangjie Yan,Chen Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于冗余机器人的自适应视觉控制与交互式零空间控制相结合的新控制方案，以实现更安全、高效的人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升人机协作性能，使协作效果优于单独的机器人或人类操作，并应对未知环境中的突发变化。

Method: 在任务空间中引入自适应基于视觉的控制项，在零空间中引入交互控制项，利用李雅普诺夫方法证明系统稳定性。

Result: 实现了无需预先标定的自主任务执行，同时可在零空间中与人类交互以应对潜在碰撞或临时需求，实验验证了位置误差收敛和系统稳定性。

Conclusion: 该控制框架能有效解耦任务空间与零空间，确保主任务不受干扰的同时实现安全高效的协作，AR引导实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration aims to extend human ability through cooperation with robots. This technology is currently helping people with physical disabilities, has transformed the manufacturing process of companies, improved surgical performance, and will likely revolutionize the daily lives of everyone in the future. Being able to enhance the performance of both sides, such that human-robot collaboration outperforms a single robot/human, remains an open issue. For safer and more effective collaboration, a new control scheme has been proposed for redundant robots in this paper, consisting of an adaptive vision-based control term in task space and an interactive control term in null space. Such a formulation allows the robot to autonomously carry out tasks in an unknown environment without prior calibration while also interacting with humans to deal with unforeseen changes (e.g., potential collision, temporary needs) under the redundant configuration. The decoupling between task space and null space helps to explore the collaboration safely and effectively without affecting the main task of the robot end-effector. The stability of the closed-loop system has been rigorously proved with Lyapunov methods, and both the convergence of the position error in task space and that of the damping model in null space are guaranteed. The experimental results of a robot manipulator guided with the technology of augmented reality (AR) are presented to illustrate the performance of the control scheme.

</details>


### [435] [DeReCo: Decoupling Representation and Coordination Learning for Object-Adaptive Decentralized Multi-Robot Cooperative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08111)
*Kazuki Shibata,Ryosuke Sota,Shandil Dhiresh Bosch,Yuki Kadokawa,Tsurumine Yoshihisa,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DeReCo的新框架，用于解决多机器人在去中心化协作运输中因物体形状和物理特性多样带来的表示学习与协调学习耦合问题，通过三阶段训练策略解耦这两个过程，显著提升了样本效率和跨物体泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化多机器人协同运输任务中，物体形态和物理属性的多样性导致表示学习（部分可观测下）与多智能体协调学习（非平稳性）相互干扰，传统端到端联合优化方法存在训练效率低、泛化差的问题，因此需要一种解耦机制来提升学习稳定性与效率。

Method: 提出DeReCo框架，采用三阶段训练策略：（1）利用全局物体信息进行集中式协调学习；（2）基于局部观测重建物体相关表示；（3）逐步去除全局信息以实现去中心化执行，从而解耦表示与协调学习。

Result: 实验表明，DeReCo在三种训练物体上的仿真性能优于基线方法，并能泛化到六种未见物体（不同质量和摩擦系数），且在真实机器人上对两种未见物体也表现出更优的运输性能。

Conclusion: DeReCo通过解耦表示学习与协调学习，有效缓解了多机器人协作运输中的双向干扰问题，实现了更高样本效率和更强的跨物体及场景泛化能力，适用于去中心化、部分可观测的实际应用环境。

Abstract: Generalizing decentralized multi-robot cooperative transport across objects with diverse shapes and physical properties remains a fundamental challenge. Under decentralized execution, two key challenges arise: object-dependent representation learning under partial observability and coordination learning in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) under non-stationarity. A typical approach jointly optimizes object-dependent representations and coordinated policies in an end-to-end manner while randomizing object shapes and physical properties during training. However, this joint optimization tightly couples representation and coordination learning, introducing bidirectional interference: inaccurate representations under partial observability destabilize coordination learning, while non-stationarity in MARL further degrades representation learning, resulting in sample-inefficient training. To address this structural coupling, we propose DeReCo, a novel MARL framework that decouples representation and coordination learning for object-adaptive multi-robot cooperative transport, improving sample efficiency and generalization across objects and transport scenarios. DeReCo adopts a three-stage training strategy: (1) centralized coordination learning with privileged object information, (2) reconstruction of object-dependent representations from local observations, and (3) progressive removal of privileged information for decentralized execution. This decoupling mitigates interference between representation and coordination learning and enables stable and sample-efficient training. Experimental results show that DeReCo outperforms baselines in simulation on three training objects, generalizes to six unseen objects with varying masses and friction coefficients, and achieves superior performance on two unseen objects in real-robot experiments.

</details>


### [436] [Towards Human-Like Manipulation through RL-Augmented Teleoperation and Mixture-of-Dexterous-Experts VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08122)
*Tutian Tang,Xingyu Ji,Wanli Xing,Ce Hao,Wenqiang Xu,Lin Shao,Cewu Lu,Qiaojun Yu,Jiangmiao Pang,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成框架，通过IMCopilot和MoDE-VLA解决灵巧操作中高保真数据获取、多技能学习和多模态感知融合的挑战，在接触丰富的任务中实现了成功率翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉-语言-动作模型扩展到类人双手机械灵巧操作面临数据采集、多技能学习与多模态感知融合的重大挑战。

Method: 提出IMCopilot（强化学习训练的原子技能）辅助遥操作数据收集，并作为低层执行单元；设计MoDE-VLA架构，通过残差注入机制融合力和触觉模态到预训练VLA主干中。

Result: 在四个复杂度递增的任务上验证，MoDE-VLA在灵巧且接触丰富的任务中相比基线模型成功率提升一倍。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效克服了灵巧操作中的关键瓶颈，实现了高性能的多模态感知与执行协同。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable success in robotic manipulation, their application has largely been confined to low-degree-of-freedom end-effectors performing simple, vision-guided pick-and-place tasks. Extending these models to human-like, bimanual dexterous manipulation-specifically contact-rich in-hand operations-introduces critical challenges in high-fidelity data acquisition, multi-skill learning, and multimodal sensory fusion. In this paper, we propose an integrated framework to address these bottlenecks, built upon two components. First, we introduce IMCopilot (In-hand Manipulation Copilot), a suite of reinforcement learning-trained atomic skills that plays a dual role: it acts as a shared-autonomy assistant to simplify teleoperation data collection, and it serves as a callable low-level execution primitive for the VLA. Second, we present MoDE-VLA (Mixture-of-Dexterous-Experts VLA), an architecture that seamlessly integrates heterogeneous force and tactile modalities into a pretrained VLA backbone. By utilizing a residual injection mechanism, MoDE-VLA enables contact-aware refinement without degrading the model's pretrained knowledge. We validate our approach on four tasks of escalating complexity, demonstrating doubled success rate improvement over the baseline in dexterous contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [437] [SaiVLA-0: Cerebrum--Pons--Cerebellum Tripartite Architecture for Compute-Aware Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08124)
*Xiang Shi,Wenlong Huang,Menglin Zou,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受神经科学启发的视觉-语言-动作（Vision-Language-Action）三联框架，通过模拟大脑、脑桥和小脑的功能实现高效、模块化的机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物神经系统结构启发，旨在构建更高效、稳定且可复现的多模态机器人控制系统，解决现有方法在计算效率与控制稳定性方面的不足。

Method: 引入类脑三部分架构：冻结的Cerebrum提取高层多模态先验；Pons Adapter融合皮层特征与实时本体感觉输入并生成执行指令；Cerebellum（ParaCAT）进行快速并行分类解码，结合滞后/指数移动平均/温度/熵机制提升稳定性；采用固定比例调度和两阶段特征缓存提升计算效率；腕部ROI通过校准投影与末端执行器几何绑定，实现运动稳定的高分辨率感知。

Result: 初步实验显示该系统在相同条件下显著减少训练时间（7.5小时降至4.5小时），平均成功率从86.5%提升至92.5%，SaiVLA0模型达到99.0%的平均成功。

Conclusion: 该模块化设计支持独立升级各组件——仅需重训Pons以升级Cerebrum，更换机器人只需重训Cerebellum，且可通过强化学习单独优化控制而不影响高层语义，具备良好的可扩展性与实用性。

Abstract: We revisit Vision-Language-Action through a neuroscience-inspired triad. Biologically, the Cerebrum provides stable high-level multimodal priors and remains frozen; the Pons Adapter integrates these cortical features with real-time proprioceptive inputs and compiles intent into execution-ready tokens; and the Cerebellum (ParaCAT) performs fast, parallel categorical decoding for online control, with hysteresis/EMA/temperature/entropy for stability. A fixed-ratio schedule and two-stage feature caching make the system compute-aware and reproducible. Inspired by active, foveated vision, our wrist ROIs are geometrically tied to the end-effector via calibrated projection, providing a movement-stabilized, high-resolution view that is sensitive to fine-grained pose changes and complements the global context of the main view.
  The design is modular: upgrading the Cerebrum only retrains the Pons; changing robots only trains the Cerebellum; cerebellum-only RL can further refine control without touching high-level semantics. As a concept-and-protocol paper with preliminary evidence, we outline a timing protocol under matched conditions (GPU, resolution, batch) to verify anticipated efficiency gains. We also report preliminary LIBERO evidence showing that split feature caching reduces training time (7.5h to 4.5h) and improves average success (86.5% to 92.5%) under official N1.5 head-only training, and that SaiVLA0 reaches 99.0% mean success.

</details>


### [438] [TRIAGE: Type-Routed Interventions via Aleatoric-Epistemic Gated Estimation in Robotic Manipulation and Adaptive Perception -- Don't Treat All Uncertainty the Same](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08128)
*Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Patrick Poggi,Amanda Sofie Rios,Nilesh Ahuja,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的后处理框架，将预测不确定性分解为偶然性和认知性两类，并据此在推理时调节系统响应，从而在机器人控制与感知任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常将不确定性聚合为单一标量，导致无法区分观测噪声与模型失配，进而采取错误的纠正措施，影响闭环性能。

Method: 通过马氏密度模型估计观测分布偏差得到偶然性不确定性，利用抗噪的前向动力学集成模型检测认知性不确定性，二者近似正交，分别触发观测恢复和控制调节，并用于自适应感知中的模型选择。

Result: 在机器人操作任务中，复合扰动下任务成功率从59.4%提升至80.4%，优于综合不确定性基线最多21.0%；在MOT17上的自适应跟踪中，计算量减少58.2%的同时保持检测精度损失仅0.4%。

Conclusion: 分解不确定性类型可实现针对性响应，有效提升系统鲁棒性与效率，为不确定性感知系统提供了更精细的调控机制。

Abstract: Most uncertainty-aware robotic systems collapse prediction uncertainty into a single scalar score and use it to trigger uniform corrective responses. This aggregation obscures whether uncertainty arises from corrupted observations or from mismatch between the learned model and the true system dynamics. As a result, corrective actions may be applied to the wrong component of the closed loop, degrading performance relative to leaving the policy unchanged. We introduce a lightweight post hoc framework that decomposes uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components and uses these signals to regulate system responses at inference time. Aleatoric uncertainty is estimated from deviations in the observation distribution using a Mahalanobis density model, while epistemic uncertainty is detected using a noise robust forward dynamics ensemble that isolates model mismatch from measurement corruption. The two signals remain empirically near orthogonal during closed loop execution and enable type specific responses. High aleatoric uncertainty triggers observation recovery, while high epistemic uncertainty moderates control actions. The same signals also regulate adaptive perception by guiding model capacity selection during tracking inference. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across both control and perception tasks. In robotic manipulation, the decomposed controller improves task success from 59.4% to 80.4% under compound perturbations and outperforms a combined uncertainty baseline by up to 21.0%. In adaptive tracking inference on MOT17, uncertainty-guided model selection reduces average compute by 58.2% relative to a fixed high capacity detector while preserving detection quality within 0.4%. Code and demo videos are available at https://divake.github.io/uncertainty-decomposition/.

</details>


### [439] [UniGround: Universal 3D Visual Grounding via Training-Free Scene Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08131)
*Jiaxi Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Wei Lu,Taowen Wang,Weisheng Xu,Shuning Zhang,Yixiao Feng,Yuetong Fang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的视觉与几何推理方法UniGround，用于开放世界3D视觉定位（3DVG），在零样本设置下实现了最先进的性能，并展现出对未见场景和真实环境的强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练模型的3D视觉定位方法受限于先验知识边界，难以泛化到未见过的空间关系和分布外场景，因此需要一种更具通用性和鲁棒性的方法。

Method: UniGround分为两个阶段：全局候选筛选阶段通过无需训练的3D拓扑结构和多视角语义编码构建候选场景；局部精确定位阶段利用多尺度视觉提示和结构化推理精确定位目标物体。

Result: 在ScanRefer和EmbodiedScan数据集上，UniGround在无任何3D监督的情况下取得了46.1%/34.1% Acc@0.25/0.5（ScanRefer）和28.7% Acc@0.25（EmbodiedScan），成为当前零样本方法中的最先进模型，并在真实环境中表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入无需训练的视觉与几何推理机制，UniGround实现了开放世界的3D视觉定位，突破了预训练模型的知识边界，为未来在复杂、动态真实环境中的应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Understanding and localizing objects in complex 3D environments from natural language descriptions, known as 3D Visual Grounding (3DVG), is a foundational challenge in embodied AI, with broad implications for robotics, augmented reality, and human-machine interaction. Large-scale pre-trained foundation models have driven significant progress on this front, enabling open-vocabulary 3DVG that allows systems to locate arbitrary objects in a given scene. However, their reliance on pre-trained models constrains 3D perception and reasoning within the inherited knowledge boundaries, resulting in limited generalization to unseen spatial relationships and poor robustness to out-of-distribution scenes. In this paper, we replace this constrained perception with training-free visual and geometric reasoning, thereby unlocking open-world 3DVG that enables the localization of any object in any scene beyond the training data. Specifically, the proposed UniGround operates in two stages: a Global Candidate Filtering stage that constructs scene candidates through training-free 3D topology and multi-view semantic encoding, and a Local Precision Grounding stage that leverages multi-scale visual prompting and structured reasoning to precisely identify the target object. Experiments on ScanRefer and EmbodiedScan show that UniGround achieves 46.1\%/34.1\% Acc@0.25/0.5 on ScanRefer and 28.7\% Acc@0.25 on EmbodiedScan, establishing a new state-of-the-art among zero-shot methods on EmbodiedScan without any 3D supervision. We further evaluate UniGround in real-world environments under uncontrolled reconstruction conditions and substantial domain shift, showing training-free reasoning generalizes robustly beyond curated benchmarks.

</details>


### [440] [POIROT: Investigating Direct Tangible vs. Digitally Mediated Interaction and Attitude Moderation in Multi-party Murder Mystery Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08136)
*Wen Chen,Rongxi Chen,Shankai Chen,Huiyang Gong,Minghui Guo,Yingri Xu,Xintong Wu,Xinyi Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在多用户游戏中，机器人作为游戏主持人时，物理交互对用户体验的影响，并发现用户对机器人的负面态度（NARS）是决定物理交互效果的关键调节因素：高NARS用户在使用有形交互时沉浸感更低，而低NARS用户则不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“物理交互普遍更优”的假设，探究在复杂人机交互场景中，个体差异（特别是对机器人的负面态度）如何影响交互效果。

Method: 通过一项被试间实验（N=67），使用自研机器人主持谋杀谜案游戏，比较线索通过有形交互与数字界面传递两种方式的效果，采用MANOVA和多层线性模型（MLM）分析数据，并结合定性访谈结果。

Result: 主效应分析显示交互方式本身无显著差异，但MLM结果显示NARS具有显著调节作用：高NARS用户在有形交互下叙事沉浸感显著降低，定性数据表明有形交互对焦虑用户造成空间侵扰，而数字界面起到社交缓冲作用。

Conclusion: 物理交互并非普遍优于数字交互，用户对机器人的态度是关键边界条件，未来社交机器人系统应根据用户特质动态调整交互模态。

Abstract: As social robots take on increasingly complex roles like game masters (GMs) in multi-party games, the expectation that physicality universally enhances user experience remains debated. This study challenges the "one-size-fits-all" view of tangible interaction by identifying a critical boundary condition: users' Negative Attitudes towards Robots (NARS). In a between-subjects experiment (N = 67), a custom-built robot GM facilitated a multi-party murder mystery game (MMG) by delivering clues either through direct tangible interaction or a digitally mediated interface. Baseline multivariate analysis (MANOVA) showed no significant main effect of delivery modality, confirming that tangibility alone does not guarantee superior engagement. However, primary analysis using multilevel linear models (MLM) revealed a reliable moderation: participants high in NARS experienced markedly lower narrative immersion under tangible delivery, whereas those with low NARS scores showed no such decrement. Qualitative findings further illuminate this divergence: tangibility provides novelty and engagement for some but imposes excessive proxemic friction for anxious users, for whom the digital interface acts as a protective social buffer. These results advance a conditional model of HRI and emphasize the necessity for adaptive systems that can tailor interaction modalities to user predispositions.

</details>


### [441] [Multifingered force-aware control for humanoid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08142)
*Pasquale Marra,Gabriele M. Caddeo,Ugo Pattacini,Lorenzo Natale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多指机器人手的力感知控制与力分配方法，通过基于估计力的模型控制策略优化接触稳定性，并在多种物体平衡任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高多指机器人手在抓取不同质量分布或不稳定接触物体时的稳定性和适应性，需要一种能够实时感知并调整接触力的控制方法。

Method: 利用Xela磁传感器采集触觉信号与真实力数据训练力估计器，设计一种基于模型的控制器，通过最小化压力中心（CoP）与指尖接触多边形质心之间的距离来动态调节躯干、手臂、手腕和手指运动以重新分配力。

Result: 在五个物体的平衡任务中达到82.7%的成功率，在多物体场景下达到80%的准确率。
